# Gome Remote Mac Agent Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give Gome Remote a Mac tile that streams the Mac screen as hardware-encoded H.264 over WebRTC straight from a small Mac agent to the viewer, with the gateway only relaying the connection set-up, so that clicking feels like Mac-to-Mac screen sharing (median click-to-picture at most 100 ms, against about 175 ms through VNC today).

**Architecture:** A Rust agent (`agent/mac`, a LaunchAgent in the owner's login session) captures the main display with ScreenCaptureKit, encodes with VideoToolbox, sends RTP with str0m, and injects input with `CGEventPost`; it exposes `GET /v1/status` and `POST /v1/sessions` on the Mac's tailnet address for the gateway only. The gateway gains an `agent` target protocol, a per-method credential schema, `POST /api/agent-sessions` (offer/answer relay) and an agent state in `/api/targets`. The page gains a WebRTC session module next to the untouched Guacamole path, bridge version 3 for the app, and a `#measure` mode that times click-to-picture with one polling loop for both methods.

**Tech Stack:** Node.js 24 (`node --test`, no new runtime dependency), TypeScript 5.9 + Vite 8 for the page, Electron 44 for the app, Rust 2021 (str0m 0.24 with aws-lc-rs, tiny_http 0.12, serde_json, subtle, libc; objc2 0.6 crates for ScreenCaptureKit; hand-written FFI for VideoToolbox, CoreGraphics, IOKit), bash for the install scripts, GitHub Actions (macOS job).

**Spec:** `docs/mac-agent-spec.md` (rev 3, converged; the source of truth, do not edit it). `docs/redesign-spec.md` still governs everything the spec says "stays as it is" (login allow list, Origin check, credentials file, parallel rollout). Executors read the spec's section for every task they implement; items are cited as D1-D10 (decisions), S1-S6 (success criteria), P1-P4 (pre-tests), V1-V6 (verification), O0-O4 (owner decisions).

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements include this section. Values are copied from the spec.

- Public repository: no owner identifiers anywhere (tailnet logins, device names, real tailnet addresses, home paths). Use `100.64.0.10` (gateway server) and `100.64.0.20` (Mac host), `owner@example.com`, `*.example.ts.net`, and `/home/user` or `/Users/user`. Other tailnet-range test values must be one of the hygiene scanner's allowed probes (`100.64.0.1`, `100.64.0.99`, `100.127.255.254`, IPv6 `fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234`). In Rust, the tailnet ranges are written as numbers (`o[0] == 100 && (64..=127).contains(&o[1])`, segments `0xfd7a, 0x115c, 0xa1e0`), never as address-shaped text. `node --test test/public-hygiene.test.js` runs after staging and before every commit.
- The Mac host is reached over `ssh` with the alias in `$MAC` (set it in your shell; never write the real alias, host or user into a tracked file).
- Authorisation does not widen (S6): only a caller that passed the gateway's Tailscale-login allow list and Origin check can open a Mac session; the agent accepts HTTP only from the one configured gateway tailnet address.
- Agent port 18471 (the spike used 18470 and keeps it; the development install uses 18472).
- Agent token: 32 random bytes, base64url without padding = exactly 43 characters; the gateway keeps it as `agentToken` in the target's credential entry; the agent compares it in constant time from a user-owned mode-600 file `~/Library/Application Support/Gome Remote Agent/token`.
- Credential entries have the shape of their target's method: `{agentToken}` for `agent`, `{username?, password}` for `rdp`/`vnc`; never mixed; a mismatch makes the whole file invalid (the existing "file-wide invalid" rule is unchanged).
- Agent HTTP: bound to the Mac's tailnet address only; `Host` must equal `<bind>:<port>`; any `Origin` header is refused; body at most 64 KiB; repeated authentication failures are answered later (100 ms doubling to 5 s), a correct request never is.
- Agent settings: `/usr/local/etc/gome-remote-agent/agent.json` (root:wheel 644; `bind`, `port`, `gateway`), program `/usr/local/libexec/gome-remote-agent` (root:wheel 755); the agent reads no command-line argument and no environment variable; refusal to start on: bind not a tailnet address, settings not root-owned, token file not mode 600, no gateway address.
- Signing: dedicated keychain under `~/Library/Application Support/Gome Remote Agent/sign/`, self-signed code-signing certificate, hardened runtime. LaunchAgent `net.gome.remote-agent`: `RunAtLoad`, `KeepAlive`, `ProcessType=Interactive`; log `~/Library/Logs/gome-remote-agent.log`, size-capped, no per-frame lines.
- Video: ScreenCaptureKit main display, `showsCursor=false`, queue depth 3, NV12; VideoToolbox H.264 constrained baseline, hardware, real-time, low-latency rate control, no B-frames, keyframe on PLI/FIR/new viewer and every 3 s. Size: the page's request (auto = viewer CSS size x devicePixelRatio, rounded down to even, 640x480 to 3840x2160) fitted to the Mac screen's aspect ratio; 60 fps up to 2560x1440 pixels, 45 fps above. Bitrate ceiling 12 Mbps at 1440p and 20 Mbps at 4K, lowered by the str0m TWCC estimate, floor 2 Mbps. Static screen: the last frame is re-sent every 100 ms. UDP socket buffers 4 MiB. RTX, playout-delay (0,0), abs-capture-time. H.264 only (AV1 has no hardware encoder; O0).
- Page: receive-only video transceiver, codec preference H.264 plus `video/rtx` (RTX must stay), `jitterBufferTarget` and `playoutDelayHint` 0, `object-fit: contain`, no ICE servers, candidate gathering limited to 1.5 s, the viewer's own pointer (O1; the capture has no cursor), automatic reconnect never (a connection starts only from an explicit user action).
- Input data channel `input` (ordered, reliable): JSON, at most 512 bytes per message (`text`: 8 KiB), out-of-range values dropped. Messages `pointer {x,y}`, `button {b:0|1|2,down,x,y}`, `wheel {dx,dy}` (pixels, cut to 2000), `key {code,down}` (`KeyboardEvent.code`), `text {s}`, `release {}`, `size {w,h}`; positions are 0..1 over the displayed picture. Control is Control, Alt is Option, Windows/Meta is Command; `Lang1`/`HangulMode` is one Caps Lock press (O3). `release` is sent on blur, fullscreen change, dialogs and session end; the agent releases everything when the channel closes or the session is replaced.
- Sessions: one per agent; a new offer closes the old one (takeover); ICE consent lost or 10 s without any packet closes the session and stops capture; sessions are refused with code `permission` (403) or `locked` (423) by status reasons.
- Gateway: `/api/agent-sessions` body at most 64 KiB, JSON content type, `sdp` a string starting `v=0`, size within 640x480 to 3840x2160; agent timeouts 2 s (status) and 10 s (session); status cached 10 s and coalesced; the response carries enumerations only. Agent targets need `platform: "mac"`, a tailnet `hostname`, `port` default 18471.
- Success thresholds (fixed before measuring; never moved to fit a result): S2 per measuring client, 30 clicks x 3 runs, median at most 100 ms and at most 0.6x the same client's VNC median at the same time of day, p95 at most 150 ms, no sample over 1 s; S3 no freeze over 500 ms in 60 s of dragging and scrolling; S4 with receive limited to 5 Mbps no stall over 1 s and median at most 200 ms (below 3 Mbps is outside support).
- The Guacamole path (`rdp`, `vnc`) and the CSP are not changed. The VNC tile stays as "Mac (VNC)" (D9); deleting the Mac account password from the server is O2 and is not done here.
- Code style: Node/TypeScript formatted with the repository's Prettier defaults (`npx prettier --write <files>`), strict TypeScript (`npm run check`); Rust formatted by `cargo fmt` using `agent/mac/rustfmt.toml` (max width 120), `cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean, `--locked` on every cargo command except the one that creates `Cargo.lock`.
- Korean copy is exactly the strings in the tests and the spec's section 6; do not paraphrase.
- Commits: one per task (more if a task says so), message a single imperative sentence like the existing history ("Add ...", "Offer ..."), plus the session's usual trailers.

## Review Focus

The spec's own tests cover the happy paths. These five inputs it does not mention are the most likely to bite a person using the software (most likely first); each has a test in the task that owns the code, marked `Review Focus N` in the test file.

1. **A window whose shape differs from the Mac's screen (black bars).** Pressing in a bar must not click the Mac's edge (Dock, menu bar), and a drag released outside the picture must still release the button. Owner: Task 3 (`test/agent-session.test.js`, `test/agent-input.test.js`).
2. **Double and triple clicks.** macOS opens a folder on a double click only when each synthetic event carries the right click count; fast clicks in the same place must count 1, 2, 3 and start over after a pause or a move. Owner: Task 9 (`ClickCounter` tests in `agent/mac/src/pressed.rs`).
3. **Text from the text window with emoji, line breaks and thousands of characters.** Characters must arrive intact (a surrogate pair is never split, CR/LF/CRLF is one Return, nothing is truncated or sent over the 8 KiB message limit). Owners: Task 3 (`splitText`, `sendText`) and Task 9 (`typing.rs`).
4. **A browser without H.264 or without `requestVideoFrameCallback`** (Firefox, some Linux Chromium builds): the session must say "연결할 수 없습니다" at once instead of hanging, and a browser lacking the callback must still report the first picture. Owner: Task 3 (`test/agent-session.test.js`).
5. **Rotating or pasting the token.** After `set-credential --agent-token` the tile must follow immediately, not after the 10 s status cache; a token pasted with a trailing newline or spaces must be stored clean, never as a 44-character value that silently fails. Owners: Task 2 (`test/agent-relay.test.js`) and Task 1 (`test/set-credential-agent.test.js`).

## Working conventions

**Where things run.**

- Node and TypeScript: from the repository root, `node --test test/<file>` for one file, `npm test` for everything, `npm run check` for the types.
- The Rust crate `agent/mac` uses macOS frameworks (ScreenCaptureKit, VideoToolbox, CoreGraphics, IOKit) and **cannot be built on the Linux host**. Everything that does not touch a framework lives outside the `mac` module and also compiles and tests on Linux with a plain `cargo test` in `agent/mac` (the `mac` module is behind `#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]`): use that as the fast inner loop. The Mac is the authority: every Rust task ends with the build and test run on the Mac below, and the tasks that add framework code (7, 9, 10) also end with an on-device check.
- The Mac build loop (exact commands; `$MAC` is the ssh alias of the Mac host, passwordless):

```sh
# 1. from the repository root: copy the crate (build output on the Mac is kept)
rsync -a --delete --exclude target agent/mac/ "$MAC":gome-remote-agent-build/
# 2. build and test on the Mac (non-interactive ssh may lack cargo on PATH, hence the first line)
ssh "$MAC" '. "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null; cd gome-remote-agent-build && cargo test --locked 2>&1 | tail -40'
# 3. formatting and lints, same place
ssh "$MAC" '. "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null; cd gome-remote-agent-build && cargo fmt --check && cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings 2>&1 | tail -40'
```

  Expected: `test result: ok.` for the library, the binary, `tests/no_overrides.rs`, and nothing from clippy or `fmt --check`. If a macOS-only file does not compile, fix it on the Mac (the files marked "ported" below were written against the spike's working code but the framework modules could not be compiled on the Linux host) and copy the fix back into the repository; never edit only the copy in `gome-remote-agent-build`.
- `$SPIKE` is the throwaway spike (read-only, never merged): `<main checkout>/.claude/worktrees/mac-agent-spike/spike/mac-agent`. Files "ported from `$SPIKE/src/...`" start from that file; where this plan shows the whole new file, use the plan's text.
- Run the public-repository check before every commit: `git add <files> && node --test test/public-hygiene.test.js`.

**Task order** follows the dependency chain: gateway first (1-2), page and app (3-5), then the agent (6-10), then packaging, CI and documentation (11-13). Tasks 1-5 and 6-10 are independent chains and may run in either order; 10 needs 6-9, 13 needs everything. The owner-assisted steps are in the last section and are not code tasks.

### Task 1: Gateway configuration, credential schema and `set-credential --agent-token`

Spec: D2 (token storage, shape per method), D7 (target settings, parser, `set-credential`), S6.

**Files:**
- Create: `test/config-agent.test.js`, `test/credentials-agent.test.js`, `test/set-credential-agent.test.js`
- Modify: `server/config.js`, `server/credentials.js`, `server/index.js`, `scripts/set-credential.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the existing `validateConfig(value)`, `readCredentialFile(file, uid)`, `credentialsFor(entries, target)`, `CredentialStore`, `main()` of `set-credential.mjs`.
- Produces:
  - `DEFAULT_AGENT_PORT = 18471` (export of `server/config.js`); `validateConfig` accepts `protocol: "agent"` and returns targets with `port` filled in; the caller's objects are not modified.
  - `AGENT_TOKEN` (export of `server/credentials.js`): `/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/`.
  - `readCredentialFile(file, uid = process.getuid?.(), protocols = new Map())`: `protocols` maps target ID to `"rdp" | "vnc" | "agent"`; an entry that does not fit its target's method throws `CredentialFileError` naming only the target ID.
  - `credentialsFor(entries, target)` returns `{agentToken}` for an agent target, `{username, password}` otherwise, `undefined` when the entry is missing or has the wrong shape.
  - `new CredentialStore(file, { uid, log, targets })`: `targets` is the configured target list; `store.lookup(target)` and `store.load()` check shapes against it.
  - `set-credential`: `node scripts/set-credential.mjs [<config>] <target-id> [--agent-token]`; exit 2 for usage errors (nothing prompted), 1 for refusals, 0 saved.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `test/config-agent.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { validateConfig } from "../server/config.js";

const target = {
  id: "linux",
  name: "Linux",
  protocol: "rdp",
  platform: "linux",
  hostname: "100.64.0.10",
  port: 3390,
};
const agent = {
  id: "mac",
  name: "Mac (새 방식)",
  platform: "mac",
  protocol: "agent",
  hostname: "100.64.0.20",
};
const base = {
  socketPath: "/tmp/gome-remote/gateway.sock",
  publicOrigin: "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8450",
  allowedLogins: ["owner@example.com"],
  credentialsFile: "/home/user/.config/gome-remote/credentials.json",
  targets: [target, agent],
};
const withTargets = (...targets) => ({ ...base, targets });

test("an agent target needs only an id, a name, the mac platform and a tailnet address; the port defaults to 18471", () => {
  const config = validateConfig(base);
  assert.equal(config.targets[1].port, 18471);
  assert.equal(config.targets[0].port, 3390, "other targets are left alone");
  assert.equal(
    validateConfig(withTargets({ ...agent, port: 19000 })).targets[0].port,
    19000,
  );
  assert.doesNotThrow(() =>
    validateConfig(withTargets({ ...agent, hostname: "fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234" })),
  );
  assert.equal(agent.port, undefined, "the caller's object is not modified");
});

test("an agent target refuses a platform other than mac, a non-tailnet address and a bad port", () => {
  for (const [what, bad] of [
    ["linux", { ...agent, platform: "linux" }],
    ["windows", { ...agent, platform: "windows" }],
    ["loopback", { ...agent, hostname: "127.0.0.1" }],
    ["lan", { ...agent, hostname: "192.168.0.5" }],
    ["name", { ...agent, hostname: "mac.example.ts.net" }],
    ["port 0", { ...agent, port: 0 }],
    ["port 70000", { ...agent, port: 70000 }],
    ["port text", { ...agent, port: "18471" }],
  ])
    assert.throws(() => validateConfig(withTargets(bad)), /./, what);
});

test("platform mac is the only one an agent accepts, with a message that says why", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => validateConfig(withTargets({ ...agent, platform: "linux" })),
    /agent.*mac/i,
  );
});

test("settings that only mean something for RDP or VNC are refused on an agent target", () => {
  for (const extra of [
    { security: "nla" },
    { ignoreCertificate: true },
    { profile: "gnome-remote-login" },
    { persistent: true },
  ])
    assert.throws(
      () => validateConfig(withTargets({ ...agent, ...extra })),
      /agent/i,
      JSON.stringify(extra),
    );
  assert.doesNotThrow(() =>
    validateConfig(withTargets({ ...agent, persistent: false })),
  );
});

test("rdp and vnc targets still need an explicit port, and unknown protocols are still refused", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => validateConfig(withTargets({ ...target, port: undefined })),
    /port/i,
  );
  assert.throws(
    () => validateConfig(withTargets({ ...target, protocol: "ssh" })),
    /RDP, VNC/,
  );
});
```

Create `test/credentials-agent.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile, chmod } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import {
  CredentialStore,
  credentialsFor,
  readCredentialFile,
} from "../server/credentials.js";

const posix = process.platform !== "win32";
const TOKEN = "A".repeat(43);
const OTHER = "b-_".repeat(14) + "cd"; // 44 characters, one too many
const protocols = new Map([
  ["ubuntu-server", "rdp"],
  ["mac-vnc", "vnc"],
  ["mac", "agent"],
]);

async function file(t, targets, mode = 0o600) {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "gr-cred-agent-"));
  t.after(() => rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const path = join(dir, "credentials.json");
  await writeFile(path, JSON.stringify({ version: 1, targets }));
  await chmod(path, mode);
  return path;
}

const mixed = {
  "ubuntu-server": { username: "u", password: "p" },
  "mac-vnc": { username: "owner", password: "macpass" },
  mac: { agentToken: TOKEN },
};

test(
  "each target method has its own entry shape, and the three can live in one file",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const path = await file(t, mixed);
    const entries = await readCredentialFile(path, process.getuid(), protocols);
    assert.deepEqual(
      credentialsFor(entries, { id: "mac", protocol: "agent" }),
      { agentToken: TOKEN },
    );
    assert.deepEqual(
      credentialsFor(entries, { id: "mac-vnc", protocol: "vnc" }),
      { username: "owner", password: "macpass" },
    );
    assert.deepEqual(
      credentialsFor(entries, { id: "ubuntu-server", protocol: "rdp" }),
      { username: "u", password: "p" },
    );
  },
);

test(
  "an agent entry is exactly {agentToken}: mixing it with a password or user name invalidates the whole file",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    for (const bad of [
      { agentToken: TOKEN, password: "p" },
      { agentToken: TOKEN, username: "u" },
      { agentToken: TOKEN, extra: 1 },
    ]) {
      const path = await file(t, { ...mixed, mac: bad });
      await assert.rejects(
        readCredentialFile(path, process.getuid(), protocols),
        /"mac".*(mix|unknown|field)/i,
      );
      // The same file is invalid without the target list too: the shapes never mix.
      await assert.rejects(readCredentialFile(path, process.getuid()), /"mac"/);
    }
  },
);

test(
  "a token is 43 base64url characters; anything else is refused without echoing it",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    for (const bad of [
      "",
      "short",
      OTHER,
      `${"A".repeat(42)}+`,
      `${"A".repeat(42)}=`,
      `${"A".repeat(42)} `,
      42,
      null,
      ["A".repeat(43)],
    ]) {
      const path = await file(t, { mac: { agentToken: bad } });
      await assert.rejects(
        readCredentialFile(path, process.getuid(), protocols),
        (error) => {
          assert.match(error.message, /"mac"/);
          assert.ok(!error.message.includes(String(bad)) || bad === "");
          return true;
        },
      );
    }
  },
);

test(
  "the entry shape must match the configured method of the target, or the whole file is invalid",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const cases = [
      ["agent target with a password", { mac: { password: "p" } }],
      ["agent target with an empty entry", { mac: {} }],
      [
        "rdp target with an agent token",
        { "ubuntu-server": { agentToken: TOKEN } },
      ],
      ["vnc target with an agent token", { "mac-vnc": { agentToken: TOKEN } }],
    ];
    for (const [what, targets] of cases) {
      const path = await file(t, { ...mixed, ...targets });
      await assert.rejects(
        readCredentialFile(path, process.getuid(), protocols),
        /Credentials for/,
        what,
      );
    }
  },
);

test(
  "an entry for a target that is not configured may have either shape",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const path = await file(t, {
      ghost: { agentToken: TOKEN },
      "ghost-two": { password: "p" },
    });
    const entries = await readCredentialFile(path, process.getuid(), protocols);
    assert.equal(entries.size, 2);
  },
);

test("credentialsFor never hands a password-shaped entry to an agent target or the reverse", () => {
  const entries = new Map([
    ["mac", { password: "p" }],
    ["ubuntu-server", { agentToken: TOKEN }],
    ["empty", {}],
  ]);
  assert.equal(
    credentialsFor(entries, { id: "mac", protocol: "agent" }),
    undefined,
  );
  assert.equal(
    credentialsFor(entries, { id: "ubuntu-server", protocol: "rdp" }),
    undefined,
  );
  assert.equal(
    credentialsFor(entries, { id: "empty", protocol: "agent" }),
    undefined,
  );
  assert.equal(
    credentialsFor(entries, { id: "absent", protocol: "agent" }),
    undefined,
  );
});

test(
  "the store checks shapes against its targets; a mismatch makes every target not ready, with one log line",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const path = await file(t, mixed);
    const lines = [];
    const store = new CredentialStore(path, {
      uid: process.getuid(),
      log: (line) => lines.push(line),
      targets: [
        { id: "ubuntu-server", protocol: "rdp" },
        { id: "mac-vnc", protocol: "vnc" },
        { id: "mac", protocol: "agent" },
      ],
    });
    assert.deepEqual(await store.lookup({ id: "mac", protocol: "agent" }), {
      agentToken: TOKEN,
    });
    assert.equal((await store.load()).size, 3);
    // Reconfigure "mac" as VNC while its entry is an agent token.
    const wrong = new CredentialStore(path, {
      uid: process.getuid(),
      log: (line) => lines.push(line),
      targets: [
        { id: "ubuntu-server", protocol: "rdp" },
        { id: "mac-vnc", protocol: "vnc" },
        { id: "mac", protocol: "vnc" },
      ],
    });
    assert.equal(
      await wrong.lookup({ id: "ubuntu-server", protocol: "rdp" }),
      undefined,
    );
    assert.equal(await wrong.lookup({ id: "mac", protocol: "vnc" }), undefined);
    assert.equal(lines.length, 1, "logged once, not per lookup");
    assert.ok(!lines[0].includes(TOKEN), "the token never reaches the log");
    await assert.rejects(wrong.load(), /"mac"/);
  },
);
```

Create `test/set-credential-agent.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { PassThrough } from "node:stream";
import { mkdtemp, readdir, rm, stat, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { main, writeCredentialFile } from "../scripts/set-credential.mjs";
import { readCredentialFile } from "../server/credentials.js";

const posix = process.platform !== "win32";
const TOKEN = "Zx9_-".repeat(8) + "abc"; // 43 characters

async function setup(t) {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "gr-setcred-agent-"));
  t.after(() => rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const credentialsFile = posix
    ? join(dir, "state", "credentials.json")
    : "/home/user/.config/gome-remote/credentials.json";
  const configPath = join(dir, "gateway.json");
  await writeFile(
    configPath,
    JSON.stringify({
      socketPath: "/home/user/.local/state/gome-remote/gateway.sock",
      publicOrigin: "http://127.0.0.1:38991",
      allowedLogins: ["owner@example.com"],
      credentialsFile,
      targets: [
        {
          id: "ubuntu-server",
          name: "Ubuntu 서버",
          platform: "linux",
          protocol: "rdp",
          hostname: "127.0.0.1",
          port: 3389,
        },
        {
          id: "mac-vnc",
          name: "Mac (VNC)",
          platform: "mac",
          protocol: "vnc",
          hostname: "100.64.0.20",
          port: 5900,
        },
        {
          id: "mac",
          name: "Mac (새 방식)",
          platform: "mac",
          protocol: "agent",
          hostname: "100.64.0.20",
        },
      ],
    }),
  );
  return { dir, configPath, credentialsFile };
}

async function run(argv, keystrokes, options = {}) {
  const stdin = new PassThrough();
  const out = [];
  const err = [];
  const stdout = new PassThrough().on("data", (c) => out.push(String(c)));
  const stderr = new PassThrough().on("data", (c) => err.push(String(c)));
  const done = main({
    argv,
    stdin,
    stdout,
    stderr,
    requireTty: false,
    ...options,
  });
  for (const chunk of keystrokes) {
    stdin.write(chunk);
    await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
  }
  stdin.end();
  const code = await done;
  return { code, out: out.join(""), err: err.join("") };
}

test(
  "--agent-token stores {agentToken} atomically with mode 600, never echoes it, and keeps the other entries",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath, credentialsFile, dir } = await setup(t);
    await run([configPath, "ubuntu-server"], ["u\n", "p1\n", "p1\n"]);
    for (const argv of [
      [configPath, "mac", "--agent-token"],
      ["--agent-token", configPath, "mac"],
    ]) {
      const result = await run(argv, [`${TOKEN}\n`]);
      assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.err);
      assert.ok(!result.out.includes(TOKEN), "the token is never echoed");
      assert.ok(!result.err.includes(TOKEN));
      assert.match(result.out, /저장했습니다: mac/);
      assert.match(result.out, /토큰/, "the prompt says it asks for a token");
      assert.doesNotMatch(result.out, /VNC 비밀번호|사용자 이름/);
    }
    assert.equal((await stat(credentialsFile)).mode & 0o777, 0o600);
    assert.deepEqual(await readdir(join(dir, "state")), ["credentials.json"]);
    const entries = await readCredentialFile(credentialsFile);
    assert.deepEqual(entries.get("mac"), { agentToken: TOKEN });
    assert.deepEqual(entries.get("ubuntu-server"), {
      username: "u",
      password: "p1",
    });
  },
);

// Review Focus 5: a token is pasted, rotated, and the tile must follow at once.
test(
  "a token pasted with surrounding spaces or CRLF is accepted; the stored value is trimmed",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath, credentialsFile } = await setup(t);
    const result = await run(
      [configPath, "mac", "--agent-token"],
      [`  ${TOKEN}  \r\n`],
    );
    assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.err);
    assert.deepEqual((await readCredentialFile(credentialsFile)).get("mac"), {
      agentToken: TOKEN,
    });
  },
);

test(
  "a malformed token, an empty answer or an early EOF writes nothing and does not print the value",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath, credentialsFile } = await setup(t);
    for (const keys of [
      ["short\n"],
      [`${TOKEN}X\n`],
      [`${TOKEN.slice(1)}\n`],
      [`${TOKEN.slice(0, 42)}+\n`],
      [`${TOKEN.slice(0, 20)} ${TOKEN.slice(21)}\n`],
      ["\n"],
      [],
    ]) {
      const result = await run([configPath, "mac", "--agent-token"], keys);
      assert.equal(result.code, 1, JSON.stringify(keys));
      assert.match(result.err, /저장하지 않았습니다|Input ended/);
      for (const key of keys)
        if (key.trim().length > 3)
          assert.ok(
            !(result.out + result.err).includes(key.trim()),
            "value echoed",
          );
    }
    await assert.rejects(stat(credentialsFile), { code: "ENOENT" });
  },
);

test("an agent target needs --agent-token, and the flag is refused on RDP and VNC targets; nothing is prompted", async (t) => {
  const { configPath, dir } = await setup(t);
  const env = {};
  const needsFlag = await run([configPath, "mac"], [], { env, home: dir });
  assert.equal(needsFlag.code, 2);
  assert.match(needsFlag.err, /--agent-token/);
  assert.equal(needsFlag.out, "");
  for (const id of ["ubuntu-server", "mac-vnc"]) {
    const refused = await run([configPath, id, "--agent-token"], [], {
      env,
      home: dir,
    });
    assert.equal(refused.code, 2, id);
    assert.match(refused.err, /--agent-token/);
    assert.equal(refused.out, "");
  }
});

test("a token can never be passed as an argument, and unknown or repeated flags are usage errors", async (t) => {
  const { configPath, dir } = await setup(t);
  const env = {};
  for (const argv of [
    [configPath, "mac", `--agent-token=${TOKEN}`],
    [configPath, "mac", "--agent-token", TOKEN],
    [configPath, "mac", "--agent-token", "--agent-token"],
    [configPath, "mac", "--token"],
    [configPath, "mac", "-t"],
  ]) {
    const result = await run(argv, [], { env, home: dir });
    assert.equal(result.code, 2, argv.join(" "));
    assert.match(result.err, /Usage/);
    assert.ok(!result.err.includes(TOKEN) && !result.out.includes(TOKEN));
    assert.equal(result.out, "");
  }
});

test(
  "with one positional argument the config comes from GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG, as for the other targets",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath, credentialsFile, dir } = await setup(t);
    const result = await run(["mac", "--agent-token"], [`${TOKEN}\n`], {
      env: { GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG: configPath },
      home: join(dir, "unused-home"),
    });
    assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.err);
    assert.equal(
      (await readCredentialFile(credentialsFile)).get("mac").agentToken,
      TOKEN,
    );
  },
);

test(
  "replacing a target's old password entry with a token works even though the shapes differ",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath, credentialsFile } = await setup(t);
    await writeCredentialFile(
      credentialsFile,
      new Map([
        ["mac", { username: "owner", password: "oldpass" }],
        ["mac-vnc", { username: "owner", password: "macpass" }],
      ]),
    );
    const result = await run(
      [configPath, "mac", "--agent-token"],
      [`${TOKEN}\n`],
    );
    assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.err);
    const entries = await readCredentialFile(credentialsFile);
    assert.deepEqual(
      entries.get("mac"),
      { agentToken: TOKEN },
      "no password is kept next to the token",
    );
    assert.deepEqual(entries.get("mac-vnc"), {
      username: "owner",
      password: "macpass",
    });
  },
);

test(
  "a file in which another target has the wrong shape is refused untouched",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath, credentialsFile } = await setup(t);
    await writeCredentialFile(
      credentialsFile,
      new Map([["mac-vnc", { agentToken: TOKEN }]]),
    );
    const before = await stat(credentialsFile);
    const result = await run(
      [configPath, "mac", "--agent-token"],
      [`${TOKEN}\n`],
    );
    assert.equal(result.code, 1);
    assert.match(result.err, /Refusing to modify/);
    assert.equal((await stat(credentialsFile)).mtimeMs, before.mtimeMs);
  },
);

test(
  "the RDP and VNC prompts are labelled for what they ask for",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const { configPath } = await setup(t);
    const rdp = await run([configPath, "ubuntu-server"], ["u\n", "p\n", "p\n"]);
    assert.match(rdp.out, /사용자 이름: /);
    assert.match(rdp.out, /비밀번호: /);
    const vnc = await run(
      [configPath, "mac-vnc"],
      ["owner\n", "macpass\n", "macpass\n"],
    );
    assert.match(vnc.out, /macOS 계정/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(vnc.out, /토큰/);
  },
);
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `node --test test/config-agent.test.js test/credentials-agent.test.js test/set-credential-agent.test.js 2>&1 | grep -E "ℹ (pass|fail)"`
Expected: most tests fail. `config-agent`: 4 of 5 (the agent protocol is refused with `Only RDP and VNC are supported.`); `credentials-agent`: 5 of 7 (an `agentToken` field is an "unknown field"); `set-credential-agent`: 7 of 9 (`--agent-token` is treated as the config path).

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `--agent-token`**

`scripts/set-credential.mjs`: the only flag is a bare `--agent-token` (anything attached to it, or any other flag, is a usage error so that a secret cannot travel in `argv`); an agent target requires it and RDP/VNC targets refuse it, before anything is prompted; the token is read like a password (no echo), trimmed, checked against `AGENT_TOKEN` and stored as the whole entry `{agentToken}`; the target's own old entry may have the other shape (its method was changed in the configuration) and is replaced; the VNC prompt now says what it really asks for (the macOS account password).

```diff
--- a/scripts/set-credential.mjs
+++ b/scripts/set-credential.mjs
@@ -6,7 +6,7 @@
 import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
 import { StringDecoder } from "node:string_decoder";
 import { loadConfig } from "../server/config.js";
-import { readCredentialFile } from "../server/credentials.js";
+import { AGENT_TOKEN, readCredentialFile } from "../server/credentials.js";
 
 // Secrets are read from the terminal only: never from argv, the environment, or a file
 // named on the command line, so they cannot appear in `ps`, shell history, or logs.
@@ -156,17 +156,26 @@
 }
 
 const usage =
-  "Usage: node scripts/set-credential.mjs [<gateway-config.json>] <target-id>\n" +
-  "Without a config path, GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG or ~/.config/gome-remote/gateway.json is used.";
+  "Usage: node scripts/set-credential.mjs [<gateway-config.json>] <target-id> [--agent-token]\n" +
+  "Without a config path, GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG or ~/.config/gome-remote/gateway.json is used.\n" +
+  "--agent-token is for (and required by) Mac agent targets; the token itself is typed at the prompt.";
 
 // One argument is the target ID and the config path is implied; two arguments name both.
+// The only flag is a bare --agent-token: a token (or anything else) attached to it, or any
+// other flag, is a usage error, so a secret can never travel in the argument list.
 function resolveArguments(argv, env, home) {
-  if (argv.length === 2) return argv;
-  if (argv.length === 1)
+  const flags = argv.filter((a) => a.startsWith("-"));
+  const positional = argv.filter((a) => !a.startsWith("-"));
+  if (flags.length > 1 || flags.some((f) => f !== "--agent-token"))
+    return undefined;
+  const agentToken = flags.length === 1;
+  if (positional.length === 2) return [...positional, agentToken];
+  if (positional.length === 1)
     return [
       env.GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG ||
         join(home, ".config", "gome-remote", "gateway.json"),
-      argv[0],
+      positional[0],
+      agentToken,
     ];
   return undefined;
 }
@@ -187,7 +196,7 @@
   };
   const resolved = resolveArguments(argv, env, home);
   if (!resolved) return fail(usage, 2);
-  const [configPath, targetId] = resolved;
+  const [configPath, targetId, agentToken] = resolved;
   if (requireTty && !stdin.isTTY)
     return fail("Run this in an interactive terminal; do not pipe secrets.", 2);

+        .map((t) => [t.id, t.protocol]),
+    );
+    entries = await readCredentialFile(config.credentialsFile, uid, protocols);
   } catch (error) {
     return fail(`Refusing to modify the credentials file: ${error.message}`);
   }

     stdout.write(
       `${target.name} (${target.id}): 비밀번호는 화면에 표시되지 않고 ${config.credentialsFile} 에만 저장됩니다.\n`,

+      const token = (
+        await prompter.ask(
+          "에이전트 토큰 (Mac의 install.sh가 보여 준 값을 붙여 넣으세요): ",
+          { secret: true },
+        )
+      ).trim();
+      if (!AGENT_TOKEN.test(token))
+        return fail(
+          "토큰은 43자의 영문·숫자·-·_ 여야 합니다. 저장하지 않았습니다.",
+        );
+      entries.set(target.id, { agentToken: token });
+      await writeCredentialFile(config.credentialsFile, entries);
+      stdout.write(`저장했습니다: ${target.id}\n`);
+      return 0;
+    }
     // RDP needs an account name. VNC takes one when the server uses Apple Remote
     // Desktop authentication (macOS Screen Sharing); empty means password only.
     const answer = await prompter.ask(
@@ -224,7 +267,9 @@
     if (username === undefined && target.protocol === "rdp")
       return fail("사용자 이름이 비어 있어 저장하지 않았습니다.");
     const password = await prompter.ask(
-      target.protocol === "rdp" ? "비밀번호: " : "VNC 비밀번호: ",
+      target.protocol === "rdp"
+        ? "비밀번호: "
+        : "비밀번호 (macOS 계정 비밀번호, 사용자 이름을 비웠다면 VNC 비밀번호): ",
       { secret: true },
```

### Task 2: Gateway relay `POST /api/agent-sessions` and agent state in `/api/targets`

Spec: D1 (the gateway only relays), D2 (token to the agent), D5 (status), D7 (routes, limits, error classes), section 6 (error wording), section 8 (fake-agent tests).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/agent-client.js`, `test/agent-relay.test.js`
- Modify: `server/gateway.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 1): credential entries `{agentToken}` through `credentials.lookup(target)`; `validateConfig` targets with `protocol: "agent"`, `hostname`, `port`.
- Produces:
  - `createAgentClient({ address, now, log, request, statusTimeoutMs = 2000, sessionTimeoutMs = 10000 })` returns `{ status(target, token), createSession(target, token, { width, height, sdp }) }`. `address(target)` returns `{ host, port }` (default: the target's `hostname` and `port`; tests point it at a fake). `status` resolves `{ issue: "none" | "permission" | "locked", display: "ok" | "asleep" | "none", lastCaptureAt: number | null }` or `undefined` (offline or not understood); results are cached 10 s per target and token, simultaneous calls share one query. `createSession` resolves the answer SDP or throws `AgentError`.
  - `AgentError` with `kind: "rejected" | "token" | "unavailable"` and `code: "permission" | "locked"` for `rejected`.
  - `normaliseStatus(raw)`: the only place that decides what a status document means.
  - `createGateway(config, { credentials, probe, dist, now, agent = createAgentClient({ now }) })`.
  - `POST /api/agent-sessions` `{ targetId, width, height, sdp }`: 200 `{ sdp }`; 400 invalid request; 404 unknown target; 409 `{ error, code: "permission" | "locked" | "setup" }`; 413 body over 64 KiB; 415 not JSON; 424 `{ error: "연결할 수 없습니다.", code: "unavailable" }` for everything else (unreachable, timeout, bad token, nonsense).
  - `/api/targets` for an agent target: `{ id, name, platform, protocol: "agent", persistent: false, online, ready, agent?: { issue, display, lastCaptureAt } }`; `ready` = has a token, `online` = the agent answered a status request.
  - `AGENT_MESSAGES`, `validateSize(width, height)` exported from `server/gateway.js`.
  - The agent's HTTP contract this client speaks (implemented by Task 6): `GET /v1/status` returns `{version, screen: "granted"|"denied"|"suspect", accessibility: "granted"|"denied", display: "ok"|"asleep"|"none", locked: boolean, lastCaptureAt: integer|null}`; `POST /v1/sessions` takes `{width, height, sdp}` and returns `{sdp}`, or 403 `{code:"permission"}`, 423 `{code:"locked"}`, 401 for a wrong token.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

The test runs the real gateway on a real unix socket against a fake agent (an HTTP server on loopback that records requests); nothing in it knows the gateway's internals.

Create `test/agent-relay.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import http from "node:http";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { createGateway, AGENT_MESSAGES } from "../server/gateway.js";
import { createAgentClient, normaliseStatus } from "../server/agent-client.js";
import { validateConfig } from "../server/config.js";
import { listenOnSocket } from "../server/listen.js";

// The gateway runs on a real unix socket; the Mac agent is a fake HTTP server on loopback
// that records what it receives. Nothing here knows the gateway's internals.
const posix = process.platform !== "win32";
const LOGIN = "owner@example.com";
const ORIGIN = "http://127.0.0.1:38989";
const TOKEN = "T".repeat(43);
const OFFER =
  "v=0\r\no=- 1 2 IN IP4 0.0.0.0\r\nm=video 9 UDP/TLS/RTP/SAVPF 102\r\n";
const HEALTHY = {
  version: "0.1.0",
  screen: "granted",
  accessibility: "granted",
  display: "ok",
  locked: false,
  lastCaptureAt: 1_760_400_000,
};
const rdp = {
  id: "ubuntu",
  name: "Ubuntu",
  protocol: "rdp",
  platform: "linux",
  hostname: "100.64.0.10",
  port: 3390,
};
const macAgent = {
  id: "mac",
  name: "Mac (새 방식)",
  protocol: "agent",
  platform: "mac",
  hostname: "100.64.0.20",
};

function request(
  socketPath,
  path,
  { method = "GET", headers = {}, body } = {},
) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const req = http.request(
      { socketPath, path, method, headers },
      async (res) => {
        const chunks = [];
        for await (const chunk of res) chunks.push(chunk);
        const text = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8");
        let json;
        try {
          json = JSON.parse(text);
        } catch {}
        resolve({ status: res.statusCode, text, json });
      },
    );
    req.on("error", reject);
    req.end(body);
  });
}

async function fixture(t, { token = TOKEN } = {}) {
  const seen = []; // what the fake agent received
  const agentBehaviour = {
    status: (_req, res) => reply(res, 200, HEALTHY),
    session: (req, res, body) => reply(res, 200, { sdp: "v=0\r\nanswer" }),
  };
  const sockets = new Set();
  const fake = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    const chunks = [];
    req.on("data", (c) => chunks.push(c));
    req.on("end", () => {
      const body = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8");
      seen.push({
        method: req.method,
        path: req.url,
        authorization: req.headers.authorization,
        contentType: req.headers["content-type"],
        host: req.headers.host,
        body,
      });
      if (req.url === "/v1/status") agentBehaviour.status(req, res);
      else if (req.url === "/v1/sessions")
        agentBehaviour.session(req, res, body ? JSON.parse(body) : undefined);
      else reply(res, 404, {});
    });
  });
  fake.on("connection", (socket) => {
    sockets.add(socket);
    socket.on("close", () => sockets.delete(socket));
  });
  fake.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
  await once(fake, "listening");
  const agentPort = fake.address().port;

  let clock = 1_000_000;
  const logs = [];
  const entries = { mac: token ? { agentToken: token } : undefined };
  const credentials = {
    entries,
    lookup: async (target) => entries[target.id],
  };
  const agent = createAgentClient({
    address: () => ({ host: "127.0.0.1", port: agentPort }),
    now: () => clock,
    log: (line) => logs.push(line),
    statusTimeoutMs: 150,
    sessionTimeoutMs: 300,
  });
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "gr-agent-"));
  const socketPath = join(dir, "g.sock");
  const config = validateConfig({
    socketPath,
    publicOrigin: ORIGIN,
    allowedLogins: [LOGIN],
    credentialsFile: join(dir, "credentials.json"),
    targets: [rdp, macAgent],
  });
  const gateway = createGateway(config, {
    credentials,
    probe: async () => true,
    agent,
    now: () => clock,
  });
  await listenOnSocket(gateway.server, socketPath);
  t.after(async () => {
    await gateway.close();
    for (const socket of sockets) socket.destroy();
    await new Promise((resolve) => fake.close(resolve));
    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });
  const call = (path, { login = LOGIN, headers = {}, ...rest } = {}) =>
    request(socketPath, path, {
      ...rest,
      headers: {
        ...(login ? { "Tailscale-User-Login": login } : {}),
        ...headers,
      },
    });
  const post = (input, { headers = {}, raw, ...rest } = {}) =>
    call("/api/agent-sessions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers },
      body: raw ?? JSON.stringify(input),
      ...rest,
    });
  return {
    seen,
    logs,
    agentBehaviour,
    call,
    post,
    entries,
    advance: (ms) => (clock += ms),
    stopAgent: () => {
      for (const socket of sockets) socket.destroy();
      return new Promise((resolve) => fake.close(resolve));
    },
  };
}

function reply(res, status, value) {
  res.writeHead(status, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
  res.end(typeof value === "string" ? value : JSON.stringify(value));
}

const good = { targetId: "mac", width: 2560, height: 1440, sdp: OFFER };
const tile = async (f) =>
  (await f.call("/api/targets")).json.targets.find((t) => t.id === "mac");

// ---- /api/targets ----

test(
  "an agent tile is online with the agent's state, and the other targets are unchanged",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const { status, json } = await f.call("/api/targets");
    assert.equal(status, 200);
    const mac = json.targets.find((x) => x.id === "mac");
    assert.deepEqual(mac, {
      id: "mac",
      name: "Mac (새 방식)",
      platform: "mac",
      protocol: "agent",
      persistent: false,
      online: true,
      ready: true,
      agent: { issue: "none", display: "ok", lastCaptureAt: 1_760_400_000 },
    });
    const ubuntu = json.targets.find((x) => x.id === "ubuntu");
    assert.equal(ubuntu.online, true);
    assert.equal(ubuntu.agent, undefined);
    // The status request carried the token and nothing else of the gateway's.
    assert.equal(f.seen[0].path, "/v1/status");
    assert.equal(f.seen[0].authorization, `Bearer ${TOKEN}`);
  },
);

test(
  "the tile carries enumerations only: no token, no address, no agent text",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) =>
      reply(res, 200, {
        ...HEALTHY,
        note: "secret detail",
        hostname: "evil.example",
      });
    const { text } = await f.call("/api/targets");
    for (const leak of [
      TOKEN,
      "100.64.0.20",
      "18471",
      "secret detail",
      "evil.example",
      "0.1.0",
    ])
      assert.ok(!text.includes(leak), leak);
  },
);

test("the agent's state becomes issue permission, locked or none", () => {
  const cases = [
    [{}, "none"],
    [{ screen: "denied" }, "permission"],
    [{ screen: "suspect" }, "permission"],
    [{ accessibility: "denied" }, "permission"],
    [{ locked: true }, "locked"],
    [{ locked: true, screen: "denied" }, "permission"],
  ];
  for (const [change, issue] of cases)
    assert.equal(
      normaliseStatus({ ...HEALTHY, ...change }).issue,
      issue,
      JSON.stringify(change),
    );
  assert.equal(
    normaliseStatus({ ...HEALTHY, lastCaptureAt: null }).lastCaptureAt,
    null,
  );
  assert.equal(
    normaliseStatus({ ...HEALTHY, display: "asleep" }).display,
    "asleep",
  );
});

test("a status the gateway does not understand makes the agent look offline", () => {
  for (const bad of [
    undefined,
    null,
    [],
    "ok",
    {},
    { ...HEALTHY, screen: "yes" },
    { ...HEALTHY, accessibility: undefined },
    { ...HEALTHY, display: "ON" },
    { ...HEALTHY, locked: "false" },
    { ...HEALTHY, lastCaptureAt: -1 },
    { ...HEALTHY, lastCaptureAt: 1.5 },
    { ...HEALTHY, lastCaptureAt: "now" },
    { ...HEALTHY, lastCaptureAt: undefined },
  ])
    assert.equal(normaliseStatus(bad), undefined, JSON.stringify(bad));
});

test(
  "without a token the tile needs setup and the agent is never asked",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t, { token: null });
    const mac = await tile(f);
    assert.equal(mac.ready, false);
    assert.equal(mac.online, false);
    assert.equal(mac.agent, undefined);
    assert.equal(f.seen.length, 0);
  },
);

test(
  "an unreachable, slow or confused agent is offline, within the time limit",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = () => {}; // never answers
    const started = Date.now();
    const slow = await tile(f);
    assert.equal(slow.online, false);
    assert.equal(slow.ready, true);
    assert.ok(
      Date.now() - started < 1500,
      "bounded by the 2 s status limit (150 ms here)",
    );
    f.advance(11_000);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) =>
      reply(res, 200, "<html>not json</html>");
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).online, false);
    f.advance(11_000);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) => reply(res, 500, { error: "boom" });
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).online, false);
    f.advance(11_000);
    await f.stopAgent();
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).online, false, "connection refused");
  },
);

// Review Focus 5: a token is pasted, rotated, and the tile must follow at once.
test(
  "status answers are cached for 10 seconds, requests share one query, and a new token is a new question",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    let answered = 0;
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) => {
      answered++;
      setTimeout(() => reply(res, 200, HEALTHY), 30);
    };
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 6 }, () => f.call("/api/targets")));
    assert.equal(answered, 1, "six simultaneous page loads, one query");
    f.advance(9_999);
    await f.call("/api/targets");
    assert.equal(answered, 1, "still cached one millisecond before expiry");
    f.advance(1);
    await f.call("/api/targets");
    assert.equal(answered, 2, "expired at 10 s");
    f.entries.mac = { agentToken: "N".repeat(43) };
    await f.call("/api/targets");
    assert.equal(
      answered,
      3,
      "set-credential takes effect without waiting for the cache",
    );
    assert.equal(f.seen.at(-1).authorization, `Bearer ${"N".repeat(43)}`);
  },
);

test(
  "an offline answer is cached too, so a dead agent is probed once per 10 seconds",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) => reply(res, 503, {});
    await f.call("/api/targets");
    await f.call("/api/targets");
    await f.call("/api/targets");
    assert.equal(f.seen.filter((s) => s.path === "/v1/status").length, 1);
  },
);

// ---- the token ----

test(
  "a rejected token is logged once with the target ID and never with the token, and looks offline",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) =>
      reply(res, 401, { error: "unauthorized" });
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).online, false);
    f.advance(11_000);
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).online, false);
    assert.equal(f.logs.length, 1, "logged once, not at every probe");
    assert.match(f.logs[0], /"mac".*token/);
    assert.ok(!f.logs.join("\n").includes(TOKEN));
    // Recovery re-arms the log line.
    f.advance(11_000);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) => reply(res, 200, HEALTHY);
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).online, true);
    f.advance(11_000);
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) => reply(res, 401, {});
    await tile(f);
    assert.equal(f.logs.length, 2);
  },
);

// ---- POST /api/agent-sessions ----

test(
  "an offer is relayed with the token, the size and the SDP, and the answer comes back",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const { status, json } = await f.post(good);
    assert.equal(status, 200);
    assert.deepEqual(json, { sdp: "v=0\r\nanswer" });
    const relayed = f.seen.find((s) => s.path === "/v1/sessions");
    assert.equal(relayed.method, "POST");
    assert.equal(relayed.authorization, `Bearer ${TOKEN}`);
    assert.match(relayed.contentType, /^application\/json/);
    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(relayed.body), {
      width: 2560,
      height: 1440,
      sdp: OFFER,
    });
  },
);

test(
  "every /api route refuses a missing or foreign login and a foreign Origin, before the agent is contacted",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    for (const login of [null, "", "other@example.com", LOGIN.toUpperCase()]) {
      const refused = await f.post(good, { login });
      assert.equal(refused.status, 403, String(login));
      assert.equal(refused.json.code, "login");
    }
    const origin = await f.post(good, {
      headers: { Origin: "https://evil.example" },
    });
    assert.equal(origin.status, 403);
    assert.equal(origin.json.code, "origin");
    const same = await f.post(good, { headers: { Origin: ORIGIN } });
    assert.equal(same.status, 200);
    assert.equal(f.seen.filter((s) => s.path === "/v1/sessions").length, 1);
  },
);

test(
  "the request is validated before the agent hears of it",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const expectations = [
      [
        "not json content type",
        () => f.post(good, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" } }),
        415,
      ],
      [
        "no content type",
        () =>
          f.call("/api/agent-sessions", {
            method: "POST",
            body: JSON.stringify(good),
          }),
        415,
      ],
      ["body is not JSON", () => f.post(null, { raw: "{nope" }), 400],
      ["body is an array", () => f.post([good]), 400],
      ["sdp missing", () => f.post({ ...good, sdp: undefined }), 400],
      ["sdp is a number", () => f.post({ ...good, sdp: 7 }), 400],
      [
        "sdp does not start with v=0",
        () => f.post({ ...good, sdp: "hello" }),
        400,
      ],
      ["width too small", () => f.post({ ...good, width: 639 }), 400],
      ["height too small", () => f.post({ ...good, height: 479 }), 400],
      ["width too large", () => f.post({ ...good, width: 3841 }), 400],
      ["height too large", () => f.post({ ...good, height: 2161 }), 400],
      ["width fractional", () => f.post({ ...good, width: 1920.5 }), 400],
      ["width a string", () => f.post({ ...good, width: "1920" }), 400],
      ["size missing", () => f.post({ targetId: "mac", sdp: OFFER }), 400],
      ["unknown target", () => f.post({ ...good, targetId: "nope" }), 404],
      [
        "a target that is not an agent",
        () => f.post({ ...good, targetId: "ubuntu" }),
        400,
      ],
      [
        "a client-supplied token",
        () => f.post({ ...good, agentToken: TOKEN }),
        400,
      ],
      [
        "a client-supplied password",
        () => f.post({ ...good, password: "x" }),
        400,
      ],
    ];
    for (const [what, send, status] of expectations)
      assert.equal((await send()).status, status, what);
    assert.equal(f.seen.length, 0, "the agent was never contacted");
    // The limits are inclusive.
    for (const [width, height] of [
      [640, 480],
      [3840, 2160],
    ])
      assert.equal((await f.post({ ...good, width, height })).status, 200);
  },
);

test(
  "the body limit is 64 KiB: one byte over is 413, exactly at the limit is relayed",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const make = (bytes) => {
      const shell = JSON.stringify({ ...good, sdp: "v=0\r\n" });
      const pad = bytes - Buffer.byteLength(shell);
      return JSON.stringify({ ...good, sdp: `v=0\r\n${"a".repeat(pad)}` });
    };
    const atLimit = make(64 * 1024);
    assert.equal(Buffer.byteLength(atLimit), 64 * 1024);
    assert.equal((await f.post(null, { raw: atLimit })).status, 200);
    const over = make(64 * 1024 + 1);
    assert.equal((await f.post(null, { raw: over })).status, 413);
    assert.equal(f.seen.filter((s) => s.path === "/v1/sessions").length, 1);
  },
);

test(
  "a target without a token answers 409 setup and the agent is not asked",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t, { token: null });
    const { status, json } = await f.post(good);
    assert.equal(status, 409);
    assert.equal(json.code, "setup");
    assert.equal(f.seen.length, 0);
  },
);

test(
  "a refusal for a state reason reaches the page with its code and the gateway's own text",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    for (const [agentStatus, code] of [
      [403, "permission"],
      [423, "locked"],
    ]) {
      f.agentBehaviour.session = (_req, res) =>
        reply(res, agentStatus, {
          error: "text the page must never see",
          code,
        });
      const { status, json } = await f.post(good);
      assert.equal(status, 409, code);
      assert.deepEqual(json, { error: AGENT_MESSAGES[code], code });
    }
  },
);

test(
  "every other agent failure is 'unavailable', in the gateway's words, and a bad token is logged apart",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const unavailable = {
      error: AGENT_MESSAGES.unavailable,
      code: "unavailable",
    };
    const failures = {
      "401 bad token": (_req, res) =>
        reply(res, 401, { error: "unauthorized" }),
      "403 with an unknown code": (_req, res) =>
        reply(res, 403, { code: "mystery" }),
      "403 without a code": (_req, res) => reply(res, 403, {}),
      "423 without a code": (_req, res) => reply(res, 423, {}),
      400: (_req, res) => reply(res, 400, { error: "offer rejected" }),
      500: (_req, res) => reply(res, 500, { error: "session failed" }),
      "503 busy": (_req, res) => reply(res, 503, "busy"),
      "200 not JSON": (_req, res) => reply(res, 200, "<html>"),
      "200 without sdp": (_req, res) => reply(res, 200, {}),
      "200 sdp not an SDP": (_req, res) => reply(res, 200, { sdp: "hello" }),
      hangs: () => {},
      "closes the connection": (req) => req.destroy(),
    };
    for (const [what, behaviour] of Object.entries(failures)) {
      f.agentBehaviour.session = behaviour;
      const { status, json, text } = await f.post(good);
      assert.equal(status, 424, what);
      assert.deepEqual(json, unavailable, what);
      assert.ok(
        !text.includes("session failed") && !text.includes("offer rejected"),
        what,
      );
    }
    assert.equal(
      f.logs.filter((l) => /token/.test(l)).length,
      1,
      "only the 401 mentions the token",
    );
    assert.ok(!f.logs.join("\n").includes(TOKEN));
  },
);

test(
  "a refusal drops the cached status so the tile shows the new state at once",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).agent.issue, "none");
    f.agentBehaviour.session = (_req, res) =>
      reply(res, 423, { code: "locked" });
    f.agentBehaviour.status = (_req, res) =>
      reply(res, 200, { ...HEALTHY, locked: true });
    assert.equal((await f.post(good)).status, 409);
    assert.equal((await tile(f)).agent.issue, "locked");
  },
);

test(
  "the Guacamole route refuses an agent target instead of building a connection for it",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const { status, json } = await f.call("/api/sessions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ targetId: "mac", width: 1920, height: 1080 }),
    });
    assert.equal(status, 400);
    assert.equal(json.ticket, undefined);
  },
);

test(
  "a second offer while the first is waiting is relayed independently (the agent decides about takeover)",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const f = await fixture(t);
    const answers = ["v=0\r\nfirst", "v=0\r\nsecond"];
    let n = 0;
    f.agentBehaviour.session = (_req, res) =>
      reply(res, 200, { sdp: answers[n++] });
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([f.post(good), f.post(good)]);
    assert.deepEqual([a.status, b.status], [200, 200]);
    assert.deepEqual(new Set([a.json.sdp, b.json.sdp]), new Set(answers));
  },
);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the agent client**

Create `server/agent-client.js`:

```js
import http from "node:http";

// The gateway's side of the Mac agent's HTTP interface (agent/mac, spec D2, D5, D7):
//   GET  /v1/status    -> {screen, accessibility, display, locked, lastCaptureAt, version}
//   POST /v1/sessions  -> {width, height, sdp} in, {sdp} out
// Both carry the target's bearer token. Only enumerations from the status ever leave this
// module; nothing the agent says is forwarded as text.
export const STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS = 2_000;
export const STATUS_TTL_MS = 10_000;
export const SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
const MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 128 * 1024;

// kind "rejected": the agent refuses for a state reason, `code` is "permission" or "locked".
// kind "token": the agent did not accept the gateway's token.
// kind "unavailable": unreachable, too slow, or an answer that makes no sense.
export class AgentError extends Error {
  constructor(kind, code) {
    super(`Mac agent: ${kind}${code ? ` (${code})` : ""}`);
    this.name = "AgentError";
    this.kind = kind;
    this.code = code;
  }
}

const ENUMS = {
  screen: ["granted", "denied", "suspect"],
  accessibility: ["granted", "denied"],
  display: ["ok", "asleep", "none"],
};

// Reduces a status document to what the page may see, or undefined when it is not one.
//   issue: "none" | "permission" | "locked"   (permission also covers a suspect screen)
//   display: "ok" | "asleep" | "none"
//   lastCaptureAt: seconds since the epoch, or null
export function normaliseStatus(raw) {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)) return undefined;
  for (const [key, allowed] of Object.entries(ENUMS))
    if (!allowed.includes(raw[key])) return undefined;
  if (typeof raw.locked !== "boolean") return undefined;
  const at = raw.lastCaptureAt;
  if (at !== null && !(Number.isSafeInteger(at) && at >= 0)) return undefined;
  const issue =
    raw.screen !== "granted" || raw.accessibility !== "granted"
      ? "permission"
      : raw.locked
        ? "locked"
        : "none";
  return { issue, display: raw.display, lastCaptureAt: at };
}

// One HTTP exchange with a hard deadline and a response size cap. Resolves
// {status, json} (json undefined when the body is not JSON); rejects on a network error,
// on the deadline, or on an oversized answer.
function exchange({ host, port }, { method, path, token, body, timeoutMs }) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const payload = body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body);
    const req = http.request(
      {
        host,
        port,
        method,
        path,
        agent: false,
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          ...(payload === undefined
            ? {}
            : {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "Content-Length": Buffer.byteLength(payload),
              }),
        },
      },
      (res) => {
        const chunks = [];
        let size = 0;
        res.on("data", (chunk) => {
          size += chunk.length;
          if (size > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) req.destroy(new Error("too large"));
          else chunks.push(chunk);
        });
        res.on("error", reject);
        res.on("end", () => {
          let json;
          try {
            json = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
          } catch {
            json = undefined;
          }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, json });
        });
      },
    );
    req.on("error", reject);
    req.end(payload);
  });
}

// `address(target)` says where the agent listens (tests point it at a local fake).
export function createAgentClient({
  address = (target) => ({ host: target.hostname, port: target.port }),
  now = Date.now,
  log = console.error,
  request = exchange,
  statusTimeoutMs = STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS,
  sessionTimeoutMs = SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS,
} = {}) {
  // One status answer per target for 10 s (offline counts as an answer, so a dead agent
  // is probed once per 10 s, not once per page load). A different token is a different
  // question: after `set-credential` the tile does not wait for the old answer to expire.
  const cache = new Map();
  const pending = new Map();
  const tokenLogged = new Set();

  const rejectedToken = (target) => {
    if (tokenLogged.has(target.id)) return;
    tokenLogged.add(target.id);
    log(
      `Mac agent for target "${target.id}" did not accept the gateway token (401).`,
    );
  };

  return {
    // The normalised status, or undefined when the agent is offline or not understood.
    async status(target, token) {
      const hit = cache.get(target.id);
      if (hit && hit.token === token && now() - hit.at < STATUS_TTL_MS)
        return hit.value;
      const running = pending.get(target.id);
      if (running && running.token === token) return running.promise;
      const promise = (async () => {
        let value;
        try {
          const res = await request(address(target), {
            method: "GET",
            path: "/v1/status",
            token,
            timeoutMs: statusTimeoutMs,
          });
          if (res.status === 200) {
            value = normaliseStatus(res.json);
            tokenLogged.delete(target.id);
          } else if (res.status === 401) rejectedToken(target);
        } catch {
          value = undefined;
        }
        cache.set(target.id, { at: now(), token, value });
        return value;
      })().finally(() => {
        if (pending.get(target.id)?.promise === promise)
          pending.delete(target.id);
      });
      pending.set(target.id, { token, promise });
      return promise;
    },

    // Relays an offer; resolves the answer SDP or throws an AgentError.
    async createSession(target, token, { width, height, sdp }) {
      let res;
      try {
        res = await request(address(target), {
          method: "POST",
          path: "/v1/sessions",
          token,
          body: { width, height, sdp },
          timeoutMs: sessionTimeoutMs,
        });
      } catch {
        throw new AgentError("unavailable");
      }
      if (res.status === 200) {
        const answer = res.json?.sdp;
        if (typeof answer === "string" && answer.startsWith("v=0"))
          return answer;
        throw new AgentError("unavailable");
      }
      if (res.status === 401) {
        rejectedToken(target);
        throw new AgentError("token");
      }
      const code = res.json?.code;
      if (
        (res.status === 403 || res.status === 423) &&
        ["permission", "locked"].includes(code)
      ) {
        cache.delete(target.id); // the tile should show the new state at once
        throw new AgentError("rejected", code);
      }
      throw new AgentError("unavailable");
    },
  };
}
```
