### Task 3: The page's Mac session (`agent` module) and its integration into the viewer

Spec: D3 (page side), D4 (input), D5 (page states), D8 (new module, resolution, `flow.ts` reused, Guacamole untouched), section 6 and 7 (texts), O1 (the viewer's own pointer). Review Focus 1, 3, 4.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/agent-input.ts`, `src/agent.ts`, `src/agent-messages.ts`, `test/agent-input.test.js`, `test/agent-session.test.js`, `test/agent-style.test.js`, `test/viewer-agent.test.js`, `test/helpers/agent-harness.js`
- Modify: `src/api.ts`, `src/flow.ts`, `src/tiles.ts`, `src/types.d.ts`, `src/main.ts`, `src/style.css`, `test/helpers/viewer-harness.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 2): `POST /api/agent-sessions` with `{targetId, width, height, sdp}` answering `{sdp}` or an error with `code`; `/api/targets` entries with `protocol: "agent"` and `agent: { issue, display, lastCaptureAt }`.
- Produces:
  - `src/agent-input.ts`: `contentRect(box, video)`, `normalise(clientX, clientY, box, video, clamp)`, `wheelDelta(event, pageHeight)`, `keyCode(event)`, `splitText(text, max = 2000)`, `message.{pointer,button,wheel,key,text,release,size}`, `preferredCodecs(all)`, `MAX_WHEEL`.
  - `src/agent.ts`: `createAgentSession({ signal, onFirstFrame, onEnded })` returns `AgentSession { element, video, start(), sendSize(w,h), sendText(text): boolean, release(), focus(), close(), clickAt(fx,fy), watchFrames(ms) }`; `EndReason = "disconnected" | "unsupported" | "timeout"`; `start()` rejects with the gateway's error (the caller shows it), everything later ends through `onEnded` exactly once and never after `close()`.
  - `src/agent-messages.ts`: `endText(reason)` returns `{title, detail}` for `permission | locked | unavailable | setup | unsupported | timeout`, else `undefined`; `startFailureReason(error)`.
  - `src/api.ts`: `ApiError.code`, `api.agentSession(input)`, `forBridge(state)` (an app older than bridge version 3 is told `"rdp"` instead of `"agent"`).
  - `src/flow.ts`: `SessionEffects.ended(reason?: string)`, `flow.ended(reason?)`.
  - `src/tiles.ts`: `TileState` gains `"permission"` and `"locked"`, `stateText(target, state)`, `LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED = false` (the P1 switch).
  - Test helpers: `agentHarness()` (fake DOM, `RTCPeerConnection`, data channel and timers for `src/agent.ts`); `viewer({ agentStart })` gains `agents` (recorded fake sessions) and `agentRequests()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests for the pure input helpers and the session module**

Create `test/agent-input.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { loadTs } from "./helpers/load-ts.js";

const plain = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
const {
  contentRect,
  normalise,
  wheelDelta,
  keyCode,
  splitText,
  message,
  preferredCodecs,
  MAX_WHEEL,
} = await loadTs("agent-input.ts");

const wide = { left: 10, top: 20, width: 1000, height: 1000 }; // a square element
const video = { width: 1920, height: 1080 }; // a 16:9 picture

test("the picture is centred in its element with bars above and below (letterbox)", () => {
  const rect = plain(contentRect(wide, video));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(rect.width - 1000) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(rect.height - 562.5) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(rect.left - 10) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(rect.top - (20 + (1000 - 562.5) / 2)) < 1e-9);
});

test("a tall picture gets bars at the sides (pillarbox) and an exact fit gets none", () => {
  const rect = plain(
    contentRect(
      { left: 0, top: 0, width: 1600, height: 900 },
      { width: 900, height: 900 },
    ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(rect, { left: 350, top: 0, width: 900, height: 900 });
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(contentRect({ left: 5, top: 6, width: 1920, height: 1080 }, video)),
    { left: 5, top: 6, width: 1920, height: 1080 },
  );
});

test("an element or video without a size has no picture", () => {
  for (const [box, size] of [
    [wide, { width: 0, height: 0 }],
    [wide, { width: 1920, height: 0 }],
    [{ ...wide, width: 0 }, video],
    [{ ...wide, height: NaN }, video],
  ])
    assert.equal(contentRect(box, size), undefined);
});

test("positions are fractions of the picture, not of the element", () => {
  const rect = contentRect(wide, video);
  const middle = plain(
    normalise(10 + 500, rect.top + rect.height / 2, wide, video, true),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(middle, { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 });
  const corner = plain(normalise(rect.left, rect.top, wide, video, true));
  assert.deepEqual(corner, { x: 0, y: 0 });
  const far = plain(
    normalise(
      rect.left + rect.width,
      rect.top + rect.height,
      wide,
      video,
      true,
    ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(far, { x: 1, y: 1 });
  const quarter = plain(
    normalise(
      rect.left + rect.width / 4,
      rect.top + rect.height * 0.75,
      wide,
      video,
      false,
    ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(quarter, { x: 0.25, y: 0.75 });
});

// Review Focus 1: a window whose shape differs from the Mac's screen has black bars.
test("a position in a bar is clamped to the picture's edge, or refused when clamping is off", () => {
  const rect = contentRect(wide, video);
  const inTopBar = (clamp) => normalise(500, rect.top - 30, wide, video, clamp);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(inTopBar(true)), { x: 0.49, y: 0 });
  assert.equal(inTopBar(false), undefined);
  const inBottomBar = (clamp) =>
    normalise(500, rect.top + rect.height + 30, wide, video, clamp);
  assert.equal(plain(inBottomBar(true)).y, 1);
  assert.equal(inBottomBar(false), undefined);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(normalise(-500, 5000, wide, video, true)), {
    x: 0,
    y: 1,
  });
  assert.equal(normalise(NaN, 10, wide, video, true), undefined);
  assert.equal(normalise(Infinity, 10, wide, video, true), undefined);
  assert.equal(normalise(5, 5, wide, { width: 0, height: 0 }, true), undefined);
});

test("coordinates carry at most five decimals", () => {
  const rect = contentRect(wide, video);
  const p = normalise(
    rect.left + rect.width / 3,
    rect.top + rect.height / 3,
    wide,
    video,
    true,
  );
  assert.equal(p.x, 0.33333);
  assert.equal(p.y, 0.33333);
});

test("wheel deltas are pixels; lines and pages are converted, and everything is cut to 2000", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: 0, deltaY: 100, deltaMode: 0 }, 900)),
    { x: 0, y: 100 },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: 1, deltaY: -3, deltaMode: 1 }, 900)),
    { x: 16, y: -48 },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: 0, deltaY: 1, deltaMode: 2 }, 900)),
    { x: 0, y: 900 },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: 0, deltaY: 5, deltaMode: 2 }, 900)),
    { x: 0, y: MAX_WHEEL },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: -1e9, deltaY: 1e9, deltaMode: 0 }, 900)),
    { x: -MAX_WHEEL, y: MAX_WHEEL },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: NaN, deltaY: 0.4, deltaMode: 0 }, 900)),
    { x: 0, y: 0 },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(wheelDelta({ deltaX: 0, deltaY: 0.6, deltaMode: 0 }, 900)),
    { x: 0, y: 1 },
  );
});

test("the Hangul key is Lang1 whichever way the browser names it; unnamed keys are not sent", () => {
  assert.equal(keyCode({ code: "KeyA", key: "a" }), "KeyA");
  assert.equal(keyCode({ code: "Lang1", key: "HangulMode" }), "Lang1");
  assert.equal(keyCode({ code: "", key: "HangulMode" }), "Lang1");
  assert.equal(keyCode({ code: "Unidentified", key: "HangulMode" }), "Lang1");
  assert.equal(keyCode({ code: "", key: "a" }), undefined);
  assert.equal(
    keyCode({ code: "Unidentified", key: "Unidentified" }),
    undefined,
  );
});

// Review Focus 3: text pasted into the text window is emoji, line breaks and long.
test("text is cut into pieces that never split an emoji or a surrogate pair", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(plain(splitText("")), []);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(splitText("짧은 글")), ["짧은 글"]);
  const long = "a".repeat(4500);
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(splitText(long).map((p) => p.length)),
    [2000, 2000, 500],
  );
  // A pair sits across the 2000 boundary: it moves wholly to the next piece.
  const text = `${"a".repeat(1999)}😀${"b".repeat(10)}`;
  const pieces = splitText(text);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(pieces.map((p) => p.length)), [1999, 12]);
  assert.equal(pieces.join(""), text);
  for (const piece of pieces) {
    const first = piece.charCodeAt(0);
    const last = piece.charCodeAt(piece.length - 1);
    assert.ok(
      !(first >= 0xdc00 && first <= 0xdfff),
      "starts with a low surrogate",
    );
    assert.ok(
      !(last >= 0xd800 && last <= 0xdbff),
      "ends with a high surrogate",
    );
  }
  // Every piece fits the agent's 8 KiB limit even when it is all four-byte characters.
  const emoji = "😀".repeat(3000);
  for (const piece of splitText(emoji))
    assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(piece) + 40 <= 8 * 1024, "piece too large");
  assert.equal(splitText(emoji).join(""), emoji);
});

test("every message has the shape agent/mac/src/protocol.rs parses", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.pointer({ x: 0.25, y: 1 })), {
    type: "pointer",
    x: 0.25,
    y: 1,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.button(2, true, { x: 0, y: 0.5 })), {
    type: "button",
    b: 2,
    down: true,
    x: 0,
    y: 0.5,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.wheel({ x: 0, y: -120 })), {
    type: "wheel",
    dx: 0,
    dy: -120,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.key("KeyA", false)), {
    type: "key",
    code: "KeyA",
    down: false,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.text("안녕")), {
    type: "text",
    s: "안녕",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.release()), { type: "release" });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(message.size(1920, 1080)), {
    type: "size",
    w: 1920,
    h: 1080,
  });
  for (const small of [
    message.pointer({ x: 0.123456, y: 0.123456 }),
    message.button(0, false, { x: 1, y: 1 }),
    message.key("ControlRight", true),
  ])
    assert.ok(small.length < 512);
});

const codecs = [
  { mimeType: "video/VP8" },
  { mimeType: "video/VP9", sdpFmtpLine: "profile-id=0" },
  { mimeType: "video/AV1" },
  {
    mimeType: "video/H264",
    sdpFmtpLine:
      "level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=1;profile-level-id=640c1f",
  },
  {
    mimeType: "video/H264",
    sdpFmtpLine:
      "level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=0;profile-level-id=42e01f",
  },
  {
    mimeType: "video/H264",
    sdpFmtpLine:
      "level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=1;profile-level-id=42e01f",
  },
  { mimeType: "video/rtx", sdpFmtpLine: "apt=102" },
  { mimeType: "video/rtx", sdpFmtpLine: "apt=104" },
  { mimeType: "video/red" },
  { mimeType: "video/ulpfec" },
];

test("the codec preference keeps H.264 and video/rtx, drops the rest, and puts constrained baseline mode 1 first", () => {
  const list = plain(preferredCodecs(codecs));
  assert.deepEqual(
    list.map((c) => `${c.mimeType}|${c.sdpFmtpLine ?? ""}`),
    [
      "video/H264|level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=1;profile-level-id=42e01f",
      "video/H264|level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=0;profile-level-id=42e01f",
      "video/H264|level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=1;profile-level-id=640c1f",
      "video/rtx|apt=102",
      "video/rtx|apt=104",
    ],
  );
  assert.ok(
    list.some((c) => c.mimeType === "video/rtx"),
    "RTX must stay: without it a lost packet freezes the picture for a second",
  );
});

test("a browser that offers no H.264 gets a list without it, which the session refuses to use", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(
      preferredCodecs([{ mimeType: "video/VP8" }, { mimeType: "video/rtx" }]),
    ).map((c) => c.mimeType),
    ["video/rtx"],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(plain(preferredCodecs([])), []);
});
```

Create the fake browser `test/helpers/agent-harness.js`:

```js
import { loadTs } from "./load-ts.js";

// A just-enough browser for src/agent.ts: elements that record their listeners, a fake
// RTCPeerConnection whose data channel records what is sent, and timers the test advances
// by hand. It knows none of the rules under test.
export class FakeNode {
  constructor(tag) {
    this.tag = tag;
    this.listeners = {};
    this.children = [];
    this.attributes = {};
    this.classNames = new Set();
    this.rect = { left: 0, top: 0, width: 1000, height: 1000 };
    this.clientHeight = 1000;
    this.focused = 0;
    this.videoWidth = 0;
    this.videoHeight = 0;
    this.frameCallbacks = [];
    this.srcObject = undefined;
    this.played = 0;
  }
  set className(value) {
    this.classNames = new Set(String(value).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
  }
  get className() {
    return [...this.classNames].join(" ");
  }
  append(...nodes) {
    this.children.push(...nodes);
  }
  setAttribute(name, value) {
    this.attributes[name] = String(value);
  }
  addEventListener(type, listener, options) {
    (this.listeners[type] ??= []).push({ listener, options });
  }
  removeEventListener(type, listener) {
    this.listeners[type] = (this.listeners[type] ?? []).filter(
      (l) => l.listener !== listener,
    );
  }
  count(type) {
    return (this.listeners[type] ?? []).length;
  }
  getBoundingClientRect() {
    return this.rect;
  }
  focus() {
    this.focused++;
  }
  play() {
    this.played++;
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
  requestVideoFrameCallback(callback) {
    this.frameCallbacks.push(callback);
  }
  // Test helpers.
  fire(type, event = {}) {
    const e = {
      prevented: false,
      stopped: false,
      preventDefault() {
        this.prevented = true;
      },
      stopPropagation() {
        this.stopped = true;
      },
      ...event,
    };
    for (const { listener } of this.listeners[type] ?? []) listener(e);
    return e;
  }
  showFrame(now = 0) {
    const callbacks = this.frameCallbacks.splice(0);
    for (const callback of callbacks) callback(now, {});
  }
}

export class FakeChannel {
  constructor(label, options) {
    this.label = label;
    this.options = options;
    this.readyState = "connecting";
    this.bufferedAmount = 0;
    this.sent = [];
    this.closed = 0;
    this.onclose = null;
  }
  open() {
    this.readyState = "open";
  }
  send(text) {
    if (this.readyState !== "open") throw new Error("InvalidStateError");
    this.sent.push(text);
  }
  close() {
    this.closed++;
    this.readyState = "closed";
  }
  // The messages sent so far, parsed.
  messages() {
    return this.sent.map((s) => JSON.parse(s));
  }
}

export async function agentHarness({
  codecs = [
    { mimeType: "video/VP8" },
    {
      mimeType: "video/H264",
      sdpFmtpLine:
        "level-asymmetry-allowed=1;packetization-mode=1;profile-level-id=42e01f",
    },
    { mimeType: "video/rtx", sdpFmtpLine: "apt=102" },
  ],
  peerConnection = true,
  gathering = "immediate", // "immediate" | "never"
  frameCallbacks = true,
} = {}) {
  const peers = [];
  const nodes = [];
  const documentNode = new FakeNode("document");
  let now = 0;
  let nextTimer = 1;
  const timers = new Map();

  class FakePeerConnection {
    constructor(config) {
      this.config = config;
      this.connectionState = "new";
      this.iceGatheringState = "new";
      this.transceivers = [];
      this.channels = [];
      this.closed = 0;
      this.remote = undefined;
      this.localDescription = null;
      this.onconnectionstatechange = null;
      this.ontrack = null;
      this.onicegatheringstatechange = null;
      peers.push(this);
    }
    addTransceiver(kind, init) {
      const transceiver = {
        kind,
        init,
        receiver: {},
        preferences: undefined,
        setCodecPreferences(list) {
          this.preferences = list;
        },
      };
      this.transceivers.push(transceiver);
      return transceiver;
    }
    createDataChannel(label, options) {
      const channel = new FakeChannel(label, options);
      this.channels.push(channel);
      return channel;
    }
    async createOffer() {
      return { type: "offer", sdp: "v=0\r\no=offer" };
    }
    async setLocalDescription(description) {
      this.localDescription = description;
      if (gathering === "immediate") this.iceGatheringState = "complete";
    }
    async setRemoteDescription(description) {
      this.remote = description;
    }
    async getStats() {
      return new Map([
        [
          "v",
          {
            type: "inbound-rtp",
            kind: "video",
            freezeCount: this.freezeCount ?? 0,
          },
        ],
      ]);
    }
    close() {
      this.closed++;
      this.connectionState = "closed";
    }
    // Test helpers.
    completeGathering() {
      this.iceGatheringState = "complete";
      this.onicegatheringstatechange?.();
    }
    setState(state) {
      this.connectionState = state;
      this.onconnectionstatechange?.();
    }
    track(stream) {
      this.ontrack?.({ streams: stream ? [stream] : [], track: { id: "t" } });
    }
  }

  const document = {
    createElement(tag) {
      const node = new FakeNode(tag);
      nodes.push(node);
      return node;
    },
    addEventListener: (...args) => documentNode.addEventListener(...args),
    removeEventListener: (...args) => documentNode.removeEventListener(...args),
  };
  class FakeMediaStream {
    constructor(tracks) {
      this.tracks = tracks;
    }
  }
  const setTimeoutFake = (callback, ms) => {
    timers.set(nextTimer, { callback, at: now + ms });
    return nextTimer++;
  };
  const clearTimeoutFake = (id) => timers.delete(id);
  const rafQueue = [];

  const globals = {
    document,
    setTimeout: setTimeoutFake,
    clearTimeout: clearTimeoutFake,
    requestAnimationFrame: (callback) => rafQueue.push(callback),
    performance: { now: () => now },
    MediaStream: FakeMediaStream,
    RTCRtpReceiver: { getCapabilities: () => ({ codecs }) },
    ...(peerConnection ? { RTCPeerConnection: FakePeerConnection } : {}),
  };
  const input = await loadTs("agent-input.ts");
  const mod = await loadTs("agent.ts", globals, { "./agent-input": input });

  const deps = {
    offers: [],
    answers: [],
    frames: 0,
    ended: [],
    signalError: undefined,
    signal(offer) {
      deps.offers.push(offer);
      if (deps.signalError) return Promise.reject(deps.signalError);
      return Promise.resolve({ sdp: "v=0\r\nanswer" });
    },
    onFirstFrame() {
      deps.frames++;
    },
    onEnded(reason) {
      deps.ended.push(reason);
    },
  };
  const settle = async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++)
      await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
  };
  const advance = (ms) => {
    const end = now + ms;
    for (;;) {
      const due = [...timers]
        .filter(([, t]) => t.at <= end)
        .sort((a, b) => a[1].at - b[1].at);
      if (!due.length) break;
      const [id, timer] = due[0];
      timers.delete(id);
      now = timer.at;
      timer.callback();
    }
    now = end;
  };
  const session = mod.createAgentSession(deps);
  const [surface, video] = nodes;
  video.videoWidth = 1920;
  video.videoHeight = 1080;
  if (!frameCallbacks) video.requestVideoFrameCallback = undefined;
  // Brings the session up to "channel open, picture showing".
  const connect = async () => {
    const started = session.start();
    await settle();
    const pc = peers.at(-1);
    pc.channels[0].open();
    pc.setState("connected");
    await started;
    pc.track({ id: "stream" });
    video.showFrame(1);
    return pc;
  };
  return {
    session,
    mod,
    surface,
    video,
    documentNode,
    deps,
    peers,
    settle,
    advance,
    connect,
    timers,
    rafQueue,
    flushFrame: () => rafQueue.splice(0).forEach((callback) => callback()),
    channel: () => peers.at(-1)?.channels[0],
    messages: () => peers.at(-1).channels[0].messages(),
  };
}
```

Create `test/agent-session.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { agentHarness } from "./helpers/agent-harness.js";

const plain = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));

// ---- connection setup ----

test("the page offers a receive-only H.264 video with RTX preferred-kept, and an ordered input channel, with no ICE servers", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const started = h.session.start();
  await h.settle();
  const pc = h.peers[0];
  assert.deepEqual(plain(pc.config), { iceServers: [] });
  assert.equal(pc.transceivers.length, 1);
  assert.equal(pc.transceivers[0].kind, "video");
  assert.equal(pc.transceivers[0].init.direction, "recvonly");
  const offered = plain(pc.transceivers[0].preferences).map((c) => c.mimeType);
  assert.deepEqual(
    offered,
    ["video/H264", "video/rtx"],
    "VP8 is gone, RTX is kept",
  );
  assert.equal(pc.transceivers[0].receiver.jitterBufferTarget, 0);
  assert.equal(pc.transceivers[0].receiver.playoutDelayHint, 0);
  assert.equal(pc.channels.length, 1);
  assert.equal(pc.channels[0].label, "input");
  assert.equal(pc.channels[0].options.ordered, true);
  await started;
  assert.deepEqual(h.deps.offers, ["v=0\r\no=offer"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(pc.remote), { type: "answer", sdp: "v=0\r\nanswer" });
});

test("the offer waits for candidate gathering, but never longer than 1.5 seconds", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness({ gathering: "never" });
  const started = h.session.start();
  await h.settle();
  assert.equal(h.deps.offers.length, 0, "still gathering");
  h.advance(1499);
  await h.settle();
  assert.equal(h.deps.offers.length, 0);
  h.advance(1);
  await h.settle();
  assert.equal(h.deps.offers.length, 1, "sent at the limit");
  await started;
  // And when gathering finishes first, the wait ends early.
  const early = await agentHarness({ gathering: "never" });
  const second = early.session.start();
  await early.settle();
  early.peers[0].completeGathering();
  await early.settle();
  assert.equal(early.deps.offers.length, 1);
  await second;
});

// Review Focus 4: a browser without H.264 or video frame callbacks must fail clearly.
test("a browser without H.264, or without WebRTC, ends the session at once and never contacts the gateway", async () => {
  const noH264 = await agentHarness({
    codecs: [{ mimeType: "video/VP8" }, { mimeType: "video/rtx" }],
  });
  await noH264.session.start();
  assert.deepEqual(noH264.deps.ended, ["unsupported"]);
  assert.equal(noH264.deps.offers.length, 0);
  assert.equal(noH264.peers.length, 0, "no connection was even created");
  const none = await agentHarness({ peerConnection: false });
  await none.session.start();
  assert.deepEqual(none.deps.ended, ["unsupported"]);
  assert.equal(none.deps.offers.length, 0);
  const empty = await agentHarness({ codecs: [] });
  await empty.session.start();
  assert.deepEqual(empty.deps.ended, ["unsupported"]);
});

test("a gateway refusal rejects start with that error, closes the connection and reports no end", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const refusal = Object.assign(new Error("권한"), { code: "permission" });
  h.deps.signalError = refusal;
  await assert.rejects(h.session.start(), (error) => error === refusal);
  assert.equal(h.peers[0].closed, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(
    h.deps.ended,
    [],
    "the caller shows the refusal; onEnded is for later endings",
  );
  assert.equal(h.deps.frames, 0);
});

// ---- the picture and the ending ----

test("the first picture reports the connection once; a picture that never comes ends it after 20 seconds", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const pc = await h.connect();
  assert.equal(h.deps.frames, 1);
  assert.equal(pc.closed, 0);
  h.video.showFrame(2);
  assert.equal(h.deps.frames, 1, "reported once");
  h.advance(60_000);
  assert.deepEqual(
    h.deps.ended,
    [],
    "a picture arrived, so there is nothing to time out",
  );

  const silent = await agentHarness();
  const started = silent.session.start();
  await silent.settle();
  await started;
  silent.advance(19_999);
  assert.deepEqual(silent.deps.ended, []);
  silent.advance(1);
  assert.deepEqual(silent.deps.ended, ["timeout"]);
  assert.equal(silent.peers[0].closed, 1);
});

// Review Focus 4: a browser without H.264 or video frame callbacks must fail clearly.
test("a browser without video frame callbacks reports the first picture when playback starts", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness({ frameCallbacks: false });
  const started = h.session.start();
  await h.settle();
  await started;
  assert.equal(h.deps.frames, 0);
  h.video.fire("playing");
  assert.equal(h.deps.frames, 1);
});

test("the stream is attached to the video element and played", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const pc = await h.connect();
  assert.equal(h.video.srcObject.id, "stream");
  assert.ok(h.video.played >= 1);
  pc.track(undefined);
  assert.equal(
    h.video.srcObject.tracks[0].id,
    "t",
    "a track without a stream gets its own",
  );
});

for (const state of ["failed", "closed"])
  test(`connection state ${state} ends the session once as disconnected`, async () => {
    const h = await agentHarness();
    const pc = await h.connect();
    pc.setState(state);
    pc.setState(state);
    assert.deepEqual(h.deps.ended, ["disconnected"]);
    assert.ok(pc.closed >= 1, "the connection is released");
  });

test("the transient 'disconnected' state is waited out, not reported", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const pc = await h.connect();
  pc.setState("disconnected");
  assert.deepEqual(h.deps.ended, []);
  pc.setState("connected");
  assert.deepEqual(h.deps.ended, []);
});

test("a closed data channel ends the session", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.channel().onclose();
  assert.deepEqual(h.deps.ended, ["disconnected"]);
});

test("nothing is reported after the page itself closes the session", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const pc = await h.connect();
  h.session.close();
  pc.setState("closed");
  pc.channels[0].onclose?.();
  h.advance(60_000);
  assert.deepEqual(h.deps.ended, []);
});

// ---- the mouse ----

const move = (h, x, y) =>
  h.surface.fire("mousemove", { clientX: x, clientY: y });
// The element is 1000 x 1000 and the picture 16:9, so it spans y 218.75 .. 781.25.
const MIDDLE = { clientX: 500, clientY: 500 };

test("moves are coalesced to one per frame, carry picture coordinates, and the newest wins", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  for (const x of [100, 200, 300, 400, 500]) move(h, x, 500);
  assert.equal(h.messages().length, 0, "nothing is sent before the frame");
  h.flushFrame();
  const sent = h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "pointer");
  assert.deepEqual(sent, [{ type: "pointer", x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }]);
  move(h, 0, 500);
  h.flushFrame();
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().at(-1), { type: "pointer", x: 0, y: 0.5 });
});

test("a press first sends the latest position, then the button, in that order", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  move(h, 250, 500);
  h.surface.fire("mousedown", { ...MIDDLE, button: 0 });
  const types = h.messages().map((m) => m.type);
  assert.deepEqual(types, ["pointer", "button"]);
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages()[1], {
    type: "button",
    b: 0,
    down: true,
    x: 0.5,
    y: 0.5,
  });
  h.flushFrame();
  assert.equal(h.messages().length, 2, "the flushed move is not sent twice");
});

test("all three buttons map to the DOM numbers, and the back and forward buttons are ignored", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  for (const button of [0, 1, 2]) {
    h.surface.fire("mousedown", { ...MIDDLE, button });
    h.documentNode.fire("mouseup", { ...MIDDLE, button });
  }
  for (const button of [3, 4]) {
    h.surface.fire("mousedown", { ...MIDDLE, button });
    h.documentNode.fire("mouseup", { ...MIDDLE, button });
  }
  const buttons = h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "button");
  assert.deepEqual(
    buttons.map((m) => [m.b, m.down]),
    [
      [0, true],
      [0, false],
      [1, true],
      [1, false],
      [2, true],
      [2, false],
    ],
  );
});

// Review Focus 1: a window whose shape differs from the Mac's screen has black bars.
test("a press on a black bar is not a click on the Mac, and its release is not sent either", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.surface.fire("mousedown", { clientX: 500, clientY: 100, button: 0 }); // above the picture
  h.documentNode.fire("mouseup", { clientX: 500, clientY: 100, button: 0 });
  h.surface.fire("mousedown", { clientX: 500, clientY: 900, button: 2 }); // below it
  assert.deepEqual(
    h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "button"),
    [],
  );
});

// Review Focus 1: a window whose shape differs from the Mac's screen has black bars.
test("a drag that ends outside the picture or the element still releases the button at the nearest edge", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.surface.fire("mousedown", { clientX: 900, clientY: 500, button: 0 });
  h.documentNode.fire("mouseup", { clientX: 5000, clientY: -300, button: 0 });
  const up = h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "button" && !m.down);
  assert.deepEqual(up, [{ type: "button", b: 0, down: false, x: 1, y: 0 }]);
  // A second release of the same button is a stray event and is not sent.
  h.documentNode.fire("mouseup", { clientX: 5, clientY: 5, button: 0 });
  assert.equal(
    h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "button" && !m.down).length,
    1,
  );
});

test("the context menu is suppressed and a click focuses the surface", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  assert.equal(h.surface.fire("contextmenu").prevented, true);
  const down = h.surface.fire("mousedown", { ...MIDDLE, button: 2 });
  assert.equal(down.prevented, true);
  assert.ok(h.surface.focused >= 1);
});

test("the wheel is sent in pixels, only over the picture, and its page scroll is suppressed", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  const over = h.surface.fire("wheel", {
    ...MIDDLE,
    deltaX: 0,
    deltaY: 100,
    deltaMode: 0,
  });
  assert.equal(over.prevented, true);
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().at(-1), { type: "wheel", dx: 0, dy: 100 });
  const before = h.messages().length;
  h.surface.fire("wheel", {
    clientX: 500,
    clientY: 50,
    deltaX: 0,
    deltaY: 100,
    deltaMode: 0,
  });
  h.surface.fire("wheel", { ...MIDDLE, deltaX: 0, deltaY: 0, deltaMode: 0 });
  assert.equal(
    h.messages().length,
    before,
    "over a bar, or a zero delta: nothing",
  );
  h.surface.fire("wheel", { ...MIDDLE, deltaX: 0, deltaY: -3, deltaMode: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().at(-1), { type: "wheel", dx: 0, dy: -48 });
  assert.equal(
    h.surface.listeners.wheel[0].options.passive,
    false,
    "must be able to prevent default",
  );
});

test("when the channel is backed up, moves are dropped but clicks are not", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.channel().bufferedAmount = 65 * 1024;
  move(h, 300, 500);
  h.flushFrame();
  assert.equal(h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "pointer").length, 0);
  h.surface.fire("mousedown", { ...MIDDLE, button: 0 });
  assert.equal(h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "button").length, 1);
});

// ---- the keyboard ----

test("keys are sent by physical code, down and up, with the browser's own action suppressed", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  const down = h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "KeyA", key: "a" });
  h.surface.fire("keyup", { code: "KeyA", key: "a" });
  assert.equal(down.prevented, true);
  assert.equal(down.stopped, true);
  assert.deepEqual(
    h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "key"),
    [
      { type: "key", code: "KeyA", down: true },
      { type: "key", code: "KeyA", down: false },
    ],
  );
});

test("modifiers keep their physical positions and key repeat sends every key-down", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  for (const code of ["ControlLeft", "AltRight", "MetaLeft"])
    h.surface.fire("keydown", { code, key: "x" });
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++)
    h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "KeyZ", key: "z", repeat: i > 0 });
  const keys = h.messages().filter((m) => m.type === "key");
  assert.deepEqual(
    keys.slice(0, 3).map((m) => m.code),
    ["ControlLeft", "AltRight", "MetaLeft"],
  );
  assert.equal(keys.filter((m) => m.code === "KeyZ").length, 3);
});

test("the Hangul key is sent as Lang1 however the browser names it; unnamed keys are not sent", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "Lang1", key: "HangulMode" });
  h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "", key: "HangulMode" });
  const before = h.messages().length;
  h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "", key: "Dead" });
  h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "Unidentified", key: "Unidentified" });
  assert.equal(h.messages().length, before);
  assert.deepEqual(
    h
      .messages()
      .filter((m) => m.type === "key")
      .map((m) => m.code),
    ["Lang1", "Lang1"],
  );
});

// ---- letting go ----

test("losing focus releases everything, and the button that was down is not released twice", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.surface.fire("mousedown", { ...MIDDLE, button: 0 });
  h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "ShiftLeft", key: "Shift" });
  h.surface.fire("blur");
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().at(-1), { type: "release" });
  const before = h.messages().length;
  h.documentNode.fire("mouseup", { ...MIDDLE, button: 0 });
  assert.equal(
    h.messages().length,
    before,
    "the late mouse-up after a release is not a second release",
  );
});

test("release() always sends a release, so a dialog, fullscreen switch or end can rely on it", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  const before = h.messages().length;
  h.session.release();
  h.session.release();
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().slice(before), [
    { type: "release" },
    { type: "release" },
  ]);
});

test("closing the session sends a release, then closes the channel and the connection, once", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const pc = await h.connect();
  h.session.close();
  h.session.close();
  assert.deepEqual(pc.channels[0].messages().at(-1), { type: "release" });
  assert.equal(pc.channels[0].closed, 1);
  assert.equal(pc.closed, 1);
  assert.equal(
    h.documentNode.count("mouseup"),
    0,
    "the document listener is removed",
  );
  assert.equal(h.video.srcObject, null);
});

test("nothing is sent while the channel is not open, and text reports that it was not sent", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const started = h.session.start();
  await h.settle();
  const channel = h.channel();
  h.surface.fire("keydown", { code: "KeyA", key: "a" });
  h.session.release();
  h.session.sendSize(1920, 1080);
  assert.equal(h.session.sendText("hi"), false);
  assert.deepEqual(channel.sent, []);
  await started;
});

// ---- size and text ----

test("a size change is sent as a size message", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.session.sendSize(2560, 1440);
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().at(-1), { type: "size", w: 2560, h: 1440 });
});

// Review Focus 3: text pasted into the text window is emoji, line breaks and long.
test("text goes as text messages that never split an emoji", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  assert.equal(h.session.sendText("안녕"), true);
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().at(-1), { type: "text", s: "안녕" });
  const text = `${"a".repeat(1999)}😀tail`;
  assert.equal(h.session.sendText(text), true);
  const pieces = h
    .messages()
    .filter((m) => m.type === "text")
    .slice(1)
    .map((m) => m.s);
  assert.equal(pieces.join(""), text);
  assert.ok(pieces.length >= 2);
  for (const piece of pieces)
    assert.ok(!/^[\udc00-\udfff]|[\ud800-\udbff]$/.test(piece));
});

// ---- the measurement hooks ----

test("clickAt sends a move, a press and a release at the same picture position", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  await h.connect();
  h.session.clickAt(0.25, 0.75);
  assert.deepEqual(h.messages().slice(-3), [
    { type: "pointer", x: 0.25, y: 0.75 },
    { type: "button", b: 0, down: true, x: 0.25, y: 0.75 },
    { type: "button", b: 0, down: false, x: 0.25, y: 0.75 },
  ]);
});

test("watchFrames reports frame gaps over 500 ms and the freeze count that changed", async () => {
  const h = await agentHarness();
  const pc = await h.connect();
  const report = h.session.watchFrames(3000);
  await h.settle();
  h.video.showFrame(100);
  h.video.showFrame(200);
  h.video.showFrame(900); // a 700 ms gap
  pc.freezeCount = 2;
  h.video.showFrame(3200);
  const result = plain(await report);
  assert.equal(result.frames, 4);
  assert.equal(result.maxGapMs, 2300);
  assert.equal(result.gapsOver500ms, 2);
  assert.equal(result.freezeCount, 2);
});
```

Run: `node --test test/agent-input.test.js test/agent-session.test.js 2>&1 | grep -E "ENOENT|ℹ (pass|fail)"`
Expected: both files fail to load (`ENOENT ... src/agent-input.ts`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the pure input helpers**

Create `src/agent-input.ts`:

```ts
// The pure parts of the agent session's input (spec D4), free of the DOM so they can be
// tested directly: where the picture is on screen, how a pointer position becomes 0..1
// coordinates, how a wheel event becomes pixels, how text is cut into messages, and the
// exact shape of every message on the `input` data channel.

export interface Rect {
  left: number;
  top: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}
export interface VideoSize {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}
export interface Point {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

// Where `object-fit: contain` draws the picture inside its element. Undefined while the
// video has no size yet.
export function contentRect(box: Rect, video: VideoSize): Rect | undefined {
  if (!(video.width > 0) || !(video.height > 0)) return undefined;
  if (!(box.width > 0) || !(box.height > 0)) return undefined;
  const scale = Math.min(box.width / video.width, box.height / video.height);
  const width = video.width * scale;
  const height = video.height * scale;
  return {
    left: box.left + (box.width - width) / 2,
    top: box.top + (box.height - height) / 2,
    width,
    height,
  };
}

const unit = (value: number) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value));
const short = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 1e5) / 1e5;

// A pointer position as fractions of the *picture*, not of the element: the black bars of a
// letterboxed video are not part of the Mac's screen. With `clamp` a position in a bar
// becomes the nearest edge of the picture (a drag that leaves the picture keeps working);
// without it a position outside the picture is undefined (a press there is not a click on
// the Mac's edge).
export function normalise(
  clientX: number,
  clientY: number,
  box: Rect,
  video: VideoSize,
  clamp: boolean,
): Point | undefined {
  const content = contentRect(box, video);
  if (!content || !Number.isFinite(clientX) || !Number.isFinite(clientY))
    return undefined;
  const x = (clientX - content.left) / content.width;
  const y = (clientY - content.top) / content.height;
  if (!clamp && (x < 0 || x > 1 || y < 0 || y > 1)) return undefined;
  return { x: short(unit(x)), y: short(unit(y)) };
}

// The agent cuts larger values to this as well; cutting here keeps the message honest.
export const MAX_WHEEL = 2000;
const LINE_PIXELS = 16;

export interface WheelLike {
  deltaX: number;
  deltaY: number;
  deltaMode: number; // 0 pixels, 1 lines, 2 pages
}
export function wheelDelta(event: WheelLike, pageHeight: number): Point {
  const factor =
    event.deltaMode === 1
      ? LINE_PIXELS
      : event.deltaMode === 2
        ? pageHeight
        : 1;
  const cut = (value: number) =>
    Number.isFinite(value)
      ? Math.max(-MAX_WHEEL, Math.min(MAX_WHEEL, Math.round(value * factor)))
      : 0;
  return { x: cut(event.deltaX), y: cut(event.deltaY) };
}

// `KeyboardEvent.code` as the agent's key table knows it. Some browsers report the Korean
// Hangul key by `key` ("HangulMode") with a different or empty `code`.
export function keyCode(event: {
  code: string;
  key: string;
}): string | undefined {
  if (event.key === "HangulMode") return "Lang1";
  return event.code && event.code !== "Unidentified" ? event.code : undefined;
}

// Cuts text into pieces of at most `max` UTF-16 units that never split a surrogate pair,
// so every message stays far below the agent's 8 KiB limit (4 bytes per pair at worst).
export function splitText(text: string, max = 2000): string[] {
  const pieces: string[] = [];
  let start = 0;
  while (start < text.length) {
    let end = Math.min(text.length, start + max);
    if (end < text.length) {
      const last = text.charCodeAt(end - 1);
      if (last >= 0xd800 && last <= 0xdbff) end -= 1; // do not end on a high surrogate
    }
    pieces.push(text.slice(start, end));
    start = end;
  }
  return pieces;
}

// The wire format (agent/mac, src/protocol.rs). Buttons follow MouseEvent.button.
export const message = {
  pointer: (p: Point) => JSON.stringify({ type: "pointer", x: p.x, y: p.y }),
  button: (b: number, down: boolean, p: Point) =>
    JSON.stringify({ type: "button", b, down, x: p.x, y: p.y }),
  wheel: (d: Point) => JSON.stringify({ type: "wheel", dx: d.x, dy: d.y }),
  key: (code: string, down: boolean) =>
    JSON.stringify({ type: "key", code, down }),
  text: (s: string) => JSON.stringify({ type: "text", s }),
  release: () => JSON.stringify({ type: "release" }),
  size: (w: number, h: number) => JSON.stringify({ type: "size", w, h }),
};

// The codecs the page offers, in order: H.264 only (the Mac encodes in hardware), with
// the constrained-baseline, packetization-mode 1 variants first, and `video/rtx` KEPT.
// Without RTX the agent cannot answer a NACK and every lost packet waits for a keyframe
// (about one second of freeze); the spike lost that to a filter that only kept H.264.
export interface CodecLike {
  mimeType: string;
  sdpFmtpLine?: string;
}
export function preferredCodecs<T extends CodecLike>(all: readonly T[]): T[] {
  const kind = (codec: T) => codec.mimeType.toLowerCase();
  const rank = (codec: T) => {
    if (kind(codec) === "video/rtx") return 0;
    const fmtp = codec.sdpFmtpLine ?? "";
    const baseline = /profile-level-id=42e01f/i.test(fmtp);
    const mode1 = /packetization-mode=1/.test(fmtp);
    return baseline && mode1 ? 3 : baseline ? 2 : 1;
  };
  return all
    .filter((codec) => ["video/h264", "video/rtx"].includes(kind(codec)))
    .map((codec, index) => ({ codec, index }))
    .sort((a, b) => rank(b.codec) - rank(a.codec) || a.index - b.index)
    .map(({ codec }) => codec);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the session module**

Create `src/agent.ts`. The ordering rules that the tests pin: a press first flushes the coalesced move; a press outside the picture is ignored together with its release; the document (not only the surface) hears the release so a drag may end anywhere; `close()` sends `release` before it closes the channel; a failed `start()` throws instead of calling `onEnded`; a browser without H.264 ends the session before the gateway is contacted.

```ts
// The Mac agent session (spec D1, D3, D4, D5, D8): a receive-only WebRTC video, an `input`
// data channel back, and the page's keyboard and mouse turned into its messages. The
// offer goes to the gateway (`signal`), which relays it to the agent; media and input then
// flow directly between this page and the Mac over the tailnet. The Guacamole path in
// main.ts is not involved.
import {
  contentRect,
  keyCode,
  message,
  normalise,
  preferredCodecs,
  splitText,
  wheelDelta,
} from "./agent-input";

// Reasons a session ends by itself; the page turns each into its own words.
export type EndReason = "disconnected" | "unsupported" | "timeout";

export interface AgentDeps {
  // Sends the page's offer to the gateway and resolves the agent's answer SDP.
  signal(offerSdp: string): Promise<{ sdp: string }>;
  // The first picture is on screen.
  onFirstFrame(): void;
  // The session ended without the page asking for it (never called after close()).
  onEnded(reason: EndReason): void;
}

export interface FrameReport {
  frames: number;
  maxGapMs: number;
  gapsOver500ms: number;
  freezeCount: number | null;
}

export interface AgentSession {
  readonly element: HTMLElement;
  readonly video: HTMLVideoElement;
  // Rejects when the gateway refuses (the ApiError is the caller's to show); ends the
  // session through onEnded for everything that goes wrong afterwards.
  start(): Promise<void>;
  sendSize(width: number, height: number): void;
  sendText(text: string): boolean;
  // Lets go of every key and button (blur, dialog, fullscreen, end).
  release(): void;
  focus(): void;
  close(): void;
  // For the measurement mode (#measure): a click at a fraction of the picture, and a
  // report of how steadily frames arrive.
  clickAt(fx: number, fy: number): void;
  watchFrames(durationMs: number): Promise<FrameReport>;
}

export const GATHER_LIMIT_MS = 1500;
export const FIRST_FRAME_LIMIT_MS = 20_000;
// A move is not sent while this much is queued in the channel: the pointer catches up
// with its newest position instead of replaying an old path.
const MAX_BUFFERED = 64 * 1024;

export function createAgentSession(deps: AgentDeps): AgentSession {
  const surface = document.createElement("div");
  surface.tabIndex = 0;
  surface.className = "agent-surface";
  surface.setAttribute("aria-label", "원격 데스크톱 화면");
  const video = document.createElement("video");
  video.className = "agent-video";
  video.muted = true;
  video.autoplay = true;
  video.playsInline = true;
  surface.append(video);

  let pc: RTCPeerConnection | undefined;
  let channel: RTCDataChannel | undefined;
  let closed = false;
  let ended = false;
  let framed = false;
  let frameTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  const pressedButtons = new Set<number>();
  const pressedKeys = new Set<string>();
  let lastPoint = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 };

  const open = () => channel?.readyState === "open";
  const send = (text: string) => {
    if (open()) channel!.send(text);
  };

  // ---- pointer moves are coalesced to one per animation frame, and ordered before clicks ----
  let pendingMove: { x: number; y: number } | undefined;
  let moveScheduled = false;
  const flushMove = () => {
    moveScheduled = false;
    if (!pendingMove) return;
    const point = pendingMove;
    pendingMove = undefined;
    if ((channel?.bufferedAmount ?? 0) > MAX_BUFFERED) return;
    send(message.pointer(point));
  };
  const queueMove = (point: { x: number; y: number }) => {
    pendingMove = point;
    if (moveScheduled) return;
    moveScheduled = true;
    if (typeof requestAnimationFrame === "function")
      requestAnimationFrame(flushMove);
    else setTimeout(flushMove, 16);
  };

  const box = () => {
    const r = surface.getBoundingClientRect();
    return { left: r.left, top: r.top, width: r.width, height: r.height };
  };
  const at = (event: MouseEvent, clamp: boolean) =>
    normalise(
      event.clientX,
      event.clientY,
      box(),
      { width: video.videoWidth, height: video.videoHeight },
      clamp,
    );

  // ---- input listeners ----
  const onMouseMove = (event: MouseEvent) => {
    const point = at(event, true);
    if (point) queueMove(point);
  };
  const onMouseDown = (event: MouseEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    surface.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    if (event.button > 2) return; // back and forward buttons have no Mac meaning
    // A press on a black bar is not a click on the Mac's edge.
    const point = at(event, false);
    if (!point) return;
    flushMove();
    pressedButtons.add(event.button);
    lastPoint = point;
    send(message.button(event.button, true, point));
  };
  const onMouseUp = (event: MouseEvent) => {
    // Also heard on the document: a drag may end outside the picture or the window.
    if (!pressedButtons.has(event.button)) return;
    flushMove();
    pressedButtons.delete(event.button);
    const point = at(event, true) ?? lastPoint;
    send(message.button(event.button, false, point));
  };
  const onWheel = (event: WheelEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!at(event, false)) return; // over a bar, not over the Mac
    const d = wheelDelta(event, surface.clientHeight || 800);
    if (d.x === 0 && d.y === 0) return;
    flushMove();
    send(message.wheel(d));
  };
  const onKey = (down: boolean) => (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    const code = keyCode(event);
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    if (!code) return;
    if (down) pressedKeys.add(code);
    else pressedKeys.delete(code);
    send(message.key(code, down));
  };
  const onKeyDown = onKey(true);
  const onKeyUp = onKey(false);
  const onContextMenu = (event: Event) => event.preventDefault();
  const onBlur = () => release();

  surface.addEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
  surface.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown);
  document.addEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
  surface.addEventListener("wheel", onWheel, { passive: false });
  surface.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  surface.addEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
  surface.addEventListener("contextmenu", onContextMenu);
  surface.addEventListener("blur", onBlur);

  function release() {
    pendingMove = undefined;
    pressedButtons.clear();
    pressedKeys.clear();
    send(message.release());
  }

  // ---- the end of a session ----
  const teardown = () => {
    if (frameTimer !== undefined) clearTimeout(frameTimer);
    frameTimer = undefined;
    document.removeEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
    if (pc) {
      pc.onconnectionstatechange = null;
      pc.ontrack = null;
      pc.onicegatheringstatechange = null;
    }
    if (channel) channel.onclose = null;
    try {
      channel?.close();
    } catch {}
    try {
      pc?.close();
    } catch {}
    video.srcObject = null;
  };
  const end = (reason: EndReason) => {
    if (closed || ended) return;
    ended = true;
    teardown();
    deps.onEnded(reason);
  };

  const gathered = (connection: RTCPeerConnection) =>
    new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      if (connection.iceGatheringState === "complete") return resolve();
      const timer = setTimeout(resolve, GATHER_LIMIT_MS);
      connection.onicegatheringstatechange = () => {
        if (connection.iceGatheringState === "complete") {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          resolve();
        }
      };
    });

  const watchFirstFrame = () => {
    const shown = () => {
      if (framed || closed || ended) return;
      framed = true;
      if (frameTimer !== undefined) clearTimeout(frameTimer);
      frameTimer = undefined;
      deps.onFirstFrame();
    };
    if (typeof video.requestVideoFrameCallback === "function")
      video.requestVideoFrameCallback(() => shown());
    else video.addEventListener("playing", shown, { once: true });
    // A session that never shows a picture is over; ICE alone can take half a minute to give up.
    frameTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!framed) end("timeout");
    }, FIRST_FRAME_LIMIT_MS);
  };

  return {
    element: surface,
    video,

    async start() {
      const supported =
        typeof RTCPeerConnection === "function" &&
        typeof RTCRtpReceiver !== "undefined" &&
        typeof RTCRtpReceiver.getCapabilities === "function";
      const codecs = supported
        ? preferredCodecs(RTCRtpReceiver.getCapabilities("video")?.codecs ?? [])
        : [];
      if (!codecs.some((c) => c.mimeType.toLowerCase() === "video/h264")) {
        end("unsupported");
        return;
      }
      const connection = new RTCPeerConnection({ iceServers: [] });
      pc = connection;
      try {
        const transceiver = connection.addTransceiver("video", {
          direction: "recvonly",
        });
        transceiver.setCodecPreferences(codecs);
        // Both are hints that only some browsers know; neither failing matters.
        try {
          (transceiver.receiver as any).jitterBufferTarget = 0;
        } catch {}
        try {
          (transceiver.receiver as any).playoutDelayHint = 0;
        } catch {}
        channel = connection.createDataChannel("input", { ordered: true });
        channel.onclose = () => end("disconnected");
        connection.ontrack = (event) => {
          video.srcObject = event.streams[0] ?? new MediaStream([event.track]);
          void video.play().catch(() => {});
        };
        connection.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
          if (["failed", "closed"].includes(connection.connectionState))
            end("disconnected");
        };
        await connection.setLocalDescription(await connection.createOffer());
        await gathered(connection);
        const answer = await deps.signal(connection.localDescription!.sdp);
        if (closed || ended) return;
        await connection.setRemoteDescription({
          type: "answer",
          sdp: answer.sdp,
        });
        watchFirstFrame();
      } catch (error) {
        if (!closed && !ended) {
          ended = true; // a failed start is reported by the rejection, not by onEnded
          teardown();
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },

    sendSize(width, height) {
      send(message.size(width, height));
    },
    sendText(text) {
      if (!open()) return false;
      for (const piece of splitText(text)) send(message.text(piece));
      return true;
    },
    release,
    focus() {
      surface.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    },
    close() {
      if (closed) return;
      release(); // nothing stays pressed on the Mac when the page lets go
      closed = true;
      teardown();
    },

    clickAt(fx, fy) {
      const point = { x: fx, y: fy };
      send(message.pointer(point));
      send(message.button(0, true, point));
      send(message.button(0, false, point));
    },

    watchFrames(durationMs) {
      return new Promise<FrameReport>((resolve) => {
        const report: FrameReport = {
          frames: 0,
          maxGapMs: 0,
          gapsOver500ms: 0,
          freezeCount: null,
        };
        const started = performance.now();
        let previous = started;
        const freezes = async () => {
          try {
            let count: number | null = null;
            (await pc?.getStats())?.forEach((s: any) => {
              if (s.type === "inbound-rtp" && s.kind === "video")
                count = s.freezeCount ?? null;
            });
            return count;
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        };
        const base = freezes();
        const tick = (now: number) => {
          const gap = now - previous;
          previous = now;
          report.frames++;
          report.maxGapMs = Math.max(report.maxGapMs, gap);
          if (gap > 500) report.gapsOver500ms++;
          if (now - started >= durationMs) {
            void Promise.all([base, freezes()]).then(([a, b]) => {
              report.freezeCount = a !== null && b !== null ? b - a : null;
              resolve(report);
            });
          } else video.requestVideoFrameCallback(tick);
        };
        video.requestVideoFrameCallback(tick);
      });
    },
  };
}
```

Create `test/viewer-agent.test.js` (the tile states, starting a Mac session without touching Guacamole, the ended and refusal cards, release hand-over, size, menu actions, text):

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { viewer } from "./helpers/viewer-harness.js";
import { loadTs } from "./helpers/load-ts.js";

const plain = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
const ENDED = "연결이 끊겼습니다";
const response = (status, body) => ({
  status,
  ok: status < 400,
  json: async () => {
    if (body === undefined) throw new Error("not JSON");
    return body;
  },
});
const mac = {
  id: "mac",
  name: "Mac (새 방식)",
  platform: "mac",
  protocol: "agent",
  persistent: false,
  online: true,
  ready: true,
  agent: { issue: "none", display: "ok", lastCaptureAt: 1_760_400_000 },
};
const vnc = {
  ...mac,
  id: "mac-vnc",
  name: "Mac (VNC)",
  protocol: "vnc",
  agent: undefined,
};
const linux = {
  id: "linux",
  name: "Linux",
  platform: "linux",
  protocol: "rdp",
  persistent: true,
  online: true,
  ready: true,
};

async function opened(options = {}) {
  const v = await viewer({
    appMode: true,
    targets: [mac, vnc, linux],
    ...options,
  });
  v.tiles()[0].click();
  await v.settle();
  return v;
}

// ---- the tile states ----

const tilesModule = await loadTs("tiles.ts");
const { tileState, tileEnabled, stateText, LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED } = tilesModule;

test("an agent tile shows its own states: 권한 필요, 잠김, 오프라인; the other targets keep their words", () => {
  const word = (t) => stateText(t, tileState(t));
  assert.equal(word(mac), "켜짐");
  assert.equal(
    word({ ...mac, agent: { ...mac.agent, issue: "permission" } }),
    "권한 필요",
  );
  assert.equal(
    word({ ...mac, agent: { ...mac.agent, issue: "locked" } }),
    "잠김",
  );
  assert.equal(word({ ...mac, online: false, agent: undefined }), "오프라인");
  assert.equal(
    word({ ...mac, ready: false, online: false, agent: undefined }),
    "설정 필요",
  );
  assert.equal(word({ ...linux, online: false }), "꺼짐");
  assert.equal(word({ ...vnc, online: false }), "꺼짐", "VNC keeps 꺼짐");
});

test("a Mac that needs permission is not openable; a locked one follows the P1 switch", () => {
  assert.equal(tileEnabled(mac), true);
  assert.equal(
    tileEnabled({ ...mac, agent: { ...mac.agent, issue: "permission" } }),
    false,
  );
  assert.equal(
    tileEnabled({ ...mac, agent: { ...mac.agent, issue: "locked" } }),
    LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED,
  );
  assert.equal(
    LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED,
    false,
    "until P1 shows the lock screen can be controlled",
  );
  assert.equal(tileEnabled({ ...mac, online: false, agent: undefined }), false);
  // A target that is not an agent never gets agent states, whatever the list says.
  assert.equal(
    tileState({ ...linux, agent: { issue: "permission" } }),
    "online",
  );
});

test("the list renders the agent words and disables the tiles that cannot open", async () => {
  const v = await viewer({
    targets: [
      mac,
      { ...mac, id: "a", agent: { ...mac.agent, issue: "permission" } },
      { ...mac, id: "b", agent: { ...mac.agent, issue: "locked" } },
      { ...mac, id: "c", online: false, agent: undefined },
    ],
  });
  const words = v
    .tiles()
    .map((t) => t.querySelector(".tile-state").textContent);
  assert.deepEqual(words, ["켜짐", "권한 필요", "잠김", "오프라인"]);
  assert.deepEqual(
    v.tiles().map((t) => t.disabled),
    [false, true, true, true],
  );
});

// ---- starting a Mac session ----

test("clicking a Mac tile relays an offer to /api/agent-sessions with the window size, and never touches Guacamole", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  assert.equal(v.agents.length, 1);
  assert.equal(v.clients.length, 0);
  assert.equal(v.tunnels.length, 0);
  assert.equal(v.posts(), 0, "nothing was sent to /api/sessions");
  assert.deepEqual(plain(v.agentRequests()), [
    { targetId: "mac", width: 800, height: 600, sdp: "v=0\r\nfake-offer" },
  ]);
  assert.equal(v.agents[0].answer.sdp, "v=0\r\nanswer");
  assert.equal(v.el("session").hidden, false);
  assert.equal(v.el("session-state").textContent, "연결 중");
  assert.equal(
    v.el("display").children[0],
    v.agents[0].element,
    "the video surface is in the display",
  );
  assert.equal(v.el("display").classes.has("agent-display"), true);
});

test("the resolution picker is available for a Mac agent, and not for a VNC Mac", async () => {
  const agentView = await opened();
  assert.equal(agentView.el("resolution").disabled, false);
  const vncView = await viewer({ appMode: true, targets: [vnc] });
  vncView.tiles()[0].click();
  await vncView.settle();
  assert.equal(vncView.el("resolution").disabled, true);
  assert.equal(vncView.clients.length, 1, "VNC still goes through Guacamole");
  assert.equal(vncView.agents.length, 0);
});

test("the first picture connects: overlay gone, 연결됨, text input and the menu enabled, keyboard focus on the picture", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  assert.equal(v.el("overlay").hidden, true);
  assert.equal(v.el("session-state").textContent, "연결됨");
  assert.equal(v.el("text-input").disabled, false);
  assert.ok(v.agents[0].focused >= 1);
  assert.equal(v.agents[0].element.classes.has("agent-connected"), true);
  const last = plain(v.bridgeCalls.viewerState.at(-1));
  assert.deepEqual(last, {
    open: true,
    connected: true,
    protocol: "agent",
    resolution: "auto",
  });
});

test("a version 3 app is told 'agent'; an older app is told 'rdp' (the same menu); a browser asks nobody", async () => {
  for (const [bridgeVersion, expected] of [
    [3, "agent"],
    [2, "rdp"],
    [1, "rdp"],
  ]) {
    const v = await opened({ bridgeVersion });
    v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
    assert.ok(v.bridgeCalls.viewerState.length > 0);
    assert.ok(
      v.bridgeCalls.viewerState.every(
        (s) => s.protocol === expected || s.protocol === null,
      ),
      `bridge ${bridgeVersion}`,
    );
    assert.equal(plain(v.bridgeCalls.viewerState.at(-1)).protocol, expected);
  }
  const browser = await opened({ appMode: false });
  browser.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  assert.deepEqual(browser.bridgeCalls.viewerState, []);
});

// ---- ending ----

test("when the session ends the ended card appears, no new attempt starts, and 다시 연결 makes one new session", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.agents[0].deps.onEnded("disconnected");
  await v.settle();
  await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
  assert.equal(
    v.agentRequests().length,
    1,
    "no attempt was started by the ending",
  );
  assert.equal(v.el("overlay").hidden, false);
  assert.equal(v.el("overlay-title").textContent, ENDED);
  assert.equal(
    v.el("overlay-detail").textContent,
    "작업은 그대로 남아 있습니다",
  );
  assert.equal(v.el("overlay-actions").hidden, false);
  assert.equal(v.el("text-input").disabled, true);
  v.el("overlay-reconnect").click();
  await v.settle();
  assert.equal(v.agentRequests().length, 2);
  assert.equal(v.agents.length, 2);
  assert.equal(
    v.agents[0].closed >= 1,
    true,
    "the old session is closed (and released its keys)",
  );
  // A live session is not reconnected by the menu.
  v.agents[1].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.action("reconnect");
  await v.settle();
  assert.equal(v.agentRequests().length, 2);
});

test("an ending reported by a replaced session cannot end the new one", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.el("overlay-back").click();
  v.tiles()[0].click();
  await v.settle();
  v.agents[1].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.agents[0].deps.onEnded("disconnected");
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  assert.equal(v.el("session-state").textContent, "연결됨");
  assert.equal(v.el("overlay").hidden, true);
});

const refusals = {
  permission: [
    409,
    { code: "permission", error: "x" },
    "Mac에서 화면 기록·손쉬운 사용 권한이 필요합니다",
    "Mac (VNC)로 들어가 허용할 수 있습니다",
  ],
  locked: [
    409,
    { code: "locked", error: "x" },
    "Mac이 잠겨 있습니다",
    "Mac (VNC)로 잠금을 해제하세요",
  ],
  setup: [
    409,
    { code: "setup", error: "x" },
    "연결할 수 없습니다",
    "서버에서 에이전트 토큰을 설정해야 합니다",
  ],
  unavailable: [
    424,
    { code: "unavailable", error: "x" },
    "연결할 수 없습니다",
    "Mac이 꺼져 있거나 에이전트가 멈췄을 수 있습니다",
  ],
  "a stopped gateway (502)": [
    502,
    undefined,
    "연결할 수 없습니다",
    "Mac이 꺼져 있거나 에이전트가 멈췄을 수 있습니다",
  ],
  "a server error": [
    500,
    { error: "boom" },
    "연결할 수 없습니다",
    "Mac이 꺼져 있거나 에이전트가 멈췄을 수 있습니다",
  ],
};
for (const [name, [status, body, title, detail]] of Object.entries(refusals))
  test(`a refusal (${name}) shows its own card, once, and starts no retry`, async () => {
    const v = await opened({
      fetchHandler: (path, init, ok) =>
        path === "/api/agent-sessions"
          ? response(status, body)
          : ok(path, init),
    });
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
    assert.equal(v.agentRequests().length, 1);
    assert.equal(v.el("overlay-title").textContent, title);
    assert.equal(v.el("overlay-detail").textContent, detail);
    assert.equal(v.el("overlay-actions").hidden, false);
    assert.equal(v.el("overlay-detail").hidden, false);
    assert.ok(v.agents[0].closed >= 0);
  });

test("a refused login while connecting leaves for the forbidden screen, as it does for Guacamole", async () => {
  const v = await opened({
    fetchHandler: (path, init, ok) =>
      path === "/api/agent-sessions"
        ? response(403, { code: "login", error: "x" })
        : ok(path, init),
  });
  assert.equal(v.el("session").hidden, true);
  assert.equal(
    v.el("state-title").textContent,
    "이 기기의 Tailscale 계정으로는 쓸 수 없습니다",
  );
  assert.equal(v.agentRequests().length, 1);
});

test("the ended card goes back to its usual words after an agent refusal", async () => {
  let refuse = true;
  const v = await opened({
    fetchHandler: (path, init, ok) =>
      path === "/api/agent-sessions" && refuse
        ? response(409, { code: "locked", error: "x" })
        : ok(path, init),
  });
  assert.equal(v.el("overlay-title").textContent, "Mac이 잠겨 있습니다");
  refuse = false;
  v.el("overlay-reconnect").click();
  await v.settle();
  v.agents[1].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.agents[1].deps.onEnded("disconnected");
  assert.equal(
    v.el("overlay-title").textContent,
    ENDED,
    "no leftover text from the earlier refusal",
  );
  assert.equal(
    v.el("overlay-detail").textContent,
    "작업은 그대로 남아 있습니다",
  );
});

// ---- input hand-over, size, text ----

test("fullscreen changes, dialogs, blur and the end of the session all release the Mac's keys", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  const before = v.agents[0].releases;
  v.action("text-input"); // opens the text dialog
  assert.ok(v.agents[0].releases > before, "text dialog");
  const afterDialog = v.agents[0].releases;
  v.el("fullscreen").click();
  await v.settle();
  assert.ok(v.agents[0].releases > afterDialog, "fullscreen switch");
  const afterFullscreen = v.agents[0].releases;
  v.el("disconnect").click();
  assert.ok(
    v.agents[0].releases > afterFullscreen || v.agents[0].closed >= 1,
    "disconnect",
  );
  assert.ok(v.agents[0].closed >= 1, "the session is closed");
});

test("leaving the viewer closes the Mac session", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.el("back").click();
  await v.settle();
  assert.ok(v.agents[0].closed >= 1);
  assert.equal(v.el("display").classes.has("agent-display"), false);
});

test("window and picker size changes are sent to the Mac as size messages after the same delay as for RDP", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.resizeViewport(1280, 720);
  v.advance(299);
  assert.deepEqual(v.agents[0].sizes, []);
  v.advance(1);
  assert.deepEqual(v.agents[0].sizes, [[1280, 720]]);
  v.setPixelRatio(2);
  v.resizeViewport(1000, 700);
  v.advance(300);
  assert.deepEqual(v.agents[0].sizes.at(-1), [2000, 1400]);
  const select = v.el("resolution");
  select.value = "1920x1080";
  select.dispatchEvent({ type: "change" });
  assert.deepEqual(v.agents[0].sizes.at(-1), [1920, 1080]);
  assert.equal(v.clients.length, 0);
});

test("the app menu's resolution entries work for a Mac agent", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.action("resolution:2560x1440");
  assert.equal(v.el("resolution").value, "2560x1440");
  assert.deepEqual(v.agents[0].sizes.at(-1), [2560, 1440]);
});

test("the text dialog types through the agent and never uses the clipboard path", async () => {
  const v = await opened();
  v.agents[0].deps.onFirstFrame();
  v.action("text-input");
  v.el("remote-text").value = "안녕하세요 😀";
  v.el("text-form").onsubmit({ preventDefault() {} });
  assert.deepEqual(v.agents[0].texts, ["안녕하세요 😀"]);
  assert.equal(v.el("remote-text").value, "");
  assert.equal(v.el("text-dialog").open, false);
  v.el("text-form").onsubmit({ preventDefault() {} });
  assert.equal(v.agents[0].texts.length, 1, "an empty text sends nothing");
});
```

Create `test/agent-style.test.js` (the stylesheet facts the coordinate maths assumes, which a fake page cannot show):

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";

// src/agent-input.ts places a pointer on the picture as `object-fit: contain` draws it.
// If the stylesheet stopped saying so, every click would land in the wrong place; the page
// cannot show that in a test without a browser, so the rule is read from the source.
const css = (
  await readFile(new URL("../src/style.css", import.meta.url), "utf8")
).replace(/\s+/g, " ");
const rule = (selector) => {
  const start = css.indexOf(`${selector} {`);
  assert.notEqual(start, -1, `rule ${selector}`);
  return css.slice(start, css.indexOf("}", start));
};

test("the Mac picture is letterboxed with object-fit: contain and fills the viewer", () => {
  assert.match(rule(".agent-video"), /object-fit: contain/);
  assert.match(rule(".agent-video"), /width: 100%/);
  assert.match(rule(".agent-video"), /height: 100%/);
  assert.match(rule(".agent-surface"), /width: 100%/);
  assert.match(rule(".display.agent-display"), /height: 100%/);
});

test("the viewer's own pointer is the one shown over a Mac picture, as an ordinary arrow", () => {
  assert.match(rule(".agent-video"), /cursor: default/);
  assert.match(rule(".agent-surface"), /cursor: default/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(rule(".agent-video"), /cursor: none/);
});

test("the new tile states are drawn like the other unavailable states", () => {
  assert.match(
    css,
    /\.is-setup \.dot, \.is-permission \.dot, \.is-locked \.dot \{/,
  );
});
```

```diff
+// Bridge version 3 knows the protocol "agent" in viewerState. An older app rejects it, and
+// the menu behaves as for RDP (the resolution can be changed), so it is told "rdp".
+const bridgeKnowsAgent = (window.desktop?.bridgeVersion ?? 0) >= 3;
+export function forBridge(state: ViewerState): ViewerState {
+  return state.protocol === "agent" && !bridgeKnowsAgent
+    ? { ...state, protocol: "rdp" }
```

Create `src/agent-messages.ts`:

```ts
// What the ended card says when a Mac (agent) session fails for a reason of its own
// (spec section 6). Reasons come from the gateway's error code (`permission`, `locked`,
// `setup`, `unavailable`) or from the session itself (`unsupported`, `timeout`,
// `disconnected`). Anything else keeps the page's usual "연결이 끊겼습니다" card, so this
// returns nothing for it.
export interface EndText {
  title: string;
  detail: string;
}

const texts: Record<string, EndText> = {
  permission: {
    title: "Mac에서 화면 기록·손쉬운 사용 권한이 필요합니다",
    detail: "Mac (VNC)로 들어가 허용할 수 있습니다",
  },
  locked: {
    title: "Mac이 잠겨 있습니다",
    detail: "Mac (VNC)로 잠금을 해제하세요",
  },
  unavailable: {
    title: "연결할 수 없습니다",
    detail: "Mac이 꺼져 있거나 에이전트가 멈췄을 수 있습니다",
  },
  setup: {
    title: "연결할 수 없습니다",
    detail: "서버에서 에이전트 토큰을 설정해야 합니다",
  },
  unsupported: {
    title: "연결할 수 없습니다",
    detail: "이 브라우저는 H.264 영상을 받을 수 없습니다",
  },
  timeout: {
    title: "연결할 수 없습니다",
    detail: "Mac에서 영상이 오지 않습니다",
  },
};

export function endText(reason: string | undefined): EndText | undefined {
  return reason !== undefined && Object.hasOwn(texts, reason)
    ? texts[reason]
    : undefined;
}

// Why a failed start ended: the gateway's code for the refusals it knows, "unavailable"
// for every other failure to reach the agent.
export function startFailureReason(error: unknown): string {
  const code = (error as { code?: unknown } | undefined)?.code;
  return code === "permission" || code === "locked" || code === "setup"
    ? code
    : "unavailable";
}
```

```diff
+// A Mac (agent) session: WebRTC video and input straight to the Mac, set up through the
+// gateway (src/agent.ts). The flow, the overlay, the menu and the fullscreen handling are
+// the ones the Guacamole path uses.
+async function connectAgent(
+  input: ConnectInput,
+  size: { width: number; height: number },
+  current: number,
+) {
+  const session = createAgentSession({
+    signal: (sdp) =>
+      api.agentSession({
+        targetId: input.targetId,
+        width: size.width,
+        height: size.height,

+      el("overlay").hidden = true;

+    },

+  el("display").append(session.element);
+  resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => resolution.viewportChanged());
+  resizeObserver.observe(el("viewport"));

+    await session.start();

+    const kind = error instanceof ApiError ? error.kind : undefined;
+    if (connectFailure(kind) === "forbidden") {
+      back(false);
+      showFailure(kind);
+    } else flow.ended(startFailureReason(error));
+  }
```

```diff
+    open: true,
+    connected,
+    protocol,
+    resolution: "auto",
+  });
+  await f.invoke("viewer-state", state("agent"));
+  assert.equal(item("resolution").enabled, true);
+  assert.equal(item("text-input").enabled, true);
+  assert.equal(item("reconnect").enabled, false);
+  await f.invoke("viewer-state", state("agent", false));
+  assert.equal(item("resolution").enabled, false, "not while connecting");
+  assert.equal(item("reconnect").enabled, true);
+  await f.invoke("viewer-state", state("vnc"));
+  assert.equal(item("resolution").enabled, false);
+  await f.invoke("viewer-state", state("rdp"));
+  assert.equal(item("resolution").enabled, true);
+});
+
+test("protocols other than the four it knows are still refused", async (t) => {
+  const f = await desktop({}, t);
+  for (const protocol of ["Agent", "ssh", "", 0, undefined])
+    await assert.rejects(
+      f.invoke("viewer-state", {
+        open: true,
+        connected: true,
+        protocol,
+        resolution: "auto",
+      }),
+      /Invalid viewer state/,
+      String(protocol),
+    );
+});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `node --test test/preload.test.js test/desktop.test.js 2>&1 | grep -E "ℹ (pass|fail)"`
Expected: failures: the bridge still announces 2, the smoke test still demands 2, `"agent"` is "Invalid viewer state".

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```diff
--- a/desktop/preload.cjs
+++ b/desktop/preload.cjs
@@ -5,10 +5,12 @@
 // Version 2 adds "auto" as a viewerState resolution and the resolution:auto menu action;
 // a version 1 app rejects any resolution but the three fixed sizes, so the page offers
 // "auto" only to version 2 or later.
+// Version 3 adds the protocol "agent" (the Mac agent) to viewerState; its menu behaves like
+// RDP's (the resolution can be changed). A page talking to an older app sends "rdp" instead.
 // The main process answers these calls only while the window shows the configured
 // gateway origin, so exposing them here grants nothing to any other page.
 const bridge = {
-  bridgeVersion: 2,
+  bridgeVersion: 3,
   fullscreen: (enabled) => ipcRenderer.invoke("remote:fullscreen", enabled),
   fullscreenState: () => ipcRenderer.invoke("remote:fullscreen-state"),
   viewerState: (state) => ipcRenderer.invoke("remote:viewer-state", state),
```

```diff
--- a/desktop/main.cjs
+++ b/desktop/main.cjs
@@ -77,7 +77,9 @@
   menu.getMenuItemById("reconnect").enabled = state.open && !state.connected;
   menu.getMenuItemById("text-input").enabled = state.open && state.connected;
   menu.getMenuItemById("resolution").enabled =
-    state.open && state.connected && state.protocol === "rdp";
+    state.open &&
+    state.connected &&
+    (state.protocol === "rdp" || state.protocol === "agent");
   for (const size of resolutions)
     menu.getMenuItemById(`resolution:${size}`).checked =
       size === state.resolution;
@@ -167,7 +169,7 @@
         !state ||
         typeof state.open !== "boolean" ||
         typeof state.connected !== "boolean" ||
-        ![null, "rdp", "vnc"].includes(state.protocol) ||
+        ![null, "rdp", "vnc", "agent"].includes(state.protocol) ||
         !resolutions.includes(state.resolution)
       )
         throw new Error("Invalid viewer state.");
@@ -304,7 +306,7 @@
       if (
         state.page !== setupPage ||
         state.title !== "Gome Remote" ||
-        state.bridgeVersion !== 2 ||
+        state.bridgeVersion !== 3 ||
         state.setup !== "function"
       )
         throw new Error("The address page or the bridge is not as expected.");
```
