### Task 5: Measurement mode `#measure` and the measuring driver

Spec: D8 (measurement mode), section 8 (the same polling loop for `agent` and `vnc`; click count must equal measure count), S2, S3.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/measure.ts`, `test/measure.test.js`, `scripts/measure-drive.mjs`, `scripts/measure-target.html`, `test/measure-drive.test.js`
- Modify: `src/main.ts`, `test/helpers/viewer-harness.js`, `test/viewer-agent.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 3): `AgentSession.video`, `AgentSession.clickAt(fx, fy)`, `AgentSession.watchFrames(ms)` returning `{frames, maxGapMs, gapsOver500ms, freezeCount}`.
- Produces:
  - `src/measure.ts`: `wantsMeasure(hash)`, `summarise(samples)`, `runMeasure(source, clock, options)`, `videoSource(video, click)`, `guacamoleSource(client, root)`, `initMeasure({ hash, source, soak, clock, publish })` (returns false and touches nothing unless the hash carries a `measure` token). `MeasureSource { readPixel(fx, fy), sendClick(fx, fy) }`.
  - On the page, with `#measure` after the address: a small panel and `globalThis.__gomeMeasure = { run(opts), soak(seconds), results }`.
  - `scripts/measure-drive.mjs` modes `measure` and `soak`; exports `LIMITS`, `readOptions(env)`, `verdictClick(summary, vncMedianMs)`, `verdictSoak(report)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`test/measure.test.js` pins: only a `measure` token turns the mode on and otherwise the module touches no document, clock or global; the changed-pixel rule (more than 60 on one channel, the VNC baseline's rule); the quantile rule of the summary; the loop's timing against a virtual clock (the first poll that sees the change), timeouts, a screen with no picture sending no click; that two different sources with the same behaviour give identical numbers (one loop for both methods); the readers for a Guacamole canvas and a video.

Create `test/measure.test.js`:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { loadTs } from "./helpers/load-ts.js";

const plain = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));

// A document that fails the test on any use, to prove that nothing is touched.
const forbidden = new Proxy(
  {},
  {
    get(_target, name) {
      throw new Error(`document.${String(name)} used`);
    },
  },
);
const measure = await loadTs("measure.ts", { document: forbidden });
const {
  wantsMeasure,
  summarise,
  runMeasure,
  changed,
  initMeasure,
  CHANGE_THRESHOLD,
} = measure;

test("only a #measure token in the address turns the mode on", () => {
  for (const hash of [
    "#measure",
    "#a=1&measure",
    "#measure&a=1",
    "#x&measure&y",
  ])
    assert.equal(wantsMeasure(hash), true, hash);
  for (const hash of [
    "",
    "#",
    "#measured",
    "#nomeasure",
    "#a=measure",
    "?measure",
    "#measure=1",
    "#Measure",
    "measure",
  ])
    assert.equal(wantsMeasure(hash), false, hash);
});

test("without #measure the panel does not exist: no element, no listener, no global, no timer", () => {
  let published = 0;
  let asked = 0;
  for (const hash of ["", "#", "#other", "#measured"])
    assert.equal(
      initMeasure({
        hash,
        source: () => {
          asked++;
        },
        publish: () => published++,
        clock: {
          now: () => assert.fail("the clock was read"),
          poll: () => assert.fail("a poll was scheduled"),
          sleep: () => assert.fail("a timer was started"),
        },
      }),
      false,
      hash,
    );
  assert.equal(published, 0);
  assert.equal(asked, 0);
  // The document proxy above would have thrown on any access.
});

test("the changed-pixel rule is the VNC baseline's: more than 60 on one channel", () => {
  assert.equal(CHANGE_THRESHOLD, 60);
  assert.equal(changed([0, 0, 0], [60, 0, 0]), false);
  assert.equal(changed([0, 0, 0], [61, 0, 0]), true);
  assert.equal(changed([200, 0, 0], [0, 0, 62]), true);
  assert.equal(
    changed([10, 10, 10], [40, 40, 40]),
    false,
    "small changes on all channels do not count",
  );
});

test("the summary uses the same quantile rule as the earlier VNC measurement and counts samples over a second", () => {
  const s = plain(summarise([100, 80, null, 120, 1500, 90, 60, 70, 110, 95]));
  assert.deepEqual(s, {
    n: 10,
    ok: 9,
    timeouts: 1,
    medianMs: 95,
    p95Ms: 1500,
    minMs: 60,
    maxMs: 1500,
    over1000ms: 1,
  });
  const empty = plain(summarise([null, null]));
  assert.equal(empty.ok, 0);
  assert.equal(empty.medianMs, null);
  assert.equal(plain(summarise([])).n, 0);
});

// A virtual clock and a screen whose pixel changes `delay` ms after each click.
function lab({ delay, readable = true, pollCost = 1 }) {
  let t = 0;
  const clicks = [];
  const reads = [];
  const sleeps = [];
  let flipAt = Infinity;
  let colour = 0;
  const source = {
    readPixel(fx, fy) {
      reads.push([fx, fy]);
      if (!readable) return undefined;
      if (t >= flipAt) {
        colour = 255 - colour;
        flipAt = Infinity;
      }
      return [colour, colour, colour];
    },
    sendClick(fx, fy) {
      clicks.push([fx, fy, t]);
      flipAt = delay === null ? Infinity : t + delay;
    },
  };
  const clock = {
    now: () => t,
    poll: (step) => {
      t += pollCost;
      step();
    },
    sleep: async (ms) => {
      sleeps.push(ms);
      t += ms;
    },
  };
  return { source, clock, clicks, reads, sleeps };
}
const options = { n: 5, fx: 0.25, fy: 0.75, pauseMs: 700, timeoutMs: 3000 };

test("each sample is the time from the click to the first poll that sees the pixel change", async () => {
  const l = lab({ delay: 77 });
  const { summary, samples } = await runMeasure(l.source, l.clock, options);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(samples), [77, 77, 77, 77, 77]);
  assert.equal(summary.medianMs, 77);
  assert.equal(
    l.clicks.length,
    5,
    "one click per sample: the page's click count equals the sample count",
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    l.clicks.map(([fx, fy]) => [fx, fy]),
    Array(5).fill([0.25, 0.75]),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(l.sleeps, Array(5).fill(700));
});

test("a polling step coarser than the delay still reports the first poll that saw it", async () => {
  const l = lab({ delay: 10, pollCost: 4 });
  const { samples } = await runMeasure(l.source, l.clock, { ...options, n: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(samples),
    [12],
    "polls at 4, 8, 12: the change is seen at 12",
  );
});

test("a pixel that never changes is a timeout, and so is a screen with no picture", async () => {
  const never = lab({ delay: null });
  const a = await runMeasure(never.source, never.clock, { ...options, n: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(plain(a.samples), [null, null]);
  assert.equal(a.summary.timeouts, 2);
  const blank = lab({ delay: 10, readable: false });
  const b = await runMeasure(blank.source, blank.clock, { ...options, n: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(plain(b.samples), [null, null]);
  assert.equal(
    blank.clicks.length,
    0,
    "no click is sent when there is nothing to read",
  );
});

test("the same loop measures any source: two different readers with the same behaviour give the same numbers", async () => {
  const one = lab({ delay: 143 });
  const two = lab({ delay: 143 });
  const a = await runMeasure(one.source, one.clock, options);
  const b = await runMeasure(two.source, two.clock, options);
  assert.deepEqual(plain(a), plain(b));
});

// ---- the panel, with a minimal document ----

function fakeDocument() {
  const made = [];
  const node = (tag) => ({
    tag,
    children: [],
    style: {},
    value: "",
    textContent: "",
    append(...c) {
      this.children.push(...c);
    },
  });
  return {
    made,
    document: {
      createElement: (tag) => {
        const n = node(tag);
        made.push(n);
        return n;
      },
      body: node("body"),
    },
  };
}

test("with #measure the panel appears and runs the measurement on demand through the published functions", async () => {
  const d = fakeDocument();
  const m = await loadTs("measure.ts", {
    document: d.document,
    MessageChannel,
    performance,
    setTimeout,
  });
  const l = lab({ delay: 88, pollCost: 1 });
  let api;
  const shown = m.initMeasure({
    hash: "#measure",
    source: () => l.source,
    clock: l.clock,
    publish: (a) => (api = a),
  });
  assert.equal(shown, true);
  assert.equal(d.document.body.children.length, 1);
  assert.equal(d.document.body.children[0].id, "measure-panel");
  const result = await api.run({ n: 3, pauseMs: 10 });
  assert.deepEqual(plain(result.samples), [88, 88, 88]);
  assert.equal(l.clicks.length, 3);
  assert.equal(plain(api.results.summary).medianMs, 88);
});

test("with no session on screen the panel says so instead of measuring", async () => {
  const d = fakeDocument();
  const m = await loadTs("measure.ts", { document: d.document });
  let api;
  m.initMeasure({
    hash: "#measure",
    source: () => undefined,
    clock: lab({ delay: 1 }).clock,
    publish: (a) => (api = a),
  });
  assert.equal(await api.run(), undefined);
});

test("the Guacamole reader takes the largest canvas and clicks through the client like a user", () => {
  const canvas = (w, h, r) => ({
    width: w,
    height: h,
    getContext: () => ({
      getImageData: (x, y) => ({ data: [r + x, y, 0, 255] }),
    }),
  });
  const root = {
    querySelectorAll: () => [
      canvas(10, 10, 1),
      canvas(1920, 1080, 100),
      canvas(640, 480, 5),
    ],
  };
  const sent = [];
  const client = {
    getDisplay: () => ({ getWidth: () => 1920, getHeight: () => 1080 }),
    sendMouseState: (state, force) => sent.push([plain(state), force]),
  };
  const source = measure.guacamoleSource(client, root);
  assert.deepEqual(
    plain(source.readPixel(0.5, 0.5)),
    [100 + 960, 540, 0],
    "the 1920x1080 canvas, at its middle",
  );
  assert.equal(
    source.readPixel(1, 1)[0],
    100 + 1919,
    "the last pixel is clamped inside the canvas",
  );
  source.sendClick(0.25, 0.5);
  assert.deepEqual(
    sent.map(([s]) => [s.x, s.y, s.left]),
    [
      [480, 540, true],
      [480, 540, false],
    ],
  );
  assert.ok(sent.every(([, force]) => force === true));
  const none = measure.guacamoleSource(client, { querySelectorAll: () => [] });
  assert.equal(none.readPixel(0.5, 0.5), undefined);
});

test("the video reader copies one source pixel and reads nothing before the video has a size", async () => {
  const draws = [];
  const context = {
    drawImage: (...args) => draws.push(args.slice(1)),
    getImageData: () => ({ data: [9, 8, 7, 255] }),
  };
  const document = {
    createElement: () => ({ width: 0, height: 0, getContext: () => context }),
  };
  const { videoSource } = await loadTs("measure.ts", { document });
  {
    const video = { videoWidth: 0, videoHeight: 0 };
    const clicks = [];
    const source = videoSource(video, (...a) => clicks.push(a));
    assert.equal(source.readPixel(0.5, 0.5), undefined);
    video.videoWidth = 2560;
    video.videoHeight = 1440;
    assert.deepEqual(plain(source.readPixel(0.5, 0.25)), [9, 8, 7]);
    assert.deepEqual(draws[0], [1280, 360, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1]);
    source.readPixel(1, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(
      draws[1].slice(0, 4),
      [2559, 1439, 1, 1],
      "clamped inside the frame",
    );
    source.sendClick(0.1, 0.2);
    assert.deepEqual(clicks, [[0.1, 0.2]]);
  }
});
```

`test/measure-drive.test.js` pins the fixed thresholds, both verdicts (at the limits and one step beyond), the option checks and the usage exit:

```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import {
  LIMITS,
  readOptions,
  verdictClick,
  verdictSoak,
} from "../scripts/measure-drive.mjs";

const script = fileURLToPath(
  new URL("../scripts/measure-drive.mjs", import.meta.url),
);
const env = {
  MODE: "measure",
  GATEWAY_URL: "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8451/",
};
const good = {
  n: 30,
  ok: 30,
  timeouts: 0,
  medianMs: 80,
  p95Ms: 120,
  minMs: 60,
  maxMs: 140,
  over1000ms: 0,
};

test("the thresholds are the ones fixed in the spec", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(LIMITS, {
    medianMs: 100,
    p95Ms: 150,
    maxMs: 1000,
    vncRatio: 0.6,
    freezeMs: 500,
  });
});

test("the click verdict passes at the limits and names every condition that fails", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(verdictClick(good, 200), []);
  assert.deepEqual(
    verdictClick({ ...good, medianMs: 100, p95Ms: 150 }, undefined),
    [],
  );
  assert.equal(verdictClick({ ...good, medianMs: 101 }, undefined).length, 1);
  assert.equal(verdictClick({ ...good, p95Ms: 151 }, undefined).length, 1);
  assert.equal(verdictClick({ ...good, over1000ms: 1 }, undefined).length, 1);
  assert.equal(verdictClick({ ...good, timeouts: 2 }, undefined).length, 1);
  // 0.6 x the same client's VNC median: 80 passes against 134, fails against 133.
  assert.deepEqual(verdictClick(good, 134), []);
  assert.match(verdictClick(good, 133)[0], /VNC 133/);
  assert.equal(
    verdictClick(
      { ...good, medianMs: 200, p95Ms: 300, over1000ms: 3, timeouts: 1 },
      100,
    ).length,
    5,
  );
});

test("the soak verdict allows no frame gap over 500 ms and no browser-counted freeze", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    verdictSoak({
      frames: 3000,
      maxGapMs: 120,
      gapsOver500ms: 0,
      freezeCount: 0,
    }),
    [],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    verdictSoak({
      frames: 3000,
      maxGapMs: 120,
      gapsOver500ms: 0,
      freezeCount: null,
    }),
    [],
  );
  assert.equal(
    verdictSoak({
      frames: 3000,
      maxGapMs: 800,
      gapsOver500ms: 1,
      freezeCount: 0,
    }).length,
    1,
  );
  assert.equal(
    verdictSoak({
      frames: 3000,
      maxGapMs: 120,
      gapsOver500ms: 0,
      freezeCount: 2,
    }).length,
    1,
  );
});

test("options are checked, and the address always ends in #measure", () => {
  const o = readOptions(env);
  assert.equal(o.url, "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8451/#measure");
  assert.equal(o.n, 30);
  assert.equal(o.fx, 0.5);
  assert.equal(
    readOptions({
      ...env,
      GATEWAY_URL: "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8451/#x?y",
    }).url,
    "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8451/#measure",
  );
  assert.equal(
    readOptions({ ...env, N: "5", FLIP_X: "0.25", VNC_MEDIAN_MS: "180" })
      .vncMedianMs,
    180,
  );
  for (const bad of [
    { MODE: "run" },
    { MODE: undefined },
    { GATEWAY_URL: undefined },
    { GATEWAY_URL: "ftp://x.example/" },
    { GATEWAY_URL: "not a url" },
    { N: "0" },
    { N: "501" },
    { N: "x" },
    { FLIP_X: "1.5" },
    { FLIP_Y: "-0.1" },
    { VIEWPORT: "100x100" },
    { VIEWPORT: "big" },
    { VNC_MEDIAN_MS: "0" },
  ])
    assert.throws(
      () => readOptions({ ...env, ...bad }),
      /./,
      JSON.stringify(bad),
    );
});

test("run without arguments the script prints its usage and exits 2 without starting a browser", () => {
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [script], {
    encoding: "utf8",
    env: { ...process.env, GATEWAY_URL: "", PW_CORE: "/nonexistent" },
  });
  assert.equal(result.status, 2);
  assert.match(result.stderr, /mode is/);
});

test("the target page flips on every press, counts the flips, and has no transition", async () => {
  const html = await readFile(
    new URL("../scripts/measure-target.html", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  assert.match(html, /addEventListener\(["']mousedown/);
  assert.match(html, /\+\+n/);
  const withoutComments = html.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "");
  assert.doesNotMatch(withoutComments, /transition\s*:|animation\s*:/);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the measurement module**

Create `src/measure.ts`. The browser clock creates its `MessageChannel` on first use: a page that never measures holds no open port (an open port keeps a Node test process alive, which is how this was found).

```ts
// Measurement mode (spec D8, section 8). Only when the address carries `#measure` does a
// small panel appear; it sends N clicks to a chosen place of the remote screen and times,
// with one polling loop, how long the displayed pixel there takes to change. The same loop
// measures a Mac (agent) session (the video) and a Guacamole session (its canvas), so the
// two methods are compared with the same ruler. Without `#measure` nothing here runs.

export type Pixel = [number, number, number];

// Everything the loop needs from the page. Tests supply fakes for all of it.
export interface MeasureSource {
  // The colour currently displayed at a fraction of the remote screen.
  readPixel(fx: number, fy: number): Pixel | undefined;
  // A left click at that place.
  sendClick(fx: number, fy: number): void;
}
export interface MeasureClock {
  now(): number;
  // Runs `step` as soon as possible, much faster than a timer (about 1 ms or less).
  poll(step: () => void): void;
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>;
}
export interface MeasureOptions {
  n: number;
  fx: number;
  fy: number;
  pauseMs: number;
  timeoutMs: number;
}
export interface MeasureSummary {
  n: number;
  ok: number;
  timeouts: number;
  medianMs: number | null;
  p95Ms: number | null;
  minMs: number | null;
  maxMs: number | null;
  over1000ms: number;
}

// A pixel counts as changed when one channel moved by more than this (the VNC baseline's rule).
export const CHANGE_THRESHOLD = 60;
export const changed = (a: Pixel, b: Pixel) =>
  Math.max(
    Math.abs(a[0] - b[0]),
    Math.abs(a[1] - b[1]),
    Math.abs(a[2] - b[2]),
  ) > CHANGE_THRESHOLD;

export const wantsMeasure = (hash: string) => /(^#|&)measure(&|$)/.test(hash);

const quantile = (sorted: number[], q: number) =>
  sorted.length
    ? sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor(sorted.length * q))]
    : null;

export function summarise(samples: (number | null)[]): MeasureSummary {
  const ok = samples
    .filter((s): s is number => s !== null)
    .sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const round = (v: number | null) =>
    v === null ? null : Math.round(v * 10) / 10;
  return {
    n: samples.length,
    ok: ok.length,
    timeouts: samples.length - ok.length,
    medianMs: round(quantile(ok, 0.5)),
    p95Ms: round(quantile(ok, 0.95)),
    minMs: round(ok[0] ?? null),
    maxMs: round(ok.at(-1) ?? null),
    over1000ms: ok.filter((s) => s > 1000).length,
  };
}

// One sample: the time from the click until the pixel differs from what it was before.
// Null when it never changes within the timeout, or when there is no picture to read.
async function sample(
  source: MeasureSource,
  clock: MeasureClock,
  o: MeasureOptions,
): Promise<number | null> {
  const base = source.readPixel(o.fx, o.fy);
  if (!base) return null;
  const started = clock.now();
  source.sendClick(o.fx, o.fy);
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const step = () => {
      const elapsed = clock.now() - started;
      if (elapsed > o.timeoutMs) return resolve(null);
      const current = source.readPixel(o.fx, o.fy);
      if (current && changed(current, base)) return resolve(elapsed);
      clock.poll(step);
    };
    clock.poll(step);
  });
}

export async function runMeasure(
  source: MeasureSource,
  clock: MeasureClock,
  o: MeasureOptions,
): Promise<{ summary: MeasureSummary; samples: (number | null)[] }> {
  const samples: (number | null)[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < o.n; i++) {
    await clock.sleep(o.pauseMs);
    samples.push(await sample(source, clock, o));
  }
  return { summary: summarise(samples), samples };
}

// ---- the readers: the displayed pixel of a video and of Guacamole's canvases ----

export function videoSource(
  video: HTMLVideoElement,
  click: (fx: number, fy: number) => void,
): MeasureSource {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = 1;
  canvas.height = 1;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true })!;
  return {
    readPixel(fx, fy) {
      if (!video.videoWidth) return undefined;
      const x = Math.min(
        video.videoWidth - 1,
        Math.floor(fx * video.videoWidth),
      );
      const y = Math.min(
        video.videoHeight - 1,
        Math.floor(fy * video.videoHeight),
      );
      context.drawImage(video, x, y, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1);
      const d = context.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
      return [d[0], d[1], d[2]];
    },
    sendClick: click,
  };
}

// Guacamole draws into several canvases; the largest is the default layer, which holds the
// desktop. Clicks go through the client exactly as the user's would.
export function guacamoleSource(client: any, root: ParentNode): MeasureSource {
  const layer = () =>
    Array.from(
      root.querySelectorAll("canvas") as ArrayLike<HTMLCanvasElement>,
    ).reduce<HTMLCanvasElement | undefined>(
      (best, c) =>
        !best || c.width * c.height > best.width * best.height ? c : best,
      undefined,
    );
  const state = (x: number, y: number, left: boolean) => ({
    x,
    y,
    left,
    middle: false,
    right: false,
    up: false,
    down: false,
  });
  return {
    readPixel(fx, fy) {
      const canvas = layer();
      if (!canvas || !canvas.width) return undefined;
      const x = Math.min(canvas.width - 1, Math.floor(fx * canvas.width));
      const y = Math.min(canvas.height - 1, Math.floor(fy * canvas.height));
      const d = canvas.getContext("2d")!.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;
      return [d[0], d[1], d[2]];
    },
    sendClick(fx, fy) {
      const display = client.getDisplay();
      const x = Math.round(fx * display.getWidth());
      const y = Math.round(fy * display.getHeight());
      client.sendMouseState(state(x, y, true), true);
      client.sendMouseState(state(x, y, false), true);
    },
  };
}

// ---- the panel ----

export interface PanelDeps {
  hash: string;
  // The source for whatever session is on screen, or undefined when none is connected.
  source(): MeasureSource | undefined;
  // For the 60 second drag/scroll check (Mac agent sessions only).
  soak?(durationMs: number): Promise<unknown> | undefined;
  clock?: MeasureClock;
  // Where the panel's functions are exposed to a driver script (Playwright). Defaults to
  // `globalThis.__gomeMeasure`.
  publish?(api: unknown): void;
}

// The channel is made on first use, so a page that never measures holds no open port.
const browserClock = (): MeasureClock => {
  let channel: MessageChannel | undefined;
  let next: (() => void) | undefined;
  return {
    now: () => performance.now(),
    poll(step) {
      if (!channel) {
        channel = new MessageChannel();
        channel.port1.onmessage = () => {
          const run = next;
          next = undefined;
          run?.();
        };
      }
      next = step;
      channel.port2.postMessage(0);
    },
    sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
  };
};

// Returns whether the panel was shown. Without `#measure` it returns false having touched
// nothing: no element, no listener, no timer.
export function initMeasure(deps: PanelDeps): boolean {
  if (!wantsMeasure(deps.hash)) return false;
  const clock = deps.clock ?? browserClock();
  const panel = document.createElement("div");
  panel.id = "measure-panel";
  panel.style.cssText =
    "position:fixed;right:8px;bottom:8px;z-index:50;background:#000c;color:#fff;font:12px monospace;padding:8px;border-radius:6px;max-width:360px";
  const field = (label: string, value: string) => {
    const wrap = document.createElement("label");
    wrap.textContent = `${label} `;
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.value = value;
    input.size = 5;
    wrap.append(input);
    panel.append(wrap, " ");
    return input;
  };
  const n = field("N", "30");
  const x = field("x", "0.5");
  const y = field("y", "0.5");
  const pause = field("pause ms", "1200");
  const button = document.createElement("button");
  button.textContent = "측정";
  const soakButton = document.createElement("button");
  soakButton.textContent = "60초 멈춤 확인";
  const out = document.createElement("pre");
  out.style.cssText = "margin:6px 0 0;white-space:pre-wrap";
  panel.append(button, " ", soakButton, out);
  document.body.append(panel);

  const api = {
    results: undefined as unknown,
    async run(overrides: Partial<MeasureOptions> = {}) {
      const source = deps.source();
      if (!source) {
        out.textContent = "연결된 화면이 없습니다";
        return undefined;
      }
      const options: MeasureOptions = {
        n: Number(n.value) || 30,
        fx: Number(x.value),
        fy: Number(y.value),
        pauseMs: Number(pause.value) || 1200,
        timeoutMs: 3000,
        ...overrides,
      };
      button.disabled = true;
      try {
        const result = await runMeasure(source, clock, options);
        api.results = result;
        out.textContent = JSON.stringify(result.summary, null, 1);
        return result;
      } finally {
        button.disabled = false;
      }
    },
    async soak(seconds = 60) {
      const result = await deps.soak?.(seconds * 1000);
      out.textContent = JSON.stringify(result ?? "지원하지 않는 방식입니다");
      return result;
    },
  };
  button.onclick = () => void api.run();
  soakButton.onclick = () => void api.soak(60);
  (deps.publish ?? ((a) => ((globalThis as any).__gomeMeasure = a)))(api);
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the driver and the target page**

`scripts/measure-drive.mjs` needs `playwright-core`, which is not a dependency of the repository (`PW_CORE` points at an installation; on the gateway server one exists next to the Chrome used for earlier measurements). Its thresholds are the spec's and are pinned by the test above.

```js
// Drives the page's measurement mode (src/measure.ts) from a headless Chrome, for the
// verification runs V1 to V3 of the Mac agent spec (section 8). It needs playwright-core,
// which is deliberately not a dependency of this repository:
//
//   PW_CORE=/path/to/node_modules/playwright-core \
//   GATEWAY_URL=https://your-server.your-tailnet.ts.net:8451/ \
//   TILE_MATCH='새 방식' node scripts/measure-drive.mjs measure
//
// Modes: `measure` (click-to-picture, N clicks) and `soak` (60 s of dragging and scrolling,
// reporting the frame gaps). Run it once per tile (Mac agent, then Mac (VNC) with
// TILE_MATCH='VNC') and compare the medians. On the Mac, the page scripts/measure-target.html
// must be open full screen in front: the Mac's pointer is moved by the clicks.
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { realpathSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

// The thresholds are fixed before measuring and are not moved to fit a result (spec S2, S3).
export const LIMITS = {
  medianMs: 100,
  p95Ms: 150,
  maxMs: 1000,
  vncRatio: 0.6,
  freezeMs: 500,
};

export function readOptions(env) {
  const mode = env.MODE;
  if (!["measure", "soak"].includes(mode))
    throw new Error("The mode is `measure` or `soak`.");
  if (!env.GATEWAY_URL) throw new Error("GATEWAY_URL is required.");
  const url = new URL(env.GATEWAY_URL);
  if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol))
    throw new Error("GATEWAY_URL must be an http(s) address.");
  const number = (name, fallback, min, max) => {
    const value = env[name] === undefined ? fallback : Number(env[name]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < min || value > max)
      throw new Error(`${name} must be a number from ${min} to ${max}.`);
    return value;
  };
  const [vw, vh] = (env.VIEWPORT || "1920x1080").split("x").map(Number);
  if (!(vw >= 640 && vh >= 480))
    throw new Error("VIEWPORT is WxH, at least 640x480.");
  return {
    mode,
    url: `${url.origin}${url.pathname}#measure`,
    tile: new RegExp(env.TILE_MATCH || "mac", "i"),
    n: number("N", 30, 1, 500),
    fx: number("FLIP_X", 0.5, 0, 1),
    fy: number("FLIP_Y", 0.5, 0, 1),
    pauseMs: number("PAUSE_MS", 1200, 100, 10_000),
    soakSeconds: number("SOAK_SECONDS", 60, 5, 600),
    vncMedianMs:
      env.VNC_MEDIAN_MS === undefined
        ? undefined
        : number("VNC_MEDIAN_MS", 0, 1, 100_000),
    viewport: { width: vw, height: vh },
    out: env.OUT_JSON,
    shots: env.SHOT_PREFIX || "/tmp/gome-measure",
  };
}

// S2 for one client and one run of 30 clicks; `vncMedianMs` is the same client's VNC median
// from the same time of day. Returns the failed conditions (empty: passed).
export function verdictClick(summary, vncMedianMs) {
  const failed = [];
  if (summary.timeouts > 0)
    failed.push(`${summary.timeouts} clicks never showed`);
  if (summary.medianMs > LIMITS.medianMs)
    failed.push(`median ${summary.medianMs} ms > ${LIMITS.medianMs}`);
  if (summary.p95Ms > LIMITS.p95Ms)
    failed.push(`p95 ${summary.p95Ms} ms > ${LIMITS.p95Ms}`);
  if (summary.over1000ms > 0)
    failed.push(`${summary.over1000ms} samples over 1 s`);
  if (
    vncMedianMs !== undefined &&
    summary.medianMs > vncMedianMs * LIMITS.vncRatio
  )
    failed.push(
      `median ${summary.medianMs} ms > ${LIMITS.vncRatio} x VNC ${vncMedianMs} ms`,
    );
  return failed;
}

// S3: no freeze longer than 500 ms during the dragging and scrolling.
export function verdictSoak(report) {
  const failed = [];
  if (report.gapsOver500ms > 0)
    failed.push(
      `${report.gapsOver500ms} frame gaps over ${LIMITS.freezeMs} ms (longest ${Math.round(report.maxGapMs)} ms)`,
    );
  if (report.freezeCount)
    failed.push(`the browser counted ${report.freezeCount} freezes`);
  return failed;
}

async function main() {
  let options;
  try {
    options = readOptions({ ...process.env, MODE: process.argv[2] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      `${error.message}\nUsage: see the top of scripts/measure-drive.mjs`,
    );
    return 2;
  }
  const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
  const { chromium } = require(process.env.PW_CORE || "playwright-core");
  const browser = await chromium.launch({
    channel: "chrome",
    headless: true,
    args: ["--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required"],
  });
  let code = 0;
  try {
    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: options.viewport });
    page.on("pageerror", (e) => console.log("[pageerror]", e.message));
    await page.goto(options.url);
    const tile = page.locator("button.tile", { hasText: options.tile }).first();
    await tile.waitFor({ timeout: 20_000 });
    await page
      .waitForFunction(
        () => !document.querySelector("button.tile")?.disabled,
        null,
        { timeout: 20_000 },
      )
      .catch(() => {});
    await tile.click();
    await page.waitForFunction(
      () => document.getElementById("session-state")?.textContent === "연결됨",
      null,
      { timeout: 30_000 },
    );
    await page.waitForTimeout(3000); // the first frames and the cursor settle
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${options.shots}-before.png` });
    let result;
    let failed;
    if (options.mode === "measure") {
      result = await page.evaluate(
        (o) =>
          window.__gomeMeasure.run({
            n: o.n,
            fx: o.fx,
            fy: o.fy,
            pauseMs: o.pauseMs,
          }),
        options,
      );
      failed = verdictClick(result.summary, options.vncMedianMs);
    } else {
      const soak = page.evaluate(
        (s) => window.__gomeMeasure.soak(s),
        options.soakSeconds,
      );
      const end = Date.now() + options.soakSeconds * 1000;
      const { width, height } = options.viewport;
      for (let i = 0; Date.now() < end; i++) {
        await page.mouse.move(
          width * 0.2,
          height * (0.2 + 0.6 * ((i % 10) / 10)),
        );
        await page.mouse.down();
        await page.mouse.move(width * 0.8, height * 0.5, { steps: 20 });
        await page.mouse.up();
        await page.mouse.wheel(0, i % 2 ? 300 : -300);
      }
      result = await soak;
      failed = result
        ? verdictSoak(result)
        : ["this tile has no frame report (soak is for the Mac agent)"];
    }
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${options.shots}-after.png` });
    console.log(JSON.stringify(result?.summary ?? result));
    console.log(failed.length ? `FAILED: ${failed.join("; ")}` : "PASSED");
    if (options.mode === "measure")
      console.log(
        `Compare the flips counter on the Mac's target page in ${options.shots}-before.png and -after.png: it must have grown by exactly ${options.n}.`,
      );
    if (options.out)
      writeFileSync(
        options.out,
        JSON.stringify({
          options: { ...options, tile: String(options.tile) },
          result,
          failed,
        }),
      );
    if (failed.length) code = 1;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("FAILED", error.message);
    code = 1;
  } finally {
    await browser.close();
  }
  return code;
}

function isMainModule() {
  if (!process.argv[1]) return false;
  try {
    return (
      realpathSync(process.argv[1]) ===
      realpathSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
if (isMainModule()) process.exitCode = await main();
```

The page the Mac shows while measuring (full screen, in front; every mouse press flips the colour and counts):

```html
<!doctype html>
<html>
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>flip target</title>
    <style>
      html,
      body {
        margin: 0;
        height: 100%;
        overflow: hidden;
        user-select: none;
        -webkit-user-select: none;
      }
      /* No transition or animation: the colour must change in a single paint on mousedown. */
      #flip {
        position: fixed;
        inset: 0;
        background: #e00000;
      }
      /* Large, so the counter can still be read in a screenshot of the remote picture. */
      #cap {
        position: fixed;
        left: 24px;
        top: 16px;
        font: 64px monospace;
        color: #fff;
        mix-blend-mode: difference;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
    </style>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="flip"></div>
    <div id="cap">flips: <span id="n">0</span> (F: fullscreen)</div>
    <script>
      // Every mouse press flips the whole window between two colours. The measurement mode
      // (#measure in the gateway page) clicks and times how long the remote picture takes to
      // show the flip. The counter proves that every click was delivered exactly once.
      const A = "#e00000",
        B = "#00c0ff";
      let state = false,
        n = 0;
      const el = document.getElementById("flip");
      addEventListener("mousedown", () => {
        state = !state;
        el.style.background = state ? B : A;
        document.getElementById("n").textContent = ++n;
      });
      addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
        if (e.key === "f" || e.key === "F")
          document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
      });
    </script>
  </body>
</html>
```

### Task 6: Agent crate: settings, token, HTTP server, `/v1/status`, `/v1/sessions` front door

Spec: D2 (source address, Host, Origin, bearer, backoff, root-owned settings, no arguments or environment), D5 (status enumerations), D6 (startup refusals, port 18471, log cap), D10 (the platform-independent tests), V5 (negative tests, run partly here).

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/mac/Cargo.toml`, `agent/mac/rustfmt.toml`, `agent/mac/Cargo.lock` (generated), `agent/mac/src/lib.rs`, `agent/mac/src/main.rs`, `agent/mac/src/{tailnet,config,paths,auth,status,size,http,log_cap}.rs`, `agent/mac/src/mac/{mod,ffi,system}.rs`, `agent/mac/tests/no_overrides.rs`
- Modify: `.gitignore`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from Tasks 1-5, but it implements the contract Task 2's client speaks (`GET /v1/status`, `POST /v1/sessions`, codes 401/403/423).
- Produces (all `pub`, platform independent unless noted):
  - `tailnet::{is_tailnet_ip(IpAddr) -> bool, filter_offer_candidates(&str) -> String}`
  - `config::{AgentConfig {bind, port, gateway}, parse_config(&str), FileMeta {mode, uid, kind}, Kind {File, Dir, Other}, check_config_file(&FileMeta, owner_uid, user_uid), check_config_dir(&FileMeta, owner_uid), check_token_file(&FileMeta, user_uid), parse_token(&str) -> Result<String, ConfigError>, DEFAULT_PORT}`
  - `paths::{locate(&Path) -> Paths {config, token, config_owner}, home_dir() -> Option<PathBuf>, ConfigOwner}`; the `dev-install` feature selects the user-directory variant.
  - `auth::{Guard::new(SocketAddr, IpAddr, String), Guard::check(&RequestMeta) -> Result<(), Reject>, Reject::{Forbidden(&str), Unauthorized}, Backoff, constant_time_eq}`
  - `status::{Status, Screen, Accessibility, DisplayState, Gate, REFUSE_WHEN_LOCKED}`; `Status::gate(refuse_when_locked) -> Result<(), Gate>`, `Status::to_json()`
  - `size::{Size {w, h}, parse_requested(i64, i64) -> Option<Size>, fit_to_display(Size, Size) -> Size, fps_for(Size) -> u32, bitrate_cap(Size) -> u32}`
  - `http::{Backend (trait: status(), start_session(SessionRequest)), SessionRequest {size, sdp}, SessionFailure {Gate, BadOffer, Internal}, Router::new(Guard, Arc<dyn Backend>), Router::route(&Head, &mut dyn Read) -> Reply, serve(SocketAddr, Arc<Router>)}`
  - `log_cap::truncate_if_large(RawFd) -> bool`
  - macOS only: `mac::run()` (loads and checks settings and token, serves HTTP with a status-only backend), `mac::system::{screen_recording_granted, accessibility_granted, prompt_for_permissions, display_state, screen_locked, native_pixels, main_bounds}`, `mac::ffi` (CoreFoundation, CoreMedia, CoreVideo, VideoToolbox, CoreGraphics, ApplicationServices, IOKit declarations).

- [ ] **Step 1: Create the crate skeleton**

`.gitignore` gains the line `agent/mac/target/`.

Create `agent/mac/Cargo.toml` (the dependency set of the whole agent; versions are those the spike ran on; macOS-only crates are target-gated so the Linux inner loop does not need them):

```toml
[package]
name = "gome-remote-agent"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"
rust-version = "1.85"
publish = false
description = "Gome Remote Mac agent: ScreenCaptureKit, VideoToolbox H.264, WebRTC (str0m), CGEvent input"

[lib]
name = "gome_agent"
path = "src/lib.rs"

[[bin]]
name = "gome-remote-agent"
path = "src/main.rs"

[features]
# Development install (spec O4): config and token live under a user directory so the
# binary can be rebuilt and replaced without sudo. Never used for the operational install.
dev-install = []

[dependencies]
str0m = { version = "0.24", default-features = false, features = ["aws-lc-rs"] }
tiny_http = "0.12"
serde_json = "1"
anyhow = "1"
libc = "0.2"
subtle = "2"

[target.'cfg(target_os = "macos")'.dependencies]
block2 = "0.6"
dispatch2 = "0.3"
objc2 = "0.6"
objc2-foundation = "0.3"
objc2-core-media = "0.3"
objc2-screen-capture-kit = "0.3"

[profile.release]
opt-level = 3
debug = 1
```

Create `agent/mac/rustfmt.toml`:

```toml
max_width = 120
use_small_heuristics = "Max"
```

Create `agent/mac/src/lib.rs` (later tasks add their module lines to this file):

```rust
//! Gome Remote Mac agent. Everything outside `mac` is platform independent, so its
//! tests run with a plain `cargo test` on any host; `mac` needs the macOS frameworks.
pub mod auth;
pub mod config;
pub mod http;
pub mod log_cap;
pub mod paths;
pub mod size;
pub mod status;
pub mod tailnet;

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
pub mod mac;
```

Create `agent/mac/src/main.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
fn main() {
    gome_agent::mac::run();
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
fn main() {
    eprintln!("gome-remote-agent runs on macOS only");
    std::process::exit(1);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests (platform-independent modules)**

Each file is created holding only its test module for now; Step 4 adds the implementation above it. (The comment line `// ---- tests ----` between the two parts is just a separator.)

`agent/mac/src/tailnet.rs`: tailnet address ranges at both edges, IPv6 prefix and IPv4-mapped addresses, and the offer candidate filter (only literal tailnet UDP candidates survive; mDNS names, LAN, public, relay-in-the-wrong-range and TCP candidates go; line endings and other lines are untouched; malformed candidates are dropped).

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn ip(text: &str) -> IpAddr {
        text.parse().unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn tailnet_ranges_are_exact_at_both_edges() {
        assert!(is_tailnet_ip(ip("100.64.0.20")));
        assert!(is_tailnet_ip(ip("100.127.255.254")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("100.63.255.255")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("100.128.0.1")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("10.0.0.5")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("127.0.0.1")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn tailnet_ipv6_prefix_and_mapped_ipv4() {
        assert!(is_tailnet_ip(ip("fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("fd7a:115c:a1e1::1")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("fe80::1")));
        assert!(is_tailnet_ip(ip("::ffff:100.64.0.20")));
        assert!(!is_tailnet_ip(ip("::ffff:192.168.0.2")));
    }

    const OFFER: &str = "v=0\r\n\
a=candidate:1 1 udp 2122260223 100.64.0.10 51000 typ host generation 0\r\n\
a=candidate:2 1 udp 2122194687 192.168.0.5 51001 typ host generation 0\r\n\
a=candidate:3 1 udp 2122129151 0b3a1c9e-77aa-4c1d-9d3e-0a1b2c3d4e5f.local 51002 typ host\r\n\
a=candidate:4 1 tcp 1518280447 100.64.0.10 9 typ host tcptype active\r\n\
a=candidate:5 1 udp 1686052607 203.0.113.9 51003 typ srflx raddr 0.0.0.0 rport 0\r\n\
a=candidate:6 1 udp 2122063615 fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234 51004 typ host\r\n\
a=candidate:7 1 UDP 100 100.64.0.99 51005 typ relay\r\n\
a=end-of-candidates\r\n\
m=video 9 UDP/TLS/RTP/SAVPF 102\r\n";

    #[test]
    fn only_literal_tailnet_udp_candidates_survive() {
        let kept = filter_offer_candidates(OFFER);
        let candidates: Vec<&str> = kept.lines().filter(|l| l.starts_with("a=candidate:")).collect();
        assert_eq!(candidates.len(), 3, "{kept}");
        assert!(candidates[0].starts_with("a=candidate:1 "));
        assert!(candidates[1].starts_with("a=candidate:6 "));
        // 100.64.0.99 is inside the tailnet range, so the relay-typed candidate stays;
        // the type is not what is filtered, the address is.
        assert!(candidates[2].starts_with("a=candidate:7 "));
        assert!(!kept.contains(".local"));
        assert!(!kept.contains("192.168.0.5"));
        assert!(!kept.contains("203.0.113.9"));
        assert!(!kept.contains("tcptype"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn other_lines_and_line_endings_are_untouched() {
        let kept = filter_offer_candidates(OFFER);
        assert!(kept.starts_with("v=0\r\n"));
        assert!(kept.contains("a=end-of-candidates\r\n"));
        assert!(kept.ends_with("m=video 9 UDP/TLS/RTP/SAVPF 102\r\n"));
        let lf_only = "v=0\na=candidate:3 1 udp 1 host.local 5 typ host\nm=video 9\n";
        assert_eq!(filter_offer_candidates(lf_only), "v=0\nm=video 9\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn malformed_candidates_are_dropped_not_trusted() {
        for line in [
            "a=candidate:",
            "a=candidate:1 1 udp",
            "a=candidate:1 1 udp 1 not-an-address 5 typ host",
            "a=candidate:1 1 udp 1 100.64.0.10.evil.example 5 typ host",
        ] {
            let sdp = format!("v=0\r\n{line}\r\n");
            assert_eq!(filter_offer_candidates(&sdp), "v=0\r\n", "{line}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_offer_without_any_candidate_passes_through() {
        let sdp = "v=0\r\nm=video 9 UDP/TLS/RTP/SAVPF 102\r\n";
        assert_eq!(filter_offer_candidates(sdp), sdp);
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/config.rs`: the settings (`bind`, `gateway` required and tailnet, `port` 1024 to 65535 default 18471, unknown keys refused), the ownership rules for the settings file, its directory and the token file, the token format.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn a_complete_config_parses_and_the_port_defaults() {
        let c = parse_config(r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"100.64.0.10"}"#).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.bind, "100.64.0.20".parse::<IpAddr>().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(c.gateway, "100.64.0.10".parse::<IpAddr>().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(c.port, 18471);
        let c = parse_config(r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"100.64.0.10","port":19000}"#).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.port, 19000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn startup_is_refused_without_a_gateway_or_on_a_non_tailnet_address() {
        for text in [
            r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20"}"#,
            r#"{"gateway":"100.64.0.10"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"0.0.0.0","gateway":"100.64.0.10"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"127.0.0.1","gateway":"100.64.0.10"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"192.168.1.5","gateway":"100.64.0.10"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"192.168.1.5"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"gateway.example"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"100.64.0.10","port":80}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"100.64.0.10","port":"18471"}"#,
            r#"{"bind":"100.64.0.20","gateway":"100.64.0.10","extra":1}"#,
            r#"[]"#,
            "not json",
        ] {
            assert!(parse_config(text).is_err(), "{text}");
        }
    }

    fn meta(mode: u32, uid: u32) -> FileMeta {
        FileMeta { mode, uid, kind: Kind::File }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_config_file_must_be_root_owned_and_not_writable_by_others() {
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o644, 0), 0, 501).is_ok());
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o444, 0), 0, 501).is_ok());
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o644, 501), 0, 501).is_err(), "owned by the user");
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o664, 0), 0, 501).is_err(), "group writable");
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o646, 0), 0, 501).is_err(), "world writable");
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o600, 0), 0, 501).is_err(), "root-only: the agent cannot read it");
        assert!(check_config_file(&FileMeta { mode: 0o644, uid: 0, kind: Kind::Other }, 0, 501).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_development_config_may_belong_to_the_user() {
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o600, 501), 501, 501).is_ok());
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o644, 501), 501, 501).is_ok());
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o666, 501), 501, 501).is_err());
        assert!(check_config_file(&meta(0o644, 0), 501, 501).is_err(), "root-owned is not the dev owner");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_settings_directory_must_be_closed_to_everybody_but_its_owner() {
        let dir = |mode, uid| FileMeta { mode, uid, kind: Kind::Dir };
        assert!(check_config_dir(&dir(0o755, 0), 0).is_ok());
        assert!(check_config_dir(&dir(0o700, 501), 501).is_ok());
        assert!(check_config_dir(&dir(0o775, 0), 0).is_err());
        assert!(check_config_dir(&dir(0o757, 0), 0).is_err());
        assert!(check_config_dir(&dir(0o755, 501), 0).is_err());
        assert!(check_config_dir(&meta(0o755, 0), 0).is_err(), "a file is not a directory");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_token_file_must_be_mode_600_and_the_users_own() {
        assert!(check_token_file(&meta(0o600, 501), 501).is_ok());
        for mode in [0o644, 0o640, 0o400, 0o700, 0o660] {
            assert!(check_token_file(&meta(mode, 501), 501).is_err(), "{mode:o}");
        }
        assert!(check_token_file(&meta(0o600, 0), 501).is_err());
        assert!(check_token_file(&FileMeta { mode: 0o600, uid: 501, kind: Kind::Dir }, 501).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_token_is_43_base64url_characters() {
        let good = "A".repeat(43);
        assert_eq!(parse_token(&good).unwrap(), good);
        assert_eq!(parse_token(&format!("{good}\n")).unwrap(), good);
        assert_eq!(parse_token(&format!("{good}\r\n")).unwrap(), good);
        for bad in [
            "".to_string(),
            "A".repeat(42),
            "A".repeat(44),
            format!("{}+", "A".repeat(42)),
            format!("{}=", "A".repeat(42)),
            format!(" {}", "A".repeat(42)),
        ] {
            assert!(parse_token(&bad).is_err(), "{bad:?}");
        }
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/auth.rs`: the gateway with the token passes; wrong, missing or malformed tokens are 401; a tailnet address that is not the gateway is 403 even with the token (and is judged before the token); IPv4-mapped peers; `Host`; any `Origin`; constant-time comparison across length relations; the backoff.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const TOKEN: &str = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ";

    fn guard() -> Guard {
        Guard::new("100.64.0.20:18471".parse().unwrap(), "100.64.0.10".parse().unwrap(), TOKEN.into())
    }
    fn good<'a>(auth: &'a str) -> RequestMeta<'a> {
        RequestMeta {
            peer: "100.64.0.10".parse().unwrap(),
            host: Some("100.64.0.20:18471"),
            origin: None,
            authorization: Some(auth),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_gateway_with_the_token_passes() {
        let bearer = format!("Bearer {TOKEN}");
        assert_eq!(guard().check(&good(&bearer)), Ok(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_wrong_missing_or_malformed_token_is_unauthorized() {
        let g = guard();
        for auth in [
            "Bearer wrong",
            "Bearer ",
            "Bearer",
            "bearer abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ",
            "Basic abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ",
            "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ",
            "Bearer abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ ",
            "Bearer abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOP",
            "Bearer abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQR",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(g.check(&good(auth)), Err(Reject::Unauthorized), "{auth:?}");
        }
        let mut none = good("");
        none.authorization = None;
        assert_eq!(g.check(&none), Err(Reject::Unauthorized));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_tailnet_address_that_is_not_the_gateway_is_forbidden_even_with_the_token() {
        let bearer = format!("Bearer {TOKEN}");
        for peer in ["100.64.0.99", "100.127.255.254", "127.0.0.1", "192.168.1.9", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234"] {
            let mut request = good(&bearer);
            request.peer = peer.parse().unwrap();
            assert_eq!(guard().check(&request), Err(Reject::Forbidden("source")), "{peer}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_ipv4_mapped_gateway_address_is_the_gateway() {
        let bearer = format!("Bearer {TOKEN}");
        let mut request = good(&bearer);
        request.peer = "::ffff:100.64.0.10".parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(guard().check(&request), Ok(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn host_must_be_the_bound_address_and_port() {
        let bearer = format!("Bearer {TOKEN}");
        for host in [
            None,
            Some("100.64.0.20"),
            Some("100.64.0.20:18472"),
            Some("localhost:18471"),
            Some("attacker.example:18471"),
            Some("100.64.0.20:18471 "),
            Some("100.64.0.99:18471"),
        ] {
            let mut request = good(&bearer);
            request.host = host;
            assert_eq!(guard().check(&request), Err(Reject::Forbidden("host")), "{host:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn any_origin_header_is_refused() {
        let bearer = format!("Bearer {TOKEN}");
        for origin in ["http://100.64.0.20:18471", "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8451", "null", ""] {
            let mut request = good(&bearer);
            request.origin = Some(origin);
            assert_eq!(guard().check(&request), Err(Reject::Forbidden("origin")), "{origin:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn source_is_judged_before_the_token_so_strangers_learn_nothing() {
        let mut request = good("Bearer wrong");
        request.peer = "100.64.0.99".parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(guard().check(&request), Err(Reject::Forbidden("source")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_ipv6_bind_expects_a_bracketed_host() {
        let g =
            Guard::new("[fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234]:18471".parse().unwrap(), "100.64.0.10".parse().unwrap(), TOKEN.into());
        assert_eq!(g.expected_host(), "[fd7a:115c:a1e0::1234]:18471");
    }

    #[test]
    fn constant_time_eq_handles_every_length_relation() {
        assert!(constant_time_eq(b"abc", b"abc"));
        assert!(constant_time_eq(b"", b""));
        assert!(!constant_time_eq(b"abc", b"abd"));
        assert!(!constant_time_eq(b"abc", b"abcd"));
        assert!(!constant_time_eq(b"abcd", b"abc"));
        assert!(!constant_time_eq(b"", b"a"));
        // The filler byte must not make a short value equal a longer one that ends in it.
        assert!(!constant_time_eq(b"a", b"a\xff"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn backoff_doubles_up_to_five_seconds_and_resets_on_success() {
        let mut b = Backoff::default();
        assert_eq!(b.failed(), Duration::from_millis(100));
        assert_eq!(b.failed(), Duration::from_millis(200));
        assert_eq!(b.failed(), Duration::from_millis(400));
        for _ in 0..40 {
            b.failed();
        }
        assert_eq!(b.failed(), Duration::from_secs(5));
        b.succeeded();
        assert_eq!(b.failed(), Duration::from_millis(100));
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/status.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn healthy() -> Status {
        Status {
            screen: Screen::Granted,
            accessibility: Accessibility::Granted,
            display: DisplayState::Ok,
            locked: false,
            last_capture_at: Some(1_760_400_000),
            version: "0.1.0",
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn json_carries_only_enumerations_and_a_time() {
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&healthy().to_json()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["screen"], "granted");
        assert_eq!(v["accessibility"], "granted");
        assert_eq!(v["display"], "ok");
        assert_eq!(v["locked"], false);
        assert_eq!(v["lastCaptureAt"], 1_760_400_000u64);
        assert_eq!(v["version"], "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(v.as_object().unwrap().len(), 6);
        let mut s = healthy();
        s.last_capture_at = None;
        s.display = DisplayState::Asleep;
        s.screen = Screen::Suspect;
        s.locked = true;
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&s.to_json()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["lastCaptureAt"], serde_json::Value::Null);
        assert_eq!(v["display"], "asleep");
        assert_eq!(v["screen"], "suspect");
        assert_eq!(v["locked"], true);
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_permissions_refuse_sessions_with_the_permission_code() {
        let mut s = healthy();
        s.screen = Screen::Denied;
        assert_eq!(s.gate(true), Err(Gate::Permission));
        let mut s = healthy();
        s.accessibility = Accessibility::Denied;
        assert_eq!(s.gate(false), Err(Gate::Permission));
        assert_eq!(Gate::Permission.code(), "permission");
        assert_eq!(Gate::Permission.http_status(), 403);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_suspect_screen_still_allows_a_session_so_it_can_recover() {
        let mut s = healthy();
        s.screen = Screen::Suspect;
        assert_eq!(s.gate(true), Ok(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn locked_refuses_only_while_the_p1_switch_says_so() {
        let mut s = healthy();
        s.locked = true;
        assert_eq!(s.gate(true), Err(Gate::Locked));
        assert_eq!(s.gate(false), Ok(()));
        assert_eq!(Gate::Locked.code(), "locked");
        assert_eq!(Gate::Locked.http_status(), 423);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_sleeping_or_missing_display_does_not_refuse_a_session() {
        // The session start wakes the display (spec D3); no display at all fails later, in capture.
        let mut s = healthy();
        s.display = DisplayState::Asleep;
        assert_eq!(s.gate(true), Ok(()));
        s.display = DisplayState::None;
        assert_eq!(s.gate(true), Ok(()));
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/size.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const D4K: Size = Size { w: 3840, h: 2160 };

    #[test]
    fn requests_outside_the_limits_are_dropped_and_odd_sides_round_down() {
        assert_eq!(parse_requested(640, 480), Some(Size { w: 640, h: 480 }));
        assert_eq!(parse_requested(3840, 2160), Some(D4K));
        assert_eq!(parse_requested(1921, 1081), Some(Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 }));
        for (w, h) in [(639, 480), (640, 479), (3841, 2160), (3840, 2161), (0, 0), (-1, 600), (i64::MAX, 600)] {
            assert_eq!(parse_requested(w, h), None, "{w}x{h}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_stream_keeps_the_display_aspect_inside_the_request() {
        // A 16:10 window on a 16:9 display: the width is the limit.
        assert_eq!(fit_to_display(Size { w: 1440, h: 900 }, D4K), Size { w: 1440, h: 810 });
        // A tall window: the height is the limit... of the width here.
        assert_eq!(fit_to_display(Size { w: 800, h: 1200 }, D4K), Size { w: 800, h: 450 });
        // Exactly the display's own aspect.
        assert_eq!(fit_to_display(Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 }, D4K), Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 });
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_stream_is_never_larger_than_the_display() {
        let small = Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 };
        assert_eq!(fit_to_display(D4K, small), small);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_non_even_result_is_rounded_down_to_even() {
        let out = fit_to_display(Size { w: 1001, h: 2000 }, Size { w: 3000, h: 2001 });
        assert_eq!(out.w % 2, 0);
        assert_eq!(out.h % 2, 0);
        assert!(out.w <= 1001 && out.h <= 2000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn frame_rate_steps_down_above_1440p_pixels() {
        assert_eq!(fps_for(Size { w: 2560, h: 1440 }), 60);
        assert_eq!(fps_for(Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 }), 60);
        assert_eq!(fps_for(Size { w: 2560, h: 1442 }), 45);
        assert_eq!(fps_for(D4K), 45);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_bitrate_ceiling_follows_the_pixel_count() {
        assert_eq!(bitrate_cap(Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 }), 12_000_000);
        assert_eq!(bitrate_cap(Size { w: 2560, h: 1440 }), 12_000_000);
        assert_eq!(bitrate_cap(D4K), 20_000_000);
        let mid = bitrate_cap(Size { w: 3200, h: 1800 });
        assert!(mid > 12_000_000 && mid < 20_000_000, "{mid}");
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/http.rs`: the router against a fake backend (status, session relay, refusals are 403 without delay, wrong tokens are slowed and a good request resets it, content type, length, 64 KiB cap (both sides), JSON and field checks, state refusal codes, unknown paths and methods are refused only after authentication).

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::status::{Accessibility, DisplayState, Screen};
    use std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool;

    const TOKEN: &str = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ";

    struct Fake {
        gate: Mutex<Option<Gate>>,
        started: AtomicBool,
        last_size: Mutex<Option<Size>>,
    }
    impl Backend for Fake {
        fn status(&self) -> Status {
            Status {
                screen: Screen::Granted,
                accessibility: Accessibility::Granted,
                display: DisplayState::Ok,
                locked: false,
                last_capture_at: None,
                version: "0.1.0",
            }
        }
        fn start_session(&self, request: SessionRequest) -> Result<String, SessionFailure> {
            if let Some(gate) = *self.gate.lock().unwrap() {
                return Err(SessionFailure::Gate(gate));
            }
            self.started.store(true, SeqCst);
            *self.last_size.lock().unwrap() = Some(request.size);
            Ok("v=0\r\nanswer".to_string())
        }
    }

    fn router() -> (Router, Arc<Fake>) {
        let fake =
            Arc::new(Fake { gate: Mutex::new(None), started: AtomicBool::new(false), last_size: Mutex::new(None) });
        let guard = Guard::new("100.64.0.20:18471".parse().unwrap(), "100.64.0.10".parse().unwrap(), TOKEN.into());
        (Router::new(guard, fake.clone()), fake)
    }

    fn head<'a>(method: &'a str, path: &'a str, auth: &'a str, length: Option<u64>) -> Head<'a> {
        Head {
            method,
            path,
            peer: "100.64.0.10".parse().unwrap(),
            host: Some("100.64.0.20:18471"),
            origin: None,
            authorization: Some(auth),
            content_type: Some("application/json"),
            content_length: length,
        }
    }
    const BEARER: &str = "Bearer abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQ";
    const GOOD_BODY: &str = r#"{"width":2560,"height":1440,"sdp":"v=0\r\nm=video"}"#;

    fn post(r: &Router, body: &str) -> Reply {
        r.route(&head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(body.len() as u64)), &mut body.as_bytes())
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_needs_the_token_and_returns_the_enumerations() {
        let (r, _) = router();
        let reply = r.route(&head("GET", "/v1/status", BEARER, None), &mut std::io::empty());
        assert_eq!(reply.status, 200);
        let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(&reply.body).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["screen"], "granted");
        let denied = r.route(&head("GET", "/v1/status", "Bearer nope", None), &mut std::io::empty());
        assert_eq!(denied.status, 401);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_session_request_reaches_the_backend_with_the_requested_size() {
        let (r, fake) = router();
        let reply = post(&r, GOOD_BODY);
        assert_eq!(reply.status, 200, "{}", reply.body);
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&reply.body).unwrap()["sdp"], "v=0\r\nanswer");
        assert!(fake.started.load(SeqCst));
        assert_eq!(*fake.last_size.lock().unwrap(), Some(Size { w: 2560, h: 1440 }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn refusals_say_forbidden_without_delay_and_never_reach_the_backend() {
        let (r, fake) = router();
        let mut stranger = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(GOOD_BODY.len() as u64));
        stranger.peer = "100.64.0.99".parse().unwrap();
        let reply = r.route(&stranger, &mut GOOD_BODY.as_bytes());
        assert_eq!((reply.status, reply.delay), (403, Duration::ZERO));
        let mut browser = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(GOOD_BODY.len() as u64));
        browser.origin = Some("https://gateway.example.ts.net:8451");
        assert_eq!(r.route(&browser, &mut GOOD_BODY.as_bytes()).status, 403);
        let mut rebinding = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(GOOD_BODY.len() as u64));
        rebinding.host = Some("attacker.example:18471");
        assert_eq!(r.route(&rebinding, &mut GOOD_BODY.as_bytes()).status, 403);
        assert!(!fake.started.load(SeqCst));
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrong_tokens_are_slowed_down_and_a_good_request_resets_it() {
        let (r, _) = router();
        let bad = |r: &Router| r.route(&head("GET", "/v1/status", "Bearer nope", None), &mut std::io::empty());
        assert_eq!(bad(&r).delay, Duration::from_millis(100));
        assert_eq!(bad(&r).delay, Duration::from_millis(200));
        let ok = r.route(&head("GET", "/v1/status", BEARER, None), &mut std::io::empty());
        assert_eq!((ok.status, ok.delay), (200, Duration::ZERO), "the gateway itself is never delayed");
        assert_eq!(bad(&r).delay, Duration::from_millis(100));
    }

    #[test]
    fn body_rules_content_type_length_cap_json_and_fields() {
        let (r, _) = router();
        let mut no_type = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(2));
        no_type.content_type = Some("text/plain");
        assert_eq!(r.route(&no_type, &mut "{}".as_bytes()).status, 415);
        no_type.content_type = None;
        assert_eq!(r.route(&no_type, &mut "{}".as_bytes()).status, 415);
        let chunked = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, None);
        assert_eq!(r.route(&chunked, &mut GOOD_BODY.as_bytes()).status, 411);
        let huge = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(MAX_BODY + 1));
        assert_eq!(r.route(&huge, &mut std::io::repeat(b'x')).status, 413);
        let at_cap = format!(r#"{{"width":2560,"height":1440,"sdp":"v=0{}"}}"#, "a".repeat(MAX_BODY as usize - 40));
        assert!(at_cap.len() as u64 <= MAX_BODY);
        assert_eq!(post(&r, &at_cap).status, 200, "a body exactly at the cap is accepted");
        assert_eq!(post(&r, "not json").status, 400);
        // A length that promises more than arrives.
        let short = head("POST", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, Some(500));
        assert_eq!(r.route(&short, &mut GOOD_BODY.as_bytes()).status, 400);
        for body in [
            r#"{"width":2560,"height":1440}"#,
            r#"{"width":2560,"sdp":"v=0"}"#,
            r#"{"width":2560,"height":1440,"sdp":"hello"}"#,
            r#"{"width":2560,"height":1440,"sdp":5}"#,
            r#"{"width":100,"height":1440,"sdp":"v=0"}"#,
            r#"{"width":"2560","height":1440,"sdp":"v=0"}"#,
            r#"[]"#,
        ] {
            assert_eq!(post(&r, body).status, 400, "{body}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn state_refusals_carry_their_codes() {
        let (r, fake) = router();
        *fake.gate.lock().unwrap() = Some(Gate::Permission);
        let reply = post(&r, GOOD_BODY);
        assert_eq!(reply.status, 403);
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&reply.body).unwrap()["code"], "permission");
        *fake.gate.lock().unwrap() = Some(Gate::Locked);
        let reply = post(&r, GOOD_BODY);
        assert_eq!(reply.status, 423);
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&reply.body).unwrap()["code"], "locked");
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_paths_and_methods_are_refused_after_authentication() {
        let (r, _) = router();
        assert_eq!(r.route(&head("GET", "/", BEARER, None), &mut std::io::empty()).status, 404);
        assert_eq!(r.route(&head("GET", "/v1/offer", BEARER, None), &mut std::io::empty()).status, 404);
        assert_eq!(r.route(&head("POST", "/v1/status", BEARER, Some(0)), &mut std::io::empty()).status, 405);
        assert_eq!(r.route(&head("GET", "/v1/sessions", BEARER, None), &mut std::io::empty()).status, 405);
        assert_eq!(
            r.route(&head("GET", "/", "Bearer nope", None), &mut std::io::empty()).status,
            401,
            "nothing is public"
        );
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/log_cap.rs` and `agent/mac/src/paths.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;

    #[test]
    fn the_cap_is_five_mebibytes_and_exact() {
        assert!(!should_truncate(0));
        assert!(!should_truncate(CAP_BYTES));
        assert!(should_truncate(CAP_BYTES + 1));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_large_append_only_file_is_emptied_and_keeps_accepting_writes() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("gome-log-cap-{}", std::process::id()));
        let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(&path).unwrap();
        file.write_all(&vec![b'x'; (CAP_BYTES + 10) as usize]).unwrap();
        assert!(truncate_if_large(file.as_raw_fd()));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::metadata(&path).unwrap().len(), 0);
        file.write_all(b"after\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read(&path).unwrap(), b"after\n", "appends at the new end, no hole");
        assert!(!truncate_if_large(file.as_raw_fd()), "a small file is left alone");
        std::fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_pipe_is_never_touched() {
        let mut fds = [0 as libc::c_int; 2];
        assert_eq!(unsafe { libc::pipe(fds.as_mut_ptr()) }, 0);
        assert!(!truncate_if_large(fds[1]));
        assert!(!truncate_if_large(-1));
        unsafe {
            libc::close(fds[0]);
            libc::close(fds[1]);
        }
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/tests/no_overrides.rs`: no source file may read arguments or the environment (spec D2, V5):

```rust
//! The agent must not read its settings location from the command line or the
//! environment (spec D2, V5): any use of `std::env` in the source is a failure.
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

fn sources(dir: &Path, out: &mut Vec<std::path::PathBuf>) {
    for entry in fs::read_dir(dir).unwrap() {
        let path = entry.unwrap().path();
        if path.is_dir() {
            sources(&path, out);
        } else if path.extension().map(|e| e == "rs").unwrap_or(false) {
            out.push(path);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn no_source_file_touches_arguments_or_the_environment() {
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    sources(&Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("src"), &mut files);
    assert!(files.len() > 10, "found only {} source files", files.len());
    let forbidden = ["env::args", "env::var", "env::vars", "env::args_os", "env::var_os", "env::current_dir", "getenv"];
    let mut problems = Vec::new();
    for file in files {
        let text = fs::read_to_string(&file).unwrap();
        for word in forbidden {
            if text.contains(word) {
                problems.push(format!("{} uses {word}", file.display()));
            }
        }
    }
    assert!(problems.is_empty(), "{problems:#?}");
}
```
