`agent/mac/src/mac/wake.rs`: `IOPMAssertionDeclareUserActivity` wakes a sleeping display, a `PreventUserIdleDisplaySleep` assertion keeps it awake until the lock is dropped (a session started while the display sleeps otherwise fails with "no displays"):

```rust
//! Waking the display and keeping it awake while somebody watches (spec D3).
use super::ffi::*;
use super::system::display_state;
use crate::status::DisplayState;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Wakes a sleeping display and keeps it awake until dropped (spec D3). Without it a
/// session started while the display sleeps fails with "no displays".
pub struct WakeLock {
    id: u32,
}

impl WakeLock {
    pub fn acquire() -> WakeLock {
        const USER_ACTIVE_LOCAL: u32 = 0;
        const LEVEL_ON: u32 = 255;
        unsafe {
            let reason = cf_string("Gome Remote session");
            let mut activity = 0u32;
            let _ = IOPMAssertionDeclareUserActivity(reason.0, USER_ACTIVE_LOCAL, &mut activity);
            let kind = cf_string("PreventUserIdleDisplaySleep");
            let mut id = 0u32;
            let status = IOPMAssertionCreateWithName(kind.0, LEVEL_ON, reason.0, &mut id);
            if status != 0 {
                eprintln!("[system] IOPMAssertionCreateWithName: {status}");
            }
            WakeLock { id }
        }
    }

    /// Waits until the display reports awake, at most `limit`.
    pub fn wait_for_display(limit: Duration) -> bool {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + limit;
        while Instant::now() < deadline {
            if display_state() == DisplayState::Ok {
                return true;
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
        }
        display_state() == DisplayState::Ok
    }
}

impl Drop for WakeLock {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.id != 0 {
            unsafe { IOPMAssertionRelease(self.id) };
        }
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/mac/capture.rs`, ported from `$SPIKE/src/capture.rs`. What changed: `setShowsCursor(false)` (the page draws the viewer's own pointer); `start` returns a `Capture` handle (the spike parked its thread for ever) with `update` (`updateConfiguration`) and `stop` (`stopCaptureWithCompletionHandler`); the frame callback is `Fn(CVPixelBufferRef, Instant)`; the click-trace timing is gone. This file uses `objc2` and could not be compiled on the Linux host: expect to fix type names on the Mac.

```rust
//! ScreenCaptureKit capture of the main display. Ported from the spike's `capture.rs`;
//! changes: the cursor is not captured (the page draws the viewer's own pointer, spec D4),
//! the stream can be reconfigured and stopped, and `start` returns a handle instead of
//! parking its thread for ever.
use super::ffi::*;
use block2::RcBlock;
use dispatch2::DispatchQueue;
use objc2::rc::Retained;
use objc2::runtime::ProtocolObject;
use objc2::{define_class, msg_send, AllocAnyThread, DefinedClass};
use objc2_core_media::{CMSampleBuffer, CMTime, CMTimeFlags};
use objc2_foundation::{NSArray, NSError, NSObject, NSObjectProtocol};
use objc2_screen_capture_kit::{
    SCContentFilter, SCShareableContent, SCStream, SCStreamConfiguration, SCStreamOutput, SCStreamOutputType, SCWindow,
};
use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::sync::mpsc::channel;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct CaptureConfig {
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub fps: i32,
    pub queue_depth: isize,
}

pub type FrameFn = Arc<dyn Fn(CVPixelBufferRef, Instant) + Send + Sync>;

struct Ivars {
    on_frame: FrameFn,
}

define_class!(
    #[unsafe(super(NSObject))]
    #[name = "GomeRemoteStreamOutput"]
    #[ivars = Ivars]
    struct StreamOutput;

    unsafe impl NSObjectProtocol for StreamOutput {}

    unsafe impl SCStreamOutput for StreamOutput {
        #[unsafe(method(stream:didOutputSampleBuffer:ofType:))]
        unsafe fn did_output(&self, _stream: &SCStream, sample: &CMSampleBuffer, kind: SCStreamOutputType) {
            if kind != SCStreamOutputType::Screen {
                return;
            }
            let sample = sample as *const CMSampleBuffer as *const c_void;
            let image = unsafe { CMSampleBufferGetImageBuffer(sample) };
            if image.is_null() {
                return; // a status-only sample (idle, paused)
            }
            (self.ivars().on_frame)(image, Instant::now());
        }
    }
);

struct SendBox<T>(T);
unsafe impl<T> Send for SendBox<T> {}

fn describe(error: *mut NSError) -> String {
    if error.is_null() {
        "unknown".into()
    } else {
        let error = unsafe { &*error };
        format!("{} (domain {}, code {})", error.localizedDescription(), error.domain(), error.code())
    }
}

/// A running capture. Dropping it without `stop` leaves the stream running.
pub struct Capture {
    stream: Retained<SCStream>,
    _output: Retained<StreamOutput>,
    _queue: dispatch2::DispatchRetained<DispatchQueue>,
    display_is_main: bool,
}
unsafe impl Send for Capture {}
unsafe impl Sync for Capture {}

fn configuration(cfg: &CaptureConfig) -> Retained<SCStreamConfiguration> {
    unsafe {
        let conf = SCStreamConfiguration::new();
        conf.setWidth(cfg.width);
        conf.setHeight(cfg.height);
        conf.setMinimumFrameInterval(CMTime { value: 1, timescale: cfg.fps, flags: CMTimeFlags::Valid, epoch: 0 });
        conf.setPixelFormat(0x34323076); // '420v' NV12, video range
        conf.setShowsCursor(false);
        conf.setQueueDepth(cfg.queue_depth);
        conf
    }
}

/// Starts capturing the main display. Blocks until ScreenCaptureKit answers (20 s at most).
/// The error text is what ScreenCaptureKit said, for the log only.
pub fn start(cfg: CaptureConfig, on_frame: FrameFn) -> Result<Capture, String> {
    let (tx, rx) = channel::<Result<SendBox<Retained<SCShareableContent>>, String>>();
    let handler = RcBlock::new(move |content: *mut SCShareableContent, error: *mut NSError| {
        if content.is_null() {
            let _ = tx.send(Err(describe(error)));
        } else {
            let content = unsafe { Retained::retain(content) }.unwrap();
            let _ = tx.send(Ok(SendBox(content)));
        }
    });
    unsafe { SCShareableContent::getShareableContentWithCompletionHandler(&handler) };
    let content = match rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(20)) {
        Ok(Ok(content)) => content.0,
        Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(format!("SCShareableContent: {e}")),
        Err(e) => return Err(format!("SCShareableContent: {e}")),
    };
    unsafe {
        let displays = content.displays();
        let main_id = CGMainDisplayID();
        let chosen = displays.iter().find(|d| d.displayID() == main_id);
        let display_is_main = chosen.is_some();
        let Some(display) = chosen.or_else(|| displays.firstObject()) else {
            return Err("no displays in SCShareableContent".into());
        };
        let excluded: Retained<NSArray<SCWindow>> = NSArray::new();
        let filter = SCContentFilter::initWithDisplay_excludingWindows(SCContentFilter::alloc(), &display, &excluded);
        let stream =
            SCStream::initWithFilter_configuration_delegate(SCStream::alloc(), &filter, &configuration(&cfg), None);
        let output = StreamOutput::alloc().set_ivars(Ivars { on_frame });
        let output: Retained<StreamOutput> = msg_send![super(output), init];
        let queue = DispatchQueue::new("net.gome.remote-agent.capture", None);
        stream
            .addStreamOutput_type_sampleHandlerQueue_error(
                ProtocolObject::from_ref(&*output),
                SCStreamOutputType::Screen,
                Some(&queue),
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("addStreamOutput: {}", e.localizedDescription()))?;
        let (started_tx, started_rx) = channel::<Result<(), String>>();
        let started = RcBlock::new(move |error: *mut NSError| {
            let _ = started_tx.send(if error.is_null() { Ok(()) } else { Err(describe(error)) });
        });
        stream.startCaptureWithCompletionHandler(Some(&started));
        match started_rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(20)) {
            Ok(Ok(())) => {}
            Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(format!("startCapture: {e}")),
            Err(e) => return Err(format!("startCapture: {e}")),
        }
        println!("[capture] started {}x{}@{}", cfg.width, cfg.height, cfg.fps);
        Ok(Capture { stream, _output: output, _queue: queue, display_is_main })
    }
}

impl Capture {
    /// Changes the size and rate of the running stream.
    pub fn update(&self, cfg: &CaptureConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
        let (tx, rx) = channel::<Result<(), String>>();
        let done = RcBlock::new(move |error: *mut NSError| {
            let _ = tx.send(if error.is_null() { Ok(()) } else { Err(describe(error)) });
        });
        unsafe { self.stream.updateConfiguration_completionHandler(&configuration(cfg), Some(&done)) };
        rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| format!("updateConfiguration: {e}"))?
    }

    pub fn stop(self) {
        let (tx, rx) = channel::<()>();
        let done = RcBlock::new(move |_error: *mut NSError| {
            let _ = tx.send(());
        });
        unsafe { self.stream.stopCaptureWithCompletionHandler(Some(&done)) };
        let _ = rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5));
        println!("[capture] stopped (main display: {})", self.display_is_main);
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/mac/encoder.rs`, ported from `$SPIKE/src/encoder.rs`. What changed: no trace, no SPS patch (it did not help), no global statistics or `FORCE_KEY`/`SINK` statics; the frame sink is passed in and reached through the VideoToolbox refcon (freed in `Drop`); `set_bitrate` for the congestion controller; the hardware flag is requested with "enable" (not "require") and logged; constrained baseline fixed; low-latency rate control with the spike's fallback.

```rust
//! VideoToolbox H.264 hardware encoder: real-time, constrained baseline, no B-frames,
//! low-latency rate control when the system offers it. Ported from the spike's
//! `encoder.rs`; the click trace, the SPS patch and the global statistics are gone, and
//! these are new: a per-encoder frame sink, a settable bitrate, and a keyframe flag.
use super::ffi::*;
use crate::frame::EncodedFrame;
use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering::Relaxed};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

pub type FrameSink = Arc<dyn Fn(EncodedFrame) + Send + Sync>;

pub struct Config {
    pub width: i32,
    pub height: i32,
    pub fps: i32,
    pub bitrate_bps: i32,
    pub key_interval: Duration,
}

pub struct Encoder {
    session: VTSession,
    cfg: Config,
    start: Instant,
    last_key: Instant,
    refcon: *mut FrameSink,
    force_key: AtomicBool,
}
// The session is only used from the capture callback thread and the keepalive thread, which
// the pipeline serialises behind one mutex.
unsafe impl Send for Encoder {}

struct Submitted {
    at: Instant,
}

const FOURCC_AVC1: u32 = 0x61766331;

extern "C" fn output_cb(
    refcon: *mut c_void,
    frame_refcon: *mut c_void,
    status: OSStatus,
    _flags: u32,
    sb: CMSampleBufferRef,
) {
    let submitted: Box<Submitted> = unsafe { Box::from_raw(frame_refcon as *mut Submitted) };
    if status != 0 || sb.is_null() {
        eprintln!("[enc] callback status {status}");
        return;
    }
    let sink: &FrameSink = unsafe { &*(refcon as *const FrameSink) };
    unsafe {
        // A keyframe has no NotSync attachment, or it is false.
        let mut key = true;
        let attachments = CMSampleBufferGetSampleAttachmentsArray(sb, 0);
        if !attachments.is_null() && CFArrayGetCount(attachments) > 0 {
            let dict = CFArrayGetValueAtIndex(attachments, 0);
            let not_sync = CFDictionaryGetValue(dict, kCMSampleAttachmentKey_NotSync);
            if !not_sync.is_null() && not_sync == kCFBooleanTrue {
                key = false;
            }
        }
        let block = CMSampleBufferGetDataBuffer(sb);
        if block.is_null() {
            return;
        }
        let len = CMBlockBufferGetDataLength(block);
        let mut raw = vec![0u8; len];
        if CMBlockBufferCopyDataBytes(block, 0, len, raw.as_mut_ptr()) != 0 {
            return;
        }
        // VideoToolbox gives length-prefixed NAL units; WebRTC wants Annex B.
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(len + 128);
        let mut nal_len_size = 4usize;
        if key {
            let fd = CMSampleBufferGetFormatDescription(sb);
            let (mut count, mut header_len) = (0usize, 0i32);
            let (mut ptr, mut size): (*const u8, usize) = (std::ptr::null(), 0);
            if CMVideoFormatDescriptionGetH264ParameterSetAtIndex(
                fd,
                0,
                &mut ptr,
                &mut size,
                &mut count,
                &mut header_len,
            ) == 0
            {
                nal_len_size = header_len as usize;
                for index in 0..count {
                    if CMVideoFormatDescriptionGetH264ParameterSetAtIndex(
                        fd,
                        index,
                        &mut ptr,
                        &mut size,
                        &mut count,
                        &mut header_len,
                    ) == 0
                    {
                        out.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0, 0, 1]);
                        out.extend_from_slice(std::slice::from_raw_parts(ptr, size));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let mut at = 0usize;
        while at + nal_len_size <= raw.len() {
            let mut n = 0usize;
            for b in &raw[at..at + nal_len_size] {
                n = (n << 8) | *b as usize;
            }
            at += nal_len_size;
            if at + n > raw.len() {
                break;
            }
            out.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0, 0, 1]);
            out.extend_from_slice(&raw[at..at + n]);
            at += n;
        }
        sink(EncodedFrame { data: out, key, submitted: submitted.at });
    }
}

fn bool_cf(b: bool) -> CFTypeRef {
    unsafe {
        if b {
            kCFBooleanTrue
        } else {
            kCFBooleanFalse
        }
    }
}

impl Encoder {
    pub fn new(cfg: Config, sink: FrameSink) -> anyhow::Result<Encoder> {
        // Low-latency rate control first; fall back without it if the session cannot be made.
        let refcon = Box::into_raw(Box::new(sink));
        match Self::create(&cfg, refcon, true).or_else(|e| {
            eprintln!("[enc] {e}; retrying without low-latency rate control");
            Self::create(&cfg, refcon, false)
        }) {
            Ok((session, low_latency)) => {
                let mut hardware: CFTypeRef = std::ptr::null();
                let status = unsafe {
                    VTSessionCopyProperty(
                        session,
                        kVTCompressionPropertyKey_UsingHardwareAcceleratedVideoEncoder,
                        std::ptr::null(),
                        &mut hardware,
                    )
                };
                println!(
                    "[enc] session {}x{} fps={} bitrate={}Mbps low_latency={low_latency} hardware={}",
                    cfg.width,
                    cfg.height,
                    cfg.fps,
                    cfg.bitrate_bps as f64 / 1e6,
                    status == 0 && hardware == unsafe { kCFBooleanTrue }
                );
                Ok(Encoder {
                    session,
                    cfg,
                    start: Instant::now(),
                    last_key: Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(3600),
                    refcon,
                    force_key: AtomicBool::new(true),
                })
            }
            Err(e) => {
                drop(unsafe { Box::from_raw(refcon) });
                Err(e)
            }
        }
    }

    fn create(cfg: &Config, refcon: *mut FrameSink, low_latency: bool) -> anyhow::Result<(VTSession, bool)> {
        let mut spec_pairs: Vec<(CFTypeRef, CFTypeRef)> =
            unsafe { vec![(kVTVideoEncoderSpecification_EnableHardwareAcceleratedVideoEncoder, kCFBooleanTrue)] };
        if low_latency {
            spec_pairs.push((unsafe { kVTVideoEncoderSpecification_EnableLowLatencyRateControl }, bool_cf(true)));
        }
        let spec = cf_dict(&spec_pairs);
        let mut session: VTSession = std::ptr::null_mut();
        let status = unsafe {
            VTCompressionSessionCreate(
                std::ptr::null(),
                cfg.width,
                cfg.height,
                FOURCC_AVC1,
                spec.0,
                std::ptr::null(),
                std::ptr::null(),
                output_cb,
                refcon as *mut c_void,
                &mut session,
            )
        };
        if status != 0 || session.is_null() {
            anyhow::bail!("VTCompressionSessionCreate failed: OSStatus {status} (low_latency={low_latency})");
        }
        let set = |key: CFTypeRef, value: CFTypeRef, name: &str| unsafe {
            let status = VTSessionSetProperty(session, key, value);
            if status != 0 {
                eprintln!("[enc] set {name} failed: OSStatus {status}");
            }
        };
        unsafe {
            set(kVTCompressionPropertyKey_RealTime, kCFBooleanTrue, "RealTime");
            set(kVTCompressionPropertyKey_AllowFrameReordering, kCFBooleanFalse, "AllowFrameReordering");
            set(
                kVTCompressionPropertyKey_ProfileLevel,
                kVTProfileLevel_H264_ConstrainedBaseline_AutoLevel,
                "ProfileLevel",
            );
            set(kVTCompressionPropertyKey_AverageBitRate, cf_i32(cfg.bitrate_bps).0, "AverageBitRate");
            set(kVTCompressionPropertyKey_ExpectedFrameRate, cf_i32(cfg.fps).0, "ExpectedFrameRate");
            set(
                kVTCompressionPropertyKey_MaxKeyFrameInterval,
                cf_i32(cfg.fps * cfg.key_interval.as_secs() as i32).0,
                "MaxKeyFrameInterval",
            );
            if !low_latency {
                // Both return -12900 under low-latency rate control, so only the classic path sets them.
                set(kVTCompressionPropertyKey_MaxFrameDelayCount, cf_i32(0).0, "MaxFrameDelayCount");
                set(
                    kVTCompressionPropertyKey_PrioritizeEncodingSpeedOverQuality,
                    kCFBooleanTrue,
                    "PrioritizeEncodingSpeedOverQuality",
                );
            }
            let status = VTCompressionSessionPrepareToEncodeFrames(session);
            if status != 0 {
                eprintln!("[enc] PrepareToEncodeFrames: OSStatus {status}");
            }
        }
        Ok((session, low_latency))
    }

    pub fn width(&self) -> i32 {
        self.cfg.width
    }
    pub fn height(&self) -> i32 {
        self.cfg.height
    }

    /// The next frame submitted is a keyframe (new viewer, PLI/FIR).
    pub fn request_keyframe(&self) {
        self.force_key.store(true, Relaxed);
    }
    pub fn keyframe_pending(&self) -> bool {
        self.force_key.load(Relaxed)
    }

    /// Applies a new average bitrate (the congestion controller's decision).
    pub fn set_bitrate(&mut self, bps: i32) {
        self.cfg.bitrate_bps = bps;
        let status =
            unsafe { VTSessionSetProperty(self.session, kVTCompressionPropertyKey_AverageBitRate, cf_i32(bps).0) };
        if status != 0 {
            eprintln!("[enc] set AverageBitRate failed: OSStatus {status}");
        }
    }

    /// Submits the frame immediately. VideoToolbox may deliver the output callback before
    /// this returns, so the sink must not call back into the encoder.
    pub fn encode(&mut self, pixel_buffer: CVPixelBufferRef) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let force = self.force_key.swap(false, Relaxed) || now.duration_since(self.last_key) >= self.cfg.key_interval;
        let props = if force {
            self.last_key = now;
            Some(cf_dict(&[(unsafe { kVTEncodeFrameOptionKey_ForceKeyFrame }, bool_cf(true))]))
        } else {
            None
        };
        let pts = CMTime::make(now.duration_since(self.start).as_micros() as i64, 1_000_000);
        let refcon = Box::into_raw(Box::new(Submitted { at: now })) as *mut c_void;
        let mut flags = 0u32;
        let status = unsafe {
            VTCompressionSessionEncodeFrame(
                self.session,
                pixel_buffer,
                pts,
                CMTime::invalid(),
                props.as_ref().map(|p| p.0).unwrap_or(std::ptr::null()),
                refcon,
                &mut flags,
            )
        };
        if status != 0 {
            eprintln!("[enc] EncodeFrame failed: OSStatus {status}");
            drop(unsafe { Box::from_raw(refcon as *mut Submitted) });
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for Encoder {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        unsafe {
            VTCompressionSessionCompleteFrames(self.session, CMTime::invalid());
            VTCompressionSessionInvalidate(self.session);
            drop(Box::from_raw(self.refcon));
        }
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/mac/pipeline.rs`, ported from the frame callback and keepalive loop in `$SPIKE/src/main.rs`. What is new: it can be started, resized, pointed at another receiver and stopped; the encoder follows the size of the frames it receives (so a resize needs no coordination); black-frame detection runs on the luma plane before the buffer reaches the encoder (locking it afterwards waits for the encoder); one statistics line every ten seconds while a receiver is attached, never per frame.

```rust
//! Capture -> encoder -> peer, running only while a session exists. Ported from the
//! spike's `main.rs` (frame callback, keepalive thread); new: it can be started, resized,
//! re-targeted at another peer and stopped, the encoder follows the size of the frames it
//! receives, and it tracks black frames for the status.
use super::capture::{self, Capture, CaptureConfig};
use super::encoder::{Config as EncoderConfig, Encoder, FrameSink};
use super::ffi::*;
use crate::black::{luma_is_black, BlackDetector};
use crate::frame::FrameTarget;
use crate::keepalive::{should_resubmit, Ages};
use crate::size::{fps_for, Size};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering::Relaxed};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

const QUEUE_DEPTH: isize = 3;
const KEY_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3);
/// The black-frame check reads a few hundred bytes; once per 250 ms is plenty.
const BLACK_CHECK_EVERY: Duration = Duration::from_millis(250);

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Plan {
    size: Size,
    fps: u32,
    bitrate: u32,
}

struct Inner {
    encoder: Mutex<Option<Encoder>>,
    last_frame: Mutex<Option<(PixBuf, Instant)>>,
    last_submit: Mutex<Instant>,
    sink: Arc<Mutex<Option<Arc<dyn FrameTarget>>>>,
    capture: Mutex<Option<Capture>>,
    running: AtomicBool,
    plan: Mutex<Plan>,
    detector: Mutex<(BlackDetector, Instant)>,
    last_content_secs: AtomicU64,
    sent_frames: AtomicU64,
    sent_bytes: AtomicU64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Pipeline {
    inner: Arc<Inner>,
}

impl Default for Pipeline {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let now = Instant::now();
        Pipeline {
            inner: Arc::new(Inner {
                encoder: Mutex::new(None),
                last_frame: Mutex::new(None),
                last_submit: Mutex::new(now),
                sink: Arc::new(Mutex::new(None)),
                capture: Mutex::new(None),
                running: AtomicBool::new(false),
                plan: Mutex::new(Plan { size: Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 }, fps: 60, bitrate: 6_000_000 }),
                detector: Mutex::new((BlackDetector::default(), now)),
                last_content_secs: AtomicU64::new(0),
                sent_frames: AtomicU64::new(0),
                sent_bytes: AtomicU64::new(0),
            }),
        }
    }
}

impl Pipeline {
    /// Starts capturing at `size`, or re-targets a running capture at `target` and the new size.
    pub fn start(&self, size: Size, bitrate: u32, target: Arc<dyn FrameTarget>) -> Result<(), String> {
        let inner = &self.inner;
        *inner.plan.lock().unwrap() = Plan { size, fps: fps_for(size), bitrate };
        *inner.sink.lock().unwrap() = Some(target);
        if inner.running.load(Relaxed) {
            self.apply_plan()?;
            self.request_keyframe();
            return Ok(());
        }
        inner.detector.lock().unwrap().0.reset();
        let config = Self::capture_config(&inner.plan.lock().unwrap());
        let me = self.clone();
        let on_frame: capture::FrameFn = Arc::new(move |pixel_buffer, at| me.on_frame(pixel_buffer, at));
        let capture = capture::start(config, on_frame)?;
        *inner.capture.lock().unwrap() = Some(capture);
        inner.running.store(true, Relaxed);
        let me = self.clone();
        std::thread::spawn(move || me.keepalive_loop());
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A new stream size (the page's `size` message).
    pub fn resize(&self, size: Size, bitrate_cap: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
        {
            let mut plan = self.inner.plan.lock().unwrap();
            plan.size = size;
            plan.fps = fps_for(size);
            plan.bitrate = plan.bitrate.min(bitrate_cap);
        }
        self.apply_plan()
    }

    fn apply_plan(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let config = Self::capture_config(&self.inner.plan.lock().unwrap());
        match self.inner.capture.lock().unwrap().as_ref() {
            Some(capture) => capture.update(&config),
            None => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    fn capture_config(plan: &Plan) -> CaptureConfig {
        CaptureConfig {
            width: plan.size.w as usize,
            height: plan.size.h as usize,
            fps: plan.fps as i32,
            queue_depth: QUEUE_DEPTH,
        }
    }

    pub fn stop(&self) {
        let inner = &self.inner;
        inner.running.store(false, Relaxed);
        *inner.sink.lock().unwrap() = None;
        let capture = inner.capture.lock().unwrap().take();
        if let Some(capture) = capture {
            capture.stop(); // consumes the stream, which releases the frame callback
        }
        *inner.encoder.lock().unwrap() = None;
        *inner.last_frame.lock().unwrap() = None;
    }

    pub fn request_keyframe(&self) {
        if let Some(encoder) = self.inner.encoder.lock().unwrap().as_ref() {
            encoder.request_keyframe();
        }
    }

    pub fn set_bitrate(&self, bps: u32) {
        self.inner.plan.lock().unwrap().bitrate = bps;
        if let Some(encoder) = self.inner.encoder.lock().unwrap().as_mut() {
            encoder.set_bitrate(bps as i32);
        }
    }

    /// Only content-free frames for a while (the status' "suspect").
    pub fn suspect(&self) -> bool {
        self.inner.detector.lock().unwrap().0.suspect(Instant::now())
    }

    /// Seconds since the epoch of the last captured frame that had content.
    pub fn last_capture_at(&self) -> Option<u64> {
        match self.inner.last_content_secs.load(Relaxed) {
            0 => None,
            secs => Some(secs),
        }
    }

    fn frame_sink(&self) -> FrameSink {
        let inner = self.inner.clone();
        Arc::new(move |frame| {
            let bytes = frame.data.len() as u64;
            if let Some(peer) = inner.sink.lock().unwrap().as_ref() {
                if peer.send_frame(frame) {
                    inner.sent_frames.fetch_add(1, Relaxed);
                    inner.sent_bytes.fetch_add(bytes, Relaxed);
                }
            }
        })
    }

    /// Runs on ScreenCaptureKit's queue for every delivered frame.
    fn on_frame(&self, pixel_buffer: CVPixelBufferRef, at: Instant) {
        let inner = &self.inner;
        // 1. Black-frame detection, before the buffer goes to the encoder (locking it after
        //    the submit waits for the encoder to let go of it).
        {
            let mut detector = inner.detector.lock().unwrap();
            if at.duration_since(detector.1) >= BLACK_CHECK_EVERY {
                detector.1 = at;
                let black = Self::is_black(pixel_buffer);
                detector.0.observe(at, black);
                if !black {
                    let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0);
                    inner.last_content_secs.store(now, Relaxed);
                }
            }
        }
        // 2. Keep the frame for the keepalive thread, then encode it.
        *inner.last_frame.lock().unwrap() = Some((PixBuf::retain(pixel_buffer), at));
        self.submit(pixel_buffer);
    }

    fn is_black(pixel_buffer: CVPixelBufferRef) -> bool {
        unsafe {
            if CVPixelBufferLockBaseAddress(pixel_buffer, 1) != 0 {
                return false; // cannot tell: not suspicious
            }
            let base = CVPixelBufferGetBaseAddressOfPlane(pixel_buffer, 0);
            let (w, h) = (CVPixelBufferGetWidth(pixel_buffer), CVPixelBufferGetHeight(pixel_buffer));
            let stride = CVPixelBufferGetBytesPerRowOfPlane(pixel_buffer, 0);
            let black = if base.is_null() {
                false
            } else {
                luma_is_black(std::slice::from_raw_parts(base, stride * h), w, h, stride)
            };
            CVPixelBufferUnlockBaseAddress(pixel_buffer, 1);
            black
        }
    }

    /// Encodes `pixel_buffer`, first making sure the encoder has the buffer's size.
    fn submit(&self, pixel_buffer: CVPixelBufferRef) {
        let inner = &self.inner;
        let (w, h) =
            unsafe { (CVPixelBufferGetWidth(pixel_buffer) as i32, CVPixelBufferGetHeight(pixel_buffer) as i32) };
        let mut encoder = inner.encoder.lock().unwrap();
        if encoder.as_ref().map(|e| (e.width(), e.height())) != Some((w, h)) {
            let plan = *inner.plan.lock().unwrap();
            let config = EncoderConfig {
                width: w,
                height: h,
                fps: plan.fps as i32,
                bitrate_bps: plan.bitrate as i32,
                key_interval: KEY_INTERVAL,
            };
            match Encoder::new(config, self.frame_sink()) {
                Ok(new) => *encoder = Some(new), // starts with a keyframe request
                Err(e) => {
                    eprintln!("[pipeline] encoder: {e}");
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(encoder) = encoder.as_mut() {
            encoder.encode(pixel_buffer);
        }
        *inner.last_submit.lock().unwrap() = Instant::now();
    }

    /// ScreenCaptureKit only delivers a frame when the screen changes: repeat the last one
    /// when a keyframe is pending and as a low-rate keepalive on a still screen.
    fn keepalive_loop(&self) {
        let inner = &self.inner;
        let mut report = (Instant::now(), 0u64, 0u64);
        while inner.running.load(Relaxed) {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
            let snapshot = inner.last_frame.lock().unwrap().as_ref().map(|(pb, at)| (PixBuf::retain(pb.0), *at));
            let Some((pixel_buffer, captured)) = snapshot else { continue };
            let pending = inner.encoder.lock().unwrap().as_ref().map(|e| e.keyframe_pending()).unwrap_or(false);
            let ages = Ages {
                since_capture: captured.elapsed(),
                since_submit: inner.last_submit.lock().unwrap().elapsed(),
                keyframe_pending: pending,
            };
            if should_resubmit(&ages) {
                self.submit(pixel_buffer.0);
            }
            // One line every ten seconds while someone watches, never per frame.
            if report.0.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(10) {
                let (frames, bytes) = (inner.sent_frames.load(Relaxed), inner.sent_bytes.load(Relaxed));
                let secs = report.0.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
                println!(
                    "[stats] sent_fps={:.1} kbps={:.0}",
                    (frames - report.1) as f64 / secs,
                    (bytes - report.2) as f64 * 8.0 / 1000.0 / secs
                );
                report = (Instant::now(), frames, bytes);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/examples/capture_smoke.rs` is a device check for this task (no WebRTC involved):

```rust
//! Device check for the capture and encode pipeline, without WebRTC: captures the main
//! display for a few seconds through the real `Pipeline`, changes its size once, and prints
//! how many frames and keyframes came out. Run it from Terminal on the Mac (Terminal needs
//! the Screen Recording permission for this; remove it again afterwards if you like):
//!
//!   cargo run --release --example capture_smoke
//!
//! Healthy output on an M-series Mac: `[enc] ... hardware=true`, 100+ frames in the first
//! phase (a moving screen), at least one keyframe, and different frame sizes per phase.
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
fn main() {
    use gome_agent::frame::{EncodedFrame, FrameTarget};
    use gome_agent::mac::{ffi, pipeline::Pipeline};
    use gome_agent::size::Size;
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering::Relaxed};
    use std::sync::Arc;
    use std::time::Duration;

    struct Count {
        frames: AtomicU64,
        keys: AtomicU64,
        bytes: AtomicU64,
    }
    impl FrameTarget for Count {
        fn send_frame(&self, frame: EncodedFrame) -> bool {
            self.frames.fetch_add(1, Relaxed);
            self.bytes.fetch_add(frame.data.len() as u64, Relaxed);
            if frame.key {
                self.keys.fetch_add(1, Relaxed);
            }
            true
        }
    }

    std::thread::spawn(|| {
        let count = Arc::new(Count { frames: AtomicU64::new(0), keys: AtomicU64::new(0), bytes: AtomicU64::new(0) });
        let pipeline = Pipeline::default();
        let phases = [(Size { w: 2560, h: 1440 }, 12_000_000u32), (Size { w: 1280, h: 720 }, 6_000_000)];
        for (index, (size, bitrate)) in phases.into_iter().enumerate() {
            let (f0, k0, b0) = (count.frames.load(Relaxed), count.keys.load(Relaxed), count.bytes.load(Relaxed));
            if index == 0 {
                pipeline.start(size, bitrate, count.clone()).expect("capture started (Screen Recording allowed?)");
            } else {
                pipeline.resize(size, bitrate).expect("stream reconfigured");
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));
            println!(
                "phase {index}: {}x{} -> {} frames, {} keyframes, {:.2} Mbps",
                size.w,
                size.h,
                count.frames.load(Relaxed) - f0,
                count.keys.load(Relaxed) - k0,
                (count.bytes.load(Relaxed) - b0) as f64 * 8.0 / 5.0 / 1e6
            );
        }
        println!("last content at: {:?}, suspect: {}", pipeline.last_capture_at(), pipeline.suspect());
        pipeline.stop();
        std::process::exit(0);
    });
    unsafe { ffi::CFRunLoopRun() };
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
fn main() {
    eprintln!("capture_smoke runs on macOS only");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Device check: capture and encode on the real screen (owner-assisted: Terminal needs the Screen Recording permission)**

On the Mac, in Terminal.app (not over ssh: the permission belongs to the app that starts the program; if macOS asks, allow Terminal under Privacy & Security > Screen & System Audio Recording, and remove it again afterwards if you like), with something moving on the screen (a video, or drag a window around during the first phase):

```sh
cd ~/gome-remote-agent-build && cargo run --release --example capture_smoke
```

Expected output, in this shape:

```text
[capture] started 2560x1440@60
[enc] session 2560x1440 fps=60 bitrate=12Mbps low_latency=true hardware=true
phase 0: 2560x1440 -> 150..300 frames, 1+ keyframes, 3..12 Mbps
[capture] ... (reconfigured)
[enc] session 1280x720 fps=60 bitrate=6Mbps low_latency=true hardware=true
phase 1: 1280x720 -> 100..300 frames, 1+ keyframes, 1..6 Mbps
last content at: Some(<seconds>), suspect: false
```

`hardware=true` is required (the M-series encoder), a second `[enc] session` line with the new size proves that the encoder follows a resize, and `suspect: false` with a moving screen proves the black-frame detector does not cry wolf. If `hardware=false`, or the program exits with "capture started" failing, record the exact text before changing anything. A static screen in phase 1 shows far fewer frames (the 100 ms repeat gives about 50 in five seconds at a few hundred kbit/s): that is correct.

### Task 8: WebRTC peer (str0m): H.264 + RTX, tailnet-only candidates, bandwidth estimate, idle close

Spec: D1 (offer/answer, direct media), D2 (candidate filter: tailnet addresses only, prflx from the tailnet allowed), D3 (str0m, H.264 + RTX, playout-delay, abs-capture-time, 4 MiB UDP buffers, TWCC), D5 (a session closes after 10 s without anything received), P2 (ICE rules).

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/mac/src/peer.rs`
- Modify: `agent/mac/src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `tailnet::{filter_offer_candidates, is_tailnet_ip}`, `frame::{EncodedFrame, FrameTarget}` (Task 7), str0m 0.24.
- Produces:
  - `peer::accept(offer_sdp: &str, params: PeerParams, hooks: Arc<dyn PeerHooks>) -> anyhow::Result<(String, PeerHandle)>`: the answer SDP and a handle.
  - `PeerParams { bind_ip: IpAddr, initial_bitrate: u32, desired_bitrate: u32 }` (`Clone`).
  - `trait PeerHooks: Send + Sync + 'static { request_keyframe(), estimate(bps: u64), input(&[u8]), closed() }`; `closed()` is called exactly once, from the peer thread, however the peer ends.
  - `PeerHandle { is_alive(), close() }` (`Clone`), and `impl FrameTarget for PeerHandle` (so a `Pipeline` can feed it).
  - `IDLE_LIMIT = 10 s`, `idle_expired(last_rx, now)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`lib.rs` gains `pub mod peer;`. Create `agent/mac/src/peer.rs` with the test module only. The tests use a second str0m as a browser-like client: the answer to its offer must contain H.264, **`rtx`** (without it every lost packet waits for a keyframe, about a second of freeze, the spike's worst finding) and `transport-cc` (without it the estimator gets no feedback), and must advertise the agent's own host candidate; a stranger's candidate in the offer, or garbage instead of an offer, must not break `accept`.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn idle_expires_only_after_ten_seconds() {
        let t0 = Instant::now();
        assert!(!idle_expired(t0, t0 + Duration::from_secs(10)));
        assert!(idle_expired(t0, t0 + Duration::from_secs(10) + Duration::from_millis(1)));
        assert!(!idle_expired(t0 + Duration::from_secs(5), t0), "a clock that went backwards is not idle");
    }

    struct Quiet;
    impl PeerHooks for Quiet {
        fn request_keyframe(&self) {}
        fn estimate(&self, _bps: u64) {}
        fn input(&self, _data: &[u8]) {}
        fn closed(&self) {}
    }

    /// An offer like a browser's: a receive-only H.264 video, built by a second str0m.
    fn browser_like_offer(extra_candidate_lines: &str) -> String {
        use str0m::media::{Direction, MediaKind};
        let mut client = RtcConfig::new().clear_codecs().enable_h264(true).build(Instant::now());
        let mut api = client.sdp_api();
        api.add_media(MediaKind::Video, Direction::RecvOnly, None, None, None);
        let (offer, _pending) = api.apply().expect("an offer");
        let mut sdp = offer.to_sdp_string();
        sdp.push_str(extra_candidate_lines);
        sdp
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_answer_offers_h264_with_rtx_and_the_congestion_feedback_the_estimator_needs() {
        let params = PeerParams {
            bind_ip: "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap(),
            initial_bitrate: 6_000_000,
            desired_bitrate: 12_000_000,
        };
        let (answer, handle) = accept(&browser_like_offer(""), params, Arc::new(Quiet)).expect("an answer");
        assert!(answer.contains("H264/90000"), "{answer}");
        assert!(answer.contains("rtx/90000"), "without RTX a lost packet freezes the picture for a second:\n{answer}");
        assert!(answer.contains("transport-cc"), "bandwidth estimation needs transport-wide feedback:\n{answer}");
        assert!(answer.contains("a=candidate:"), "the agent advertises its own host candidate");
        assert!(answer.contains("127.0.0.1"), "and it is the address the socket is bound to");
        handle.close();
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stranger_candidate_in_the_offer_does_not_make_the_offer_unusable() {
        // The filter removes these lines before str0m sees them (tailnet.rs tests the filter itself).
        let lines = "a=candidate:9 1 udp 2122260223 192.168.0.5 51001 typ host\r\n\
                     a=candidate:10 1 udp 2122260223 0b3a1c9e-77aa-4c1d-9d3e-0a1b2c3d4e5f.local 51002 typ host\r\n";
        let params = PeerParams {
            bind_ip: "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap(),
            initial_bitrate: 6_000_000,
            desired_bitrate: 12_000_000,
        };
        let (answer, handle) = accept(&browser_like_offer(lines), params, Arc::new(Quiet)).expect("an answer");
        assert!(answer.contains("H264/90000"));
        handle.close();
    }

    #[test]
    fn garbage_instead_of_an_offer_is_an_error_not_a_panic() {
        let params = PeerParams {
            bind_ip: "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap(),
            initial_bitrate: 6_000_000,
            desired_bitrate: 12_000_000,
        };
        for bad in ["", "hello", "v=0\r\n", "v=0\r\nm=video"] {
            assert!(accept(bad, params.clone(), Arc::new(Quiet)).is_err(), "{bad:?}");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `cd agent/mac && cargo test --lib peer 2>&1 | grep -E "^error" | sort | uniq -c`
Expected: ``cannot find function `accept` `` and ``cannot find struct `PeerParams` ``.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the peer**

`agent/mac/src/peer.rs` is ported from `$SPIKE/src/peer.rs`. What changed: the pacer is gone (it did not help); the click trace and shared statistics are gone; the offer's candidates are filtered (`filter_offer_candidates`) and every received datagram whose source is not a tailnet address is dropped before ICE sees it, which is how a peer-reflexive candidate from any other address is kept out while the tailnet address of a browser that hid its host candidate behind an mDNS name still works; bandwidth estimation is on (`enable_bwe`, `set_desired_bitrate`, `Event::EgressBitrateEstimate` forwarded to `PeerHooks::estimate`); nothing received for 10 s ends the peer; `PeerHooks::closed` always runs; the receiving thread owns the socket reads and the peer thread owns str0m (sans-IO), as in the spike.

```rust
//! One WebRTC peer (str0m, sans-IO) per session: sends the H.264 track, receives the
//! `input` data channel. Ported from the spike's `peer.rs`; the pacer, the click trace
//! and the shared statistics are gone, and these are new: tailnet-only candidates and
//! source addresses, bandwidth estimation, a 10 s idle close, and the release hook.
use crate::frame::{EncodedFrame, FrameTarget};
use crate::tailnet::{filter_offer_candidates, is_tailnet_ip};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::net::{IpAddr, SocketAddr, UdpSocket};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering::Relaxed};
use std::sync::mpsc::{channel, RecvTimeoutError, Sender};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use str0m::bwe::{Bitrate, BweKind};
use str0m::change::SdpOffer;
use str0m::media::{Frequency, MediaTime, Mid};
use str0m::net::{Protocol, Receive};
use str0m::rtp::AbsCaptureTime;
use str0m::{Candidate, Event, IceConnectionState, Input, Output, Rtc, RtcConfig};

/// Nothing received from the peer for this long closes the session (spec D5).
pub const IDLE_LIMIT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
/// UDP send/receive buffers. The macOS defaults (9 KiB / 768 KiB) lost packets at 4K.
pub const SOCKET_BUFFER: usize = 4 << 20;
/// RTX history in packets (the spike's value).
pub const RTX_PACKETS: usize = 4000;

pub fn idle_expired(last_rx: Instant, now: Instant) -> bool {
    now.saturating_duration_since(last_rx) > IDLE_LIMIT
}

/// The session's side of the peer. Called from the peer thread.
pub trait PeerHooks: Send + Sync + 'static {
    /// A new viewer or a PLI/FIR: the next encoded frame should be a keyframe.
    fn request_keyframe(&self);
    /// The sender-side bandwidth estimate in bits per second.
    fn estimate(&self, bps: u64);
    /// One data channel message from the page.
    fn input(&self, data: &[u8]);
    /// The peer thread ended for any reason (input must release everything held).
    fn closed(&self);
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct PeerParams {
    pub bind_ip: IpAddr,
    /// The estimator's starting point and the ceiling it probes towards.
    pub initial_bitrate: u32,
    pub desired_bitrate: u32,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct PeerHandle {
    alive: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    frames: Sender<Msg>,
}
impl PeerHandle {
    pub fn is_alive(&self) -> bool {
        self.alive.load(Relaxed)
    }
    pub fn close(&self) {
        self.alive.store(false, Relaxed);
    }
}
impl FrameTarget for PeerHandle {
    /// False when the peer is gone.
    fn send_frame(&self, frame: EncodedFrame) -> bool {
        self.is_alive() && self.frames.send(Msg::Frame(frame)).is_ok()
    }
}

enum Msg {
    Udp(Instant, SocketAddr, Vec<u8>),
    Frame(EncodedFrame),
}

fn set_socket_buffers(socket: &UdpSocket, bytes: usize) {
    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
    let value = bytes as i32;
    for option in [libc::SO_SNDBUF, libc::SO_RCVBUF] {
        // SAFETY: a valid socket descriptor and a pointer to a live i32 of the stated size.
        let rc = unsafe {
            libc::setsockopt(
                socket.as_raw_fd(),
                libc::SOL_SOCKET,
                option,
                &value as *const i32 as *const _,
                std::mem::size_of::<i32>() as libc::socklen_t,
            )
        };
        if rc != 0 {
            eprintln!("[peer] setsockopt({option}) failed: {}", std::io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
    }
}

/// Handles an SDP offer: returns the answer and a handle to feed frames and close the peer.
pub fn accept(offer_sdp: &str, params: PeerParams, hooks: Arc<dyn PeerHooks>) -> Result<(String, PeerHandle)> {
    // Only tailnet UDP candidates are ever considered (spec D2).
    let offer =
        SdpOffer::from_sdp_string(&filter_offer_candidates(offer_sdp)).map_err(|e| anyhow!("bad offer: {e:?}"))?;
    let mut rtc = RtcConfig::new()
        .clear_codecs()
        .enable_h264(true)
        .set_send_buffer_video(RTX_PACKETS)
        .enable_bwe(Some(Bitrate::bps(params.initial_bitrate as u64)))
        .set_stats_interval(Some(Duration::from_secs(1)))
        .build(Instant::now());
    rtc.bwe().set_desired_bitrate(Bitrate::bps(params.desired_bitrate as u64));

    // The media socket is bound to the tailnet address, which is also the only host candidate.
    let socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::new(params.bind_ip, 0))?;
    let local = socket.local_addr()?;
    set_socket_buffers(&socket, SOCKET_BUFFER);
    rtc.add_local_candidate(Candidate::host(local, "udp").map_err(|e| anyhow!("candidate: {e:?}"))?);
    let answer = rtc.sdp_api().accept_offer(offer).map_err(|e| anyhow!("accept_offer: {e:?}"))?;
    let answer_sdp = answer.to_sdp_string();

    let (tx, rx) = channel::<Msg>();
    let alive = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    {
        let socket = socket.try_clone()?;
        let tx = tx.clone();
        let alive = alive.clone();
        socket.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_millis(500)))?;
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut buf = vec![0u8; 2048];
            while alive.load(Relaxed) {
                match socket.recv_from(&mut buf) {
                    // A datagram from outside the tailnet never reaches ICE: this is what
                    // keeps a peer-reflexive candidate from any other address out.
                    Ok((n, source)) if is_tailnet_ip(source.ip()) => {
                        if tx.send(Msg::Udp(Instant::now(), source, buf[..n].to_vec())).is_err() {
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    _ => continue,
                }
            }
        });
    }
    let handle = PeerHandle { alive: alive.clone(), frames: tx };
    let thread_alive = alive.clone();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        if let Err(e) = run(rtc, socket, local, rx, &thread_alive, hooks.as_ref()) {
            eprintln!("[peer] ended with error: {e:?}");
        }
        thread_alive.store(false, Relaxed);
        hooks.closed();
        println!("[peer] closed");
    });
    Ok((answer_sdp, handle))
}

fn run(
    mut rtc: Rtc,
    socket: UdpSocket,
    local: SocketAddr,
    rx: std::sync::mpsc::Receiver<Msg>,
    alive: &AtomicBool,
    hooks: &dyn PeerHooks,
) -> Result<()> {
    let epoch = Instant::now();
    let mut mid: Option<Mid> = None;
    let mut payload_type = None;
    let mut connected = false;
    let mut sent_key = false;
    let mut rtx_configured = false;
    let mut last_rx = Instant::now();
    loop {
        let timeout = loop {
            match rtc.poll_output()? {
                Output::Timeout(t) => break t,
                Output::Transmit(t) => {
                    if let Err(e) = socket.send_to(&t.contents, t.destination) {
                        eprintln!("[peer] send_to error: {e}");
                    }
                }
                Output::Event(event) => match event {
                    Event::IceConnectionStateChange(IceConnectionState::Disconnected) => return Ok(()),
                    Event::Connected => {
                        println!("[peer] connected");
                        connected = true;
                        hooks.request_keyframe();
                    }
                    Event::MediaAdded(m) => {
                        mid = Some(m.mid);
                        if !rtx_configured {
                            if let Some(stream) = rtc.direct_api().stream_tx_by_mid(m.mid, None) {
                                // RTX with a 3 s history; without it a lost packet waits for a PLI (about 1 s).
                                stream.set_rtx_cache(RTX_PACKETS, Duration::from_secs(3), Some(0.15f32));
                                rtx_configured = true;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Event::KeyframeRequest(_) => hooks.request_keyframe(),
                    Event::EgressBitrateEstimate(BweKind::Twcc { estimate, .. }) => {
                        hooks.estimate(estimate.as_u64());
                    }
                    Event::ChannelData(data) => hooks.input(&data.data),
                    Event::Closed => return Ok(()),
                    _ => {}
                },
            }
        };
        if !rtc.is_alive() || !alive.load(Relaxed) {
            return Ok(());
        }
        if idle_expired(last_rx, Instant::now()) {
            println!("[peer] idle for {IDLE_LIMIT:?}, closing");
            return Ok(());
        }
        let wait = timeout.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_millis(200));
        match rx.recv_timeout(wait) {
            Ok(Msg::Udp(at, source, data)) => {
                last_rx = at;
                let Ok(contents) = data.as_slice().try_into() else { continue };
                rtc.handle_input(Input::Receive(
                    at,
                    Receive { proto: Protocol::Udp, source, destination: local, contents },
                ))?;
            }
            Ok(Msg::Frame(frame)) => {
                let Some(m) = mid else { continue };
                if !connected || (!sent_key && !frame.key) {
                    continue;
                }
                if payload_type.is_none() {
                    payload_type = rtc.writer(m).and_then(|w| w.payload_params().next().map(|p| p.pt()));
                }
                let Some(pt) = payload_type else { continue };
                let rtp = (frame.submitted.duration_since(epoch).as_micros() as u64) * 90 / 1000;
                let key = frame.key;
                if let Some(writer) = rtc.writer(m) {
                    let capture = std::time::SystemTime::now() - frame.submitted.elapsed();
                    let writer = writer
                        .playout_delay(MediaTime::ZERO, MediaTime::ZERO)
                        .abs_capture_time(AbsCaptureTime { capture_time: capture, clock_offset: None });
                    match writer.write(pt, Instant::now(), MediaTime::new(rtp, Frequency::NINETY_KHZ), frame.data) {
                        Ok(_) => sent_key |= key,
                        Err(e) => eprintln!("[peer] write error: {e:?}"),
                    }
                }
            }
            Err(RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => rtc.handle_input(Input::Timeout(Instant::now()))?,
            Err(RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => return Ok(()),
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 9: Input: message rules, key table, buttons and drag, wheel, typed text, release-all

Spec: D4 (every rule of the input channel), O3 (Hangul key = Caps Lock press), Review Focus 2 and 3.

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/mac/src/{keymap,protocol,typing,pressed}.rs`, `agent/mac/src/mac/inject.rs`, `agent/mac/examples/input_probe.rs`
- Modify: `agent/mac/src/lib.rs`, `agent/mac/src/mac/mod.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 6): `size::{Size, parse_requested}`.
- Produces:
  - `keymap::{lookup(&str) -> Option<KeyAction>, KeyAction::{Code(u16), CapsLockTap}, modifier_flag(u16) -> Option<u64>, KEY_CAPS_LOCK = 57}`
  - `protocol::{parse(&[u8]) -> Option<InputMsg>, InputMsg::{Pointer{x,y}, Button{button,down,x,y}, Wheel{dx,dy}, Key{action,down}, Text(String), Release, Size(Size)}, Button::{Left, Middle, Right}, MAX_MESSAGE = 512, MAX_TEXT_MESSAGE = 8192, MAX_WHEEL = 2000}`
  - `typing::{plan(&str) -> Vec<TypeStep>, TypeStep::{Return, Tab, Units(Vec<u16>)}, MAX_UNITS = 20}`
  - `pressed::{Pressed {key(code, down) -> bool(auto-repeat), button(b, down) -> bool(changed), flags(), held_button(), drain()}, ClickCounter {on_down(button, at, x, y) -> i64}}`
  - macOS: `mac::inject::Injector::{default(), handle(InputMsg), release_all()}` (thread-safe; ignores everything, with one log line, while Accessibility is not granted).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests (platform-independent modules)**

`lib.rs` gains `pub mod keymap; pub mod pressed; pub mod protocol; pub mod typing;`. Create the four files holding only their test modules.

`agent/mac/src/keymap.rs`: well-known keys, modifiers in their physical positions (Alt is Option, Windows is Command), both spellings of the Hangul key, unknown codes dropped, no duplicated code or key in the table, every letter, digit and F-key present, both sides of each modifier share a flag and Caps Lock is not a held modifier.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::collections::HashSet;

    #[test]
    fn well_known_keys_map_to_their_virtual_key_codes() {
        for (code, key) in [
            ("KeyA", 0),
            ("KeyZ", 6),
            ("KeyM", 46),
            ("Digit1", 18),
            ("Digit0", 29),
            ("Enter", 36),
            ("Tab", 48),
            ("Space", 49),
            ("Backspace", 51),
            ("Escape", 53),
            ("ArrowLeft", 123),
            ("ArrowUp", 126),
            ("F1", 122),
            ("F12", 111),
            ("Delete", 117),
            ("Home", 115),
            ("End", 119),
            ("PageUp", 116),
            ("PageDown", 121),
            ("NumpadEnter", 76),
            ("Numpad0", 82),
            ("Numpad9", 92),
            ("Backquote", 50),
            ("IntlBackslash", 10),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(lookup(code), Some(KeyAction::Code(key)), "{code}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn modifiers_keep_their_physical_positions() {
        assert_eq!(lookup("ControlLeft"), Some(KeyAction::Code(59)));
        assert_eq!(lookup("AltLeft"), Some(KeyAction::Code(58)), "Alt is Option");
        assert_eq!(lookup("AltRight"), Some(KeyAction::Code(61)));
        assert_eq!(lookup("MetaLeft"), Some(KeyAction::Code(55)), "Windows key is Command");
        assert_eq!(lookup("MetaRight"), Some(KeyAction::Code(54)));
        assert_eq!(lookup("ShiftRight"), Some(KeyAction::Code(60)));
        assert_eq!(lookup("CapsLock"), Some(KeyAction::Code(KEY_CAPS_LOCK)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn both_spellings_of_the_hangul_key_become_one_caps_lock_tap() {
        assert_eq!(lookup("Lang1"), Some(KeyAction::CapsLockTap));
        assert_eq!(lookup("HangulMode"), Some(KeyAction::CapsLockTap));
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_codes_are_dropped() {
        for code in
            ["", "Unidentified", "keya", "KeyAA", "Lang2", "PrintScreen", "Pause", "F21", "MediaPlayPause", "Key A"]
        {
            assert_eq!(lookup(code), None, "{code:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_table_has_no_duplicate_code_or_key() {
        let names: HashSet<_> = TABLE.iter().map(|(n, _)| *n).collect();
        let keys: HashSet<_> = TABLE.iter().map(|(_, k)| *k).collect();
        assert_eq!(names.len(), TABLE.len(), "a code appears twice");
        assert_eq!(keys.len(), TABLE.len(), "a virtual key code appears twice");
    }

    #[test]
    fn all_letters_digits_and_function_keys_are_present() {
        for c in 'A'..='Z' {
            assert!(lookup(&format!("Key{c}")).is_some(), "Key{c}");
        }
        for d in 0..=9 {
            assert!(lookup(&format!("Digit{d}")).is_some(), "Digit{d}");
            assert!(lookup(&format!("Numpad{d}")).is_some(), "Numpad{d}");
        }
        for f in 1..=20 {
            assert!(lookup(&format!("F{f}")).is_some(), "F{f}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn modifier_flags_cover_both_sides_of_each_modifier() {
        assert_eq!(modifier_flag(56), modifier_flag(60));
        assert_eq!(modifier_flag(59), modifier_flag(62));
        assert_eq!(modifier_flag(58), modifier_flag(61));
        assert_eq!(modifier_flag(55), modifier_flag(54));
        assert_eq!(modifier_flag(0), None);
        assert_eq!(modifier_flag(KEY_CAPS_LOCK), None, "Caps Lock is a toggle, not a held modifier");
        let all: HashSet<_> = [56, 59, 58, 55].iter().map(|k| modifier_flag(*k).unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(all.len(), 4);
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/protocol.rs`: every message kind; button numbers; coordinates outside 0..1 dropped, not clamped; wheel cut to 2000 in both directions; unknown or malformed messages dropped; size messages obey the stream limits; only `text` may exceed 512 bytes and nothing exceeds 8 KiB (the limit is in bytes and Korean is three bytes a character); 512 bytes exactly is accepted for a non-text message and 513 is not.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn p(text: &str) -> Option<InputMsg> {
        parse(text.as_bytes())
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_message_kind_parses() {
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"pointer","x":0.25,"y":1}"#), Some(InputMsg::Pointer { x: 0.25, y: 1.0 }));
        assert_eq!(
            p(r#"{"type":"button","b":2,"down":true,"x":0,"y":0.5}"#),
            Some(InputMsg::Button { button: Button::Right, down: true, x: 0.0, y: 0.5 })
        );
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"wheel","dx":0,"dy":-120}"#), Some(InputMsg::Wheel { dx: 0, dy: -120 }));
        assert_eq!(
            p(r#"{"type":"key","code":"KeyA","down":false}"#),
            Some(InputMsg::Key { action: KeyAction::Code(0), down: false })
        );
        assert_eq!(
            p(r#"{"type":"key","code":"Lang1","down":true}"#),
            Some(InputMsg::Key { action: KeyAction::CapsLockTap, down: true })
        );
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"text","s":"안녕 😀"}"#), Some(InputMsg::Text("안녕 😀".into())));
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"release"}"#), Some(InputMsg::Release));
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"size","w":1920,"h":1080}"#), Some(InputMsg::Size(Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn button_numbers_follow_the_dom_order() {
        for (b, expected) in [(0, Button::Left), (1, Button::Middle), (2, Button::Right)] {
            let msg = format!(r#"{{"type":"button","b":{b},"down":true,"x":0.5,"y":0.5}}"#);
            assert!(matches!(p(&msg), Some(InputMsg::Button { button, .. }) if button == expected));
        }
        for b in ["3", "4", "-1", "1.5", "\"0\"", "null"] {
            let msg = format!(r#"{{"type":"button","b":{b},"down":true,"x":0.5,"y":0.5}}"#);
            assert_eq!(p(&msg), None, "{b}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn coordinates_outside_zero_to_one_are_dropped_not_clamped() {
        for xy in [
            r#""x":-0.01,"y":0.5"#,
            r#""x":1.01,"y":0.5"#,
            r#""x":0.5,"y":2"#,
            r#""x":"0.5","y":0.5"#,
            r#""x":null,"y":0.5"#,
            r#""x":0.5"#,
            r#""y":0.5"#,
        ] {
            assert_eq!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"pointer",{xy}}}"#)), None, "{xy}");
        }
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"pointer","x":1e999,"y":0}"#), None, "overflow to infinity");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wheel_deltas_are_cut_to_the_limit_in_both_directions() {
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"wheel","dx":99999,"dy":-99999}"#), Some(InputMsg::Wheel { dx: 2000, dy: -2000 }));
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"wheel","dx":1.9,"dy":-1.9}"#), Some(InputMsg::Wheel { dx: 1, dy: -1 }));
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"wheel","dx":"1","dy":0}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"wheel","dx":1}"#), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_key_codes_and_types_are_dropped() {
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"key","code":"Nope","down":true}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"key","code":"KeyA"}"#), None, "down is required");
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"key","code":"KeyA","down":1}"#), None, "down must be a boolean");
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"key","code":7,"down":true}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"click","x":0.5,"y":0.5}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"x":0.5,"y":0.5}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":5}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(p("[]"), None);
        assert_eq!(p("not json"), None);
        assert_eq!(p(""), None);
        assert_eq!(parse(&[0xff, 0xfe, 0x00]), None, "not UTF-8");
    }

    #[test]
    fn size_messages_obey_the_stream_limits() {
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"size","w":640,"h":480}"#), Some(InputMsg::Size(Size { w: 640, h: 480 })));
        assert_eq!(p(r#"{"type":"size","w":1921,"h":1081}"#), Some(InputMsg::Size(Size { w: 1920, h: 1080 })));
        for body in [
            r#""w":639,"h":480"#,
            r#""w":640,"h":479"#,
            r#""w":3841,"h":2160"#,
            r#""w":640.5,"h":480"#,
            r#""w":"640","h":480"#,
        ] {
            assert_eq!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"size",{body}}}"#)), None, "{body}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn only_text_may_exceed_512_bytes_and_nothing_exceeds_8_kib() {
        let pad = " ".repeat(600);
        assert_eq!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"pointer","x":0.5,"y":0.5,"pad":"{pad}"}}"#)), None);
        let text = "a".repeat(4000);
        assert_eq!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"text","s":"{text}"}}"#)), Some(InputMsg::Text(text)));
        let too_long = "a".repeat(MAX_TEXT_MESSAGE);
        assert_eq!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"text","s":"{too_long}"}}"#)), None);
        // The limit is on bytes, and Korean is three bytes a character.
        let korean = "가".repeat(2800);
        assert_eq!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"text","s":"{korean}"}}"#)), None);
        let fits = "가".repeat(2700);
        assert!(p(&format!(r#"{{"type":"text","s":"{fits}"}}"#)).is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_512_byte_message_is_the_largest_non_text_message() {
        let base = r#"{"type":"pointer","x":0.5,"y":0.5,"pad":""}"#.len();
        let exact = format!(r#"{{"type":"pointer","x":0.5,"y":0.5,"pad":"{}"}}"#, "x".repeat(MAX_MESSAGE - base));
        assert_eq!(exact.len(), MAX_MESSAGE);
        assert!(p(&exact).is_some());
        let over = format!(r#"{{"type":"pointer","x":0.5,"y":0.5,"pad":"{}"}}"#, "x".repeat(MAX_MESSAGE - base + 1));
        assert_eq!(p(&over), None);
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/typing.rs` (Review Focus 3): short text is one step; CR, LF and CRLF are one Return each; tab is a key and other control characters are dropped; 45 characters are chunked 20, 20, 5; a surrogate pair is never split across chunks.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn units(s: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
        s.encode_utf16().collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn short_text_is_one_step() {
        assert_eq!(plan("hello"), vec![TypeStep::Units(units("hello"))]);
        assert_eq!(plan("한글 입력"), vec![TypeStep::Units(units("한글 입력"))]);
        assert_eq!(plan(""), vec![]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn line_breaks_become_returns_and_crlf_is_one() {
        assert_eq!(
            plan("a\r\nb\nc\rd"),
            vec![
                TypeStep::Units(units("a")),
                TypeStep::Return,
                TypeStep::Units(units("b")),
                TypeStep::Return,
                TypeStep::Units(units("c")),
                TypeStep::Return,
                TypeStep::Units(units("d")),
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(plan("\n\n"), vec![TypeStep::Return, TypeStep::Return]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn tab_is_a_key_and_other_control_characters_are_dropped() {
        assert_eq!(plan("a\tb"), vec![TypeStep::Units(units("a")), TypeStep::Tab, TypeStep::Units(units("b"))]);
        assert_eq!(plan("a\u{0}\u{7}\u{1b}b"), vec![TypeStep::Units(units("ab"))]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn long_text_is_chunked_at_20_units() {
        let steps = plan(&"x".repeat(45));
        let sizes: Vec<usize> = steps
            .iter()
            .map(|s| match s {
                TypeStep::Units(u) => u.len(),
                _ => 0,
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(sizes, vec![20, 20, 5]);
    }

    #[test]
    // Review Focus 3: text pasted into the text window is emoji, line breaks and long.
    fn a_surrogate_pair_is_never_split_across_chunks() {
        // 19 units, then an emoji (2 units) that would be units 20 and 21.
        let text = format!("{}😀tail", "a".repeat(19));
        let steps = plan(&text);
        for step in &steps {
            if let TypeStep::Units(u) = step {
                assert!(u.len() <= MAX_UNITS);
                if let Some(last) = u.last() {
                    assert!(!(0xD800..0xDC00).contains(last), "chunk ends in a lone high surrogate");
                }
                if let Some(first) = u.first() {
                    assert!(!(0xDC00..0xE000).contains(first), "chunk starts with a lone low surrogate");
                }
            }
        }
        let rejoined: Vec<u16> = steps
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|s| match s {
                TypeStep::Units(u) => u.clone(),
                _ => vec![],
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(String::from_utf16(&rejoined).unwrap(), text);
    }
}
```

`agent/mac/src/pressed.rs` (Review Focus 2): keys are tracked and released in reverse order of pressing; a second key-down is an auto-repeat and a stray key-up is ignored; buttons report whether anything changed; modifier flags follow the modifiers that are down; consecutive clicks in the same place count 1, 2, 3 and wrap, a slow or distant click or another button starts over.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn pressed_keys_are_tracked_and_released_in_reverse_order() {
        let mut p = Pressed::default();
        assert!(!p.key(55, true), "first press is not a repeat");
        p.key(8, true);
        p.key(9, true);
        p.key(8, false);
        let (keys, buttons) = p.drain();
        assert_eq!(keys, vec![9, 55]);
        assert!(buttons.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(p.drain().0, Vec::<u16>::new(), "drain empties the set");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_second_key_down_is_an_auto_repeat_and_a_stray_key_up_is_ignored() {
        let mut p = Pressed::default();
        p.key(0, true);
        assert!(p.key(0, true));
        assert!(!p.key(5, false));
        assert!(!p.is_key_down(5));
        p.key(0, false);
        assert!(!p.is_key_down(0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn buttons_report_whether_anything_changed() {
        let mut p = Pressed::default();
        assert!(p.button(Button::Left, true));
        assert!(!p.button(Button::Left, true), "already down");
        assert_eq!(p.held_button(), Some(Button::Left));
        assert!(p.button(Button::Right, true));
        assert_eq!(p.held_button(), Some(Button::Right), "the newest held button drives drags");
        assert!(p.button(Button::Left, false));
        assert!(!p.button(Button::Left, false), "already up");
        assert_eq!(p.drain().1, vec![Button::Right]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn flags_follow_the_modifiers_that_are_down() {
        let mut p = Pressed::default();
        assert_eq!(p.flags(), 0);
        p.key(55, true); // command
        p.key(56, true); // shift
        assert_eq!(p.flags(), 0x0010_0000 | 0x0002_0000);
        p.key(60, true); // right shift adds nothing new
        assert_eq!(p.flags(), 0x0010_0000 | 0x0002_0000);
        p.key(55, false);
        assert_eq!(p.flags(), 0x0002_0000);
        p.key(0, true); // a letter is not a modifier
        assert_eq!(p.flags(), 0x0002_0000);
    }

    #[test]
    // Review Focus 2: macOS needs a click count, or a double click never opens a folder.
    fn consecutive_clicks_in_the_same_place_count_up_and_wrap_after_three() {
        let t0 = Instant::now();
        let mut c = ClickCounter::default();
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Left, t0, 0.5, 0.5), 1);
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Left, t0 + Duration::from_millis(200), 0.5, 0.5), 2);
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Left, t0 + Duration::from_millis(400), 0.501, 0.5), 3);
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Left, t0 + Duration::from_millis(600), 0.5, 0.5), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_slow_far_or_different_button_click_starts_over() {
        let t0 = Instant::now();
        let mut c = ClickCounter::default();
        c.on_down(Button::Left, t0, 0.5, 0.5);
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Left, t0 + Duration::from_millis(501), 0.5, 0.5), 1, "too slow");
        let t1 = t0 + Duration::from_secs(5);
        c.on_down(Button::Left, t1, 0.5, 0.5);
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Left, t1 + Duration::from_millis(100), 0.6, 0.5), 1, "too far");
        let t2 = t1 + Duration::from_secs(5);
        c.on_down(Button::Left, t2, 0.5, 0.5);
        assert_eq!(c.on_down(Button::Right, t2 + Duration::from_millis(100), 0.5, 0.5), 1, "other button");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `cd agent/mac && cargo test --lib 2>&1 | grep -E "^error" | sort | uniq -c | head`
