```js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { validateConfig } from "../server/config.js";
import { buildConfig, defaultPaths } from "../scripts/init-gateway.mjs";

const posix = process.platform !== "win32";
const root = new URL("../", import.meta.url);
const read = (path) => readFile(new URL(path, root), "utf8");
const script = fileURLToPath(new URL("scripts/init-gateway.mjs", root));
const targetsFile = fileURLToPath(new URL("deploy/targets.example.json", root));

test("the example targets build a configuration whose agent target has its default port and no secret", async () => {
  const examples = JSON.parse(await read("deploy/targets.example.json"));
  const config = buildConfig({
    origin: "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8450",
    logins: ["owner@example.com"],
    targets: examples,
    ...defaultPaths("/home/user"),
  });
  const mac = config.targets.find((t) => t.id === "mac");
  assert.equal(mac.protocol, "agent");
  assert.equal(mac.port, 18471);
  assert.equal(config.targets.find((t) => t.id === "mac-vnc").protocol, "vnc");
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => validateConfig(config));
});

test(
  "init-gateway tells which set-credential command each target needs, with --agent-token only for agents",
  { skip: !posix },
  async (t) => {
    const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "gr-init-agent-"));
    t.after(() => rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
    const result = spawnSync(
      process.execPath,
      [
        script,
        join(dir, "gateway.json"),
        "--origin",
        "https://gateway.example.ts.net:8450",
        "--login",
        "owner@example.com",
        "--targets",
        targetsFile,
        "--socket",
        join(dir, "g.sock"),
        "--credentials",
        join(dir, "credentials.json"),
      ],
      { encoding: "utf8" },
    );
    assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);
    const lines = result.stdout
      .split("\n")
      .filter((l) => l.includes("set-credential.mjs"));
    assert.equal(lines.length, 3);
    const config = join(dir, "gateway.json");
    assert.ok(
      lines.some((l) => l.endsWith(`${config} ubuntu-server`)),
      lines.join("\n"),
    );
    assert.ok(lines.some((l) => l.endsWith(`${config} mac --agent-token`)));
    assert.ok(lines.some((l) => l.endsWith(`${config} mac-vnc`)));
  },
);
```

+route measured about 170 to 180 ms from click to picture; it stays as the tile for
+unlocking the Mac, allowing permissions and as a fallback.
+
+### The Mac agent
+
+A small program in [`agent/mac`](agent/mac) (Rust) that captures the screen with
+ScreenCaptureKit, encodes H.264 on the Mac's hardware encoder and sends it over WebRTC;
+keyboard and mouse come back on a data channel and are posted as system events. It runs
+in the owner's login session, so it exists only while the owner is logged in (after a
+reboot, before login, the tile says 오프라인; the Mac (VNC) tile does not help then either).
+
+Requirements: Rust, the Xcode command line tools and Tailscale on the Mac (developed and
+tested on macOS 27). Install and update are the same command, in Terminal on the Mac:
+
+```sh
+git clone https://github.com/WONJUNE-LEE/gome-remote.git ~/gome-remote   # or update it
+cd ~/gome-remote/agent/mac
+./install.sh
+```
+
+It builds (`cargo build --release --locked`), signs the program with a certificate it
+keeps in a keychain of its own (a stable signature is what keeps the macOS permissions
+across updates), installs it as root-owned `/usr/local/libexec/gome-remote-agent` (it asks
+for the administrator password), writes the root-owned settings
+`/usr/local/etc/gome-remote-agent/agent.json` once (the Mac's Tailscale address, the
+gateway server's Tailscale address, port 18471), creates the token once, loads the
+LaunchAgent `net.gome.remote-agent`, and checks that the new program answers within 10
+seconds; if it does not, the previous program (`gome-remote-agent.prev`) is put back.
+
+After the **first** install, two things need a person at the Mac: in System Settings >
+Privacy & Security allow `gome-remote-agent` under **Screen & System Audio Recording** and
+under **Accessibility**, then restart it with
+`launchctl kickstart -k gui/$(id -u)/net.gome.remote-agent` (macOS only applies a new
+permission to a program that starts afterwards). On the gateway server run
+`node scripts/set-credential.mjs mac --agent-token` and paste the token the installer
+showed (it is shown once; it stays in `~/Library/Application Support/Gome Remote
+Agent/token`). In Korean Mac settings, "한/영" needs **System Settings > Keyboard > Input
+Sources > Use the Caps Lock key to switch to and from ABC** to be on: the Windows Hangul
+key is sent as one Caps Lock press.
+
+Good to know:
+
+- **Security.** The agent listens only on the Mac's Tailscale address, answers only the
+  gateway's address, and only with the token. Its program and settings are root-owned, it
+  takes no argument and no environment variable, and it is signed with the hardened
+  runtime. Anyone who controls the gateway server can therefore control the Mac's screen
+  and input (as before, when the server held the Mac account password).
+- **The 30-day reconfirmation.** macOS 27 asks the owner every 30 days to confirm that a
+  program may record the screen. If the screen goes blank or the tile says 권한 필요 around
+  then, open Mac (VNC) and click the confirmation window.
+- **Updating.** Run `./install.sh` again; the token and settings are kept. The administrator
+  password is needed every time, because the program lives in a root-owned folder.
+  `./install.sh --dev` installs a separate copy under your home directory (its own port
+  18472, label, token and settings; no password) for development.
+- **Removing.** `./uninstall.sh` (add `--dev` for the development copy) removes the
+  program, settings, token, log and keychain; then delete the two entries in System
+  Settings yourself.
+- **Logs.** `~/Library/Logs/gome-remote-agent.log` holds start-up, permission and
+  session lines, one statistics line every ten seconds while someone watches, and is
+  emptied when it passes 5 MiB.
+- **Scope.** The main display only, no sound, no clipboard sync, no lock-screen automation.

## Owner-assisted and operational steps (not code tasks)

These are the pre-tests, the verification runs and the rollout of spec sections 3, 8 and 9. They are carried out by a person or by a coding-agent session on the operator's behalf, never by a Sonnet implementer working through Tasks 1-13. Each step is marked:

- **[OWNER]**: needs the owner at the Mac or on their own devices (clicking in System Settings, locking the Mac, using the Windows and Mac apps, approving a switch-over).
- **[SUDO]**: needs a `sudo` password (the Mac's administrator password for the agent's root-owned files, or the server's for Tailscale Serve and `tc`). A coding-agent session cannot type it; the owner runs the command or supplies it interactively.
- **[OPERATOR]**: a coding-agent session (or the owner) with ssh to the Mac and shell access to the gateway server; no password needed.

Evidence goes into `docs/verification.md` (no tailnet login, device name or tailnet address; results as numbers and words) and into the private handoff notes (with the identifiers). Do not write "pass" without the evidence. Nothing here touches the production gateway (`gome-remote-gateway-v2`, its configuration and credentials files, Serve `:8450`) before rollout step 5.

### Pre-tests (spec section 3)

Order: P1 (a) and (c) and P2 can start now with the spike that is installed on the Mac; P1 (b) needs the Task 9/10 agent; P3 needs Task 11; P4 starts now and runs in parallel with everything until 2026-11-12. The spike stays installed until P4 is finished (rollout step 1).

**P1. Lock screen** [OWNER at the Mac, OPERATOR]. Tell the owner first. Passwords are never typed or recorded: meaningless characters are typed and deleted.

1. [OPERATOR] (a) With the spike's page connected to the spike (`http://<MAC_IP>:18470/#token=<token from ~/gome-agent-spike/token>`, in Chrome on the server or anywhere on the tailnet) and showing the Mac, [OWNER] locks the Mac (Control-Command-Q) and wakes the display. Record what the video shows: the lock screen (pass), black, or the frozen last picture (fail). Also record what `GET /stats` on the spike says about capture fps.
2. [OPERATOR + OWNER] (b) needs key injection, which the spike lacks. With the Task 10 agent running (Task 10 Step 7 set-up), edit locally and do not commit: `REFUSE_WHEN_LOCKED = false` in `agent/mac/src/status.rs`, `LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED = true` in `src/tiles.ts`; rebuild the page (`npm run build`), rsync and restart the agent. [OWNER] locks the Mac; the operator opens the dev tile and types three meaningless characters into the password field. Pass: dots appear (then delete them). Record. Discard the local edits afterwards (`git checkout agent/mac/src/status.rs src/tiles.ts`).
3. [OWNER] (c) With the Mac locked, open the **Mac (VNC)** tile and type the owner's password into the lock screen: does it unlock?

Branch:

- (a) and (b) pass: the agent unlocks the Mac too. Change `REFUSE_WHEN_LOCKED` to `false` (`agent/mac/src/status.rs`) and `LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED` to `true` (`src/tiles.ts`); update the two tests that pin the old values (`test/viewer-agent.test.js`: "a Mac that needs permission is not openable; a locked one follows the P1 switch" expects `false`; `agent/mac/src/status.rs` tests already pass both values to `gate`), and the README sentence "잠김: unlock through Mac (VNC)". Commit as its own change.
- (a) or (b) fails, (c) passes: nothing changes; the tile says 잠김 and the owner unlocks with the VNC tile (D9 keeps it). Say so in `docs/verification.md`.
- (c) fails as well: there is no unlock path today either, so nothing regresses. Continue and tell the owner.

**P2. Real clients** [OWNER on the Windows device and the Mac, OPERATOR]. For each of the owner's Windows and Mac devices, in Chrome:

1. (a) ICE and the selected pair. Open the spike page, press Connect; in `chrome://webrtc-internals` (or `getStats()` in the page console) record the nominated candidate pair's local and remote types (`host`, `prflx`, `srflx`, `relay`) and whether `tailscale ping <Mac>` on that device says direct or via a relay (DERP). [OPERATOR] writes down "direct" or "DERP", never the names.
2. (b) With `scripts/measure-target.html` full screen on the Mac and the spike running at 2560x1440 (`scripts/install-agent.sh <MAC_IP> --size 2560x1440 --fps 60 --bitrate-mbps 12` from the spike), press the spike page's Measure button with N = 30 and record median, p95, maximum.
3. (c) Hold after a still period. Restart the spike with `--keepalive-ms 1000`, measure 30 clicks, and count samples whose `ms - recvMs` exceeds 250 ms (`node analyze-trace.mjs run.json` in the spike prints the table); repeat with `--keepalive-ms 100`.
4. (d) In the page's stats record `decoder` and `powerEfficientDecoder` (the decoder implementation name and whether it is hardware).

Branch:

- ICE does not form: fix the candidate rules (`tailnet::filter_offer_candidates` and the source-address filter in `agent/mac/src/peer.rs`; D2 requires that a tailnet-address prflx candidate is accepted) and re-run. If it still fails, report to the owner and stop.
- (b) does not meet S2: find the cause (path, decoder). Prefer making a direct path (DERP means check `tailscale netcheck` and the Mac's firewall first). Do not change S2's thresholds. If it cannot be solved, report to the owner and stop.
- (c) with a hardware decoder holds frames even at 100 ms: the first implementation task becomes re-deciding the static-screen repeat (`agent/mac/src/keepalive.rs` constants and approach); S2 and S3 judge the result.
- Otherwise record the four results and continue.

**P3. Permissions survive updates** [OWNER at the Mac, SUDO, OPERATOR]. After Task 11, with the operational procedure of rollout step 2 (the root-owned install), not the development one:

1. [OWNER, SUDO] `./install.sh`; allow `gome-remote-agent` under Screen & System Audio Recording and Accessibility; `launchctl kickstart -k gui/$(id -u)/net.gome.remote-agent`.
2. [OPERATOR] Confirm: `GET /v1/status` from the gateway server shows `screen` and `accessibility` `granted`; open a session (dev or candidate gateway) and see the picture and the input work. Confirm the hardened runtime: `ssh "$MAC" 'codesign -dv --verbose=4 /usr/local/libexec/gome-remote-agent 2>&1 | grep -i flags'` shows `runtime`.
3. [OWNER, SUDO] Two updates with the same certificate: change nothing but the version in `agent/mac/Cargo.toml` (0.1.0, 0.1.1, 0.1.2, with `cargo generate-lockfile` offline if needed, not committed), run `./install.sh` after each. After each, repeat step 2 (status granted, picture, input).
4. [OWNER, SUDO] A new certificate: remove `~/Library/Application Support/Gome Remote Agent/sign`, run `./install.sh`, repeat step 2. Expected: the permissions are gone and must be given again.

Branch: if the grant survives step 3: nothing changes (D6 as written). If it does not: updates need a re-allow (possible remotely through the VNC tile): add that step to the end-of-script text in `install.sh` and to the README "Updating" bullet, in one commit with its tests (`test/agent-install.test.js` pins the "Next steps" text).

**P4. The 30-day screen-recording reconfirmation** [OPERATOR, OWNER]. The spike's entry is scheduled for 2026-11-12 (`ScreenCaptureApprovals.plist`, 30 days). Do not move the date.

1. [OPERATOR] On 2026-11-11 verify that the spike is running and start a session from the server that stays open (its page connected); on the day, also keep one `curl` of `/stats` per minute for the day, plus `tail -f ~/gome-agent-spike/run/agent.log` over ssh.
2. [OWNER] Be at the Mac on 2026-11-12 (and 11-13) and say when a window appears.
3. Record: does the capture continue; if it stops, whether ScreenCaptureKit returned an error (log) or kept delivering black/unchanged frames (`/stats` captured fps and the picture); where the confirmation window appeared; whether the Mac (VNC) tile can click it (with a screenshot of the tile; no identifiers).
4. If a window appears, [OWNER] answers it; record how long the capture stood still.

Branch:

- Capture continues: nothing changes (D5).
- It stops with an error: the status says `screen: denied` (D5) and the tile 권한 필요; recovery is the VNC tile. Add the sentence to the README if the wording differs.
- It continues with black frames: the black-frame detector (`agent/mac/src/black.rs`, `SUSPECT_AFTER` 5 s, `lastCaptureAt` counts frames with content) reports `suspect`; adjust only the thresholds the observation justifies.
- The VNC tile cannot click the window: the owner must press it at the Mac on each reconfirmation date. Tell the owner; add the date to the post-cutover checks (rollout step 6); the cutover decision is the owner's.

**No cutover (rollout step 5) happens before the P4 result is recorded.**

### Rollout (spec section 9)

The production gateway, its unit, configuration, credentials and Serve `:8450` are not touched before step 5.

**Step 1. The spike stays until P4 is done** [OPERATOR, OWNER]. Ports (18470 and 18471) and permission entries differ, so it may coexist with the agent. Note the exposure meanwhile: two programs hold the Screen Recording permission, and the spike answers on the tailnet with a token-protected `/offer` and public `/stats`, `/trace`, `/target`. When P4 is finished (before step 5): [OPERATOR] `launchctl bootout gui/$(id -u)/net.gome.agent-spike`, remove `~/Library/LaunchAgents/net.gome.agent-spike.plist`, remove `~/gome-agent-spike` (token and signing keychain included); [OWNER] removes the spike's entries from System Settings > Privacy & Security.

**Step 2. Install the agent** [OWNER at the Mac, SUDO]. `cd ~/gome-remote-agent-build` (or a fresh checkout of the branch) and `./install.sh`; give the two addresses when asked; allow both permissions; `launchctl kickstart -k gui/$(id -u)/net.gome.remote-agent`; keep the token the script printed for step 3. (P3 is performed here.)

**Step 3. Parallel gateway** [OPERATOR, SUDO for Serve].

1. Back up Serve before changing it: `tailscale serve status --json > ~/serve-before-candidate.json`. Add exactly one port: `sudo tailscale serve --bg --https=8451 unix:$HOME/.local/state/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.sock`. Check that the earlier routes (`:443`, `:8450`) are unchanged: `diff <(jq -S . ~/serve-before-candidate.json) <(tailscale serve status --json | jq -S .)` may show only additions for `:8451`, and `https://<server>.<tailnet>.ts.net:8450/` must still answer.
2. Put the candidate in its own directory and build it: `mkdir -p ~/remote-candidates/<version>` with a checkout of the branch (`git worktree add` or a clone), `npm ci && npm run build`.
3. New unit, configuration and credentials, none shared with production:

```sh
mkdir -p ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate ~/.config/systemd/user
sed -e 's#^WorkingDirectory=.*#WorkingDirectory=%h/remote-candidates/<version>#' \
    -e 's#^Environment=GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG=.*#Environment=GOME_REMOTE_CONFIG=%h/.config/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.json#' \
    deploy/gome-remote-gateway.service > ~/.config/systemd/user/gome-remote-gateway-candidate.service
node scripts/init-gateway.mjs ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.json \
  --origin https://<server>.<tailnet>.ts.net:8451 --login <each allowed login> \
  --targets <a private targets file with Ubuntu (rdp), "Mac (새 방식)" (agent), "Mac (VNC)" (vnc)> \
  --socket $HOME/.local/state/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.sock \
  --credentials $HOME/.config/gome-remote-candidate/credentials.json
```

   The Ubuntu target keeps its id and name; the agent target is `id: "mac"` named `Mac (새 방식)` (renamed to `Mac` at the cutover); the VNC target is `id: "mac-vnc"` named `Mac (VNC)`.
4. [OWNER] enters three credentials into the **new** file: `node scripts/set-credential.mjs ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.json ubuntu-server` (the Ubuntu system Remote Login values), `... mac-vnc` (the Mac account), `... mac --agent-token` (the token from step 2). The production credentials file is neither read nor written.
5. `systemctl --user daemon-reload && systemctl --user enable --now gome-remote-gateway-candidate.service`. `journalctl --user -u gome-remote-gateway-candidate -n 20` shows `Gome Remote is listening`.

**Step 4. V1-V6** [see below]. The owner opens the **`:8451`** address in the browser or app. **Caution for the app:** the gateway compares the request origin with `publicOrigin` exactly, port included, and the Electron app stores one address. If the owner changes the app's address to `:8451` for testing, they **must change it back to `:8450` after the cutover** (or test in a browser instead and leave the app alone). The cutover step below makes `:8451` answer 403, so an app left on `:8451` shows the "서버에 연결할 수 없습니다" screen.

**Step 5. Cutover** (only after V1-V6 are recorded, P4 is recorded, and the owner says go) [OPERATOR, SUDO for Serve]. In this order:

1. Back up the candidate configuration (`cp ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.json ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.json.at-8451`). Change `publicOrigin` to the `:8450` origin and rename the agent target to `Mac`:

```sh
cd ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate
jq '.publicOrigin = "https://<server>.<tailnet>.ts.net:8450" | (.targets[] | select(.id=="mac") | .name) = "Mac"' gateway.json > gateway.json.new \
  && mv gateway.json.new gateway.json && chmod 600 gateway.json
systemctl --user restart gome-remote-gateway-candidate.service
```

   From this moment `:8451` answers 403 for every API call and the production `:8450` is still the old gateway.
2. Back up and switch Serve `:8450` to the new socket: `tailscale serve status --json > ~/serve-before-cutover.json`; `sudo tailscale serve --bg --https=8450 unix:$HOME/.local/state/gome-remote-candidate/gateway.sock`; `tailscale serve status --json > ~/serve-after-cutover.json`; `diff <(jq -S . ~/serve-before-cutover.json) <(jq -S . ~/serve-after-cutover.json)` shows only the `:8450` handler.
3. At `https://<server>.<tailnet>.ts.net:8450/` check the list, the Ubuntu tile and the two Mac tiles. If anything fails: **at once** `sudo tailscale serve --bg --https=8450 unix:<the old gateway's socket path from ~/serve-before-cutover.json>` (the old unit is still running).
4. When it is fine: `systemctl --user stop gome-remote-gateway-v2.service` (stop, do not disable or delete), `sudo tailscale serve --https=8451 off`. The app's address is `:8450` as before. If the app was pointed at `:8451` for testing, [OWNER] changes it back to `:8450` now (**원격 → 서버 주소 바꾸기**).

**Step 6. After the cutover** [OPERATOR, in later sessions]. The first signal is the tile state the owner sees when opening the app. In addition, at +1 day, +7 days and +30 days (the 30-day date includes the screen-recording reconfirmation date): check the Mac tile state (`/api/targets` as the allowed login, or the agent's status with the token read into a shell variable and never printed: `TOKEN=$(jq -r '.targets.mac.agentToken' ~/.config/gome-remote-candidate/credentials.json); curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" http://$MAC_IP:18471/v1/status`), `lastCaptureAt`, and the agent log (`ssh "$MAC" 'tail -n 50 ~/Library/Logs/gome-remote-agent.log'`); record the result in the private handoff notes and tell the owner of anything unusual. The dates are written into the handoff notes at cutover time and the first session after each date does the check.

**Rollback.**

- After step 5: `systemctl --user start gome-remote-gateway-v2.service`; `sudo tailscale serve --bg --https=8450 unix:<old socket>`. The old configuration and credentials were never touched, so it works as before (rehearsed in V6). To test the new gateway again: put `publicOrigin` back to the `:8451` backup (`gateway.json.at-8451`), restart the candidate unit, re-add Serve `:8451`.
- The agent: `install.sh` restores `.prev` by itself when the new program does not answer; to remove it altogether run `./uninstall.sh`.
- Partial failure in steps 1-4: the production gateway is unaffected (separate files and unit); stop the candidate unit and turn the new Serve port off (`sudo tailscale serve --https=8451 off`). The VNC route works the whole time.

### Verification runs (spec section 8)

All runs use the candidate gateway on `:8451` (rollout step 3) with the agent installed by `install.sh`. The measuring tool is the same for every client: `#measure` after the address, or `scripts/measure-drive.mjs` for the server's headless Chrome, with `scripts/measure-target.html` open full screen and frontmost on the Mac. A run is valid only if the target page's flip counter grew by exactly N (the driver's screenshots show it).

**V1. Agent measured from the gateway server's Chrome** [OPERATOR; SUDO for `tc`]. For the Mac (agent) tile and for the Mac (VNC) tile in turn, at the same time of day: three runs of `PW_CORE=<dir> GATEWAY_URL=https://<server>.<tailnet>.ts.net:8451/ TILE_MATCH='새 방식' N=30 node scripts/measure-drive.mjs measure` (then `TILE_MATCH='VNC'`), passing the VNC run's median as `VNC_MEDIAN_MS` to the agent runs. S2: median at most 100 ms and at most 0.6 x the VNC median, p95 at most 150 ms, nothing over 1 s, in all three runs. S3: `SOAK_SECONDS=60 node scripts/measure-drive.mjs soak` once: no frame gap over 500 ms. S4: limit this machine's **receive** rate to 5 Mbps while measuring (`sudo` required):

```sh
sudo modprobe ifb numifbs=1 && sudo ip link set ifb0 up
sudo tc qdisc add dev tailscale0 handle ffff: ingress
sudo tc filter add dev tailscale0 parent ffff: protocol all u32 match u32 0 0 action mirred egress redirect dev ifb0
sudo tc qdisc add dev ifb0 root tbf rate 5mbit burst 64kbit latency 100ms
# run the measure and soak modes again: S4 needs no stall over 1 s and a median of at most 200 ms
sudo tc qdisc del dev tailscale0 ingress; sudo tc qdisc del dev ifb0 root; sudo ip link set ifb0 down
```

Also record the idle bandwidth and the agent's CPU during a still screen (the agent log's `[stats]` line and `ssh "$MAC" top -l 2 -pid <pid> | tail -3`). If the numbers miss a threshold, find the cause (decoder, path, BWE start point `bitrate::START_BPS`); never move a threshold.

**V2. Windows app** [OWNER]. Windows app on the candidate address ("Mac (새 방식)" tile): mouse movement, the three buttons, drag, scroll (the direction should match the Windows habit; if it is inverted, change the sign in `agent/mac/src/mac/inject.rs` `post_scroll` and the test that pins it), keyboard, Korean typing, the 한/영 key (needs System Settings > Keyboard > Input Sources > "Use the Caps Lock key to switch to and from ABC"; if the owner uses another switch key, tell the operator, O3), the text window, resolution auto and the fixed sizes, fullscreen, reconnect; `#measure` for S2/S3; the owner's verdict on feel (S5). Record the decoder name and whether the path was direct.

**V3. Mac app** [OWNER]. Repeat V2 from the owner's Mac app (a different Mac than the one being controlled).

**V4. Agent states** [OPERATOR + OWNER]. Lock screen as P1 decided; connect while the display sleeps (the session start wakes it); takeover from a second device; kill the agent (`ssh "$MAC" 'launchctl kickstart -k gui/$(id -u)/net.gome.remote-agent'`) and see it return and the tile become 켜짐 again; after the session ends nothing stays pressed on the Mac (hold Shift and a mouse button in the page, close the tab); remove a permission in System Settings (or use a build run before it was granted) and see 권한 필요 on the tile and the exact error card text.

**V5. Negative tests** [OPERATOR]. All must be refused or ignored: a wrong token (`401`, answered after a delay); a call from a tailnet address that is not the gateway's (from the Mac itself or another device: `403`); a call with an `Origin` header (`403`); a login outside `allowedLogins` against the gateway (`403` with the login message); starting the program with an extra argument or an environment variable naming another settings file (`GOME_AGENT_CONFIG=... /usr/local/libexec/gome-remote-agent --config x` must behave exactly as without them).

**V6. Cutover and rollback rehearsal** [OWNER informed, OPERATOR, SUDO]. In a short window the owner knows about, perform rollout step 5 exactly as written, check `:8450` for Ubuntu, Mac (agent) and Mac (VNC), then perform the rollback exactly as written (old unit back, Serve back), check again, then (if the owner says go) do the cutover for real. Also run the agent's `.prev` rollback once on the operational install: make a deliberately failing build (the Task 11 rehearsal edit), `./install.sh`, observe the rollback message, then install the good build.

Record every result in `docs/verification.md` (no identifiers) and the private handoff notes.

### Owner decisions still open

- O2: whether to delete the Mac account password from the server one day. Not done here (D9 keeps it for the VNC tile).
- O3: confirm the Korean switch key (the README says Caps Lock-based; V2 settles it).
- O4: confirm that the operational install uses the root-owned path and that every update asks for the administrator password; development uses `install.sh --dev`.
- After P1: whether a locked Mac may be opened from the agent tile.
- After P4: whether to cut over if the reconfirmation window cannot be clicked from the VNC tile.

## Self-review

Run after writing and after each revision of this plan.

1. **Spec coverage.** Every D, S, P, V and O item is in the table at the top and maps to a task or an operational step; the checks are Task 1-13 for D1-D10, Task 5 plus V1-V3 for S2-S4, "Pre-tests" for P1-P4, "Verification runs" for V1-V6.
2. **Placeholders.** No step says "TBD", "add error handling" or "similar to Task N"; every code step shows its code; each file marked "ported" says what it started from and what changed.
3. **Type consistency.** Names used across tasks: `createAgentSession`/`AgentSession` (Task 3, used by Tasks 4-5), `AgentError`/`createAgentClient` (Task 2), `Pipeline::start(Size, u32, Arc<dyn FrameTarget>)` (Task 7, used in Task 10), `PeerHooks`/`PeerHandle`/`FrameTarget` (Task 8, used in Task 10), `Injector::{handle, release_all}` (Task 9), `Slot` (Task 10), `REFUSE_WHEN_LOCKED` and `LOCKED_TILE_ENABLED` (the P1 switch, Tasks 3 and 6). The wire format is defined once by `src/agent-input.ts` `message.*` and parsed once by `agent/mac/src/protocol.rs`; both have a test that names the same shapes.
4. **Review Focus.** The five items each have a test in their owning task, marked in the test files.
